01细菌学总重点

TU02 Bacteriology Comprehensive Pass-Focused Notes

细菌学总重点 · 中英对照 · 打印背诵版 · 一份搞定

使用方法 / How to use：先背★★★，再补★★。这份不是百科全书，是为了让你能在卷面上写出结构题、机制题、对比题和代表例子。结构题固定按：where it is → composition → function → clinical/exam importance。

0. First-aid page: membrane/envelope layers｜先救命：膜和分层名称

★★★ Gram-negative envelope outside → inside｜革兰阴性菌包膜从外到内
O-antigen / O抗原
LPS最外侧重复糖单位；serotyping, immune interaction

Outer membrane / 外膜：asymmetric bilayer；outer leaflet = LPS；inner leaflet = phospholipids

Porins + OM proteins / 孔蛋白和外膜蛋白：permeability, transport, receptor role

Lpp/Braun lipoprotein, OmpA, Pal：connect OM to peptidoglycan; maintain envelope mechanics

Periplasm / 周质空间
soluble proteins + thin peptidoglycan mesh + oligosaccharides

Thin peptidoglycan / 薄肽聚糖：shape, protection against turgor pressure

Inner/cytoplasmic membrane / 内膜/细胞质膜：symmetric phospholipid bilayer, transport, protein translocation

Cytoplasm / 细胞质
DNA, ribosomes, metabolism
★★★ Gram-positive surface outside → inside｜革兰阳性菌表面从外到内
Capsule / 荚膜
if present, outermost; anti-phagocytosis, immune evasion
S-layer / S层
if present, protein crystalline network, most external envelope layer

Surface proteins / 表面蛋白：adhesins, proteases, autolysins; often virulence factors

Wall teichoic acids (WTA) / 壁磷壁酸：anchored/covalently bound in peptidoglycan

Thick peptidoglycan / 厚肽聚糖：rigid mesh, shape, osmotic protection

Lipoteichoic acids (LTA) / 脂磷壁酸：anchored in cytoplasmic membrane via glycolipid anchor

Cytoplasmic membrane / 细胞质膜：selective barrier and transport

★★★ Bacterial spore layers outside → inside｜细菌孢子从外到内
Exosporium / 外孢层
only in some species, e.g. B. anthracis, B. cereus; adhesion/resistance
Coat / 孢衣
multilayered protein shell; chemical, lysozyme and enzyme resistance

Outer/external membrane / 外膜：between coat and cortex in spore structure diagrams

Cortex / 皮层
special thick PG with muramic-δ-lactam; degraded during germination

Germ cell wall / primordial PG / 胚芽细胞壁/原始PG：retained during outgrowth

Inner membrane / 内膜：very low permeability, protects core even from water

Core / 核心
DNA, RNA, enzymes, low water, low pH, Ca-DPA, SASP proteins

1. Whole-course map and exam logic｜整门细菌学总地图与答题逻辑

★★★ Core modules
bacterial surface, peptidoglycan, gene transfer/regulation, host response, cytoskeleton hacking, spores, toxins. ｜ 核心模块：表面结构、肽聚糖、基因转移/调控、宿主反应、细胞骨架劫持、孢子、毒素。
★★★ Best exam answer pattern: definition → location/structure → mechanism/function → example/clinical importance. ｜ 最佳答题模板：定义 → 位置/结构 → 机制/功能 → 例子/临床意义。
★★★ Do not write only isolated keywords. Link structure to consequence. ｜ 不要只堆名词，要把结构和后果连起来。

Question type｜题型

Must write｜必须写

Example sentence｜卷面句

Structure｜结构题

Layer order + composition + function

The Gram-negative envelope contains an inner membrane, a periplasm with thin PG, and an outer membrane containing LPS.

Mechanism｜机制题

Recognition → pathway → output

Lipid A is transferred by LBP to CD14/MD-2/TLR4, activating NF-kB/AP-1 and cytokine production.

Comparison｜对比题

At least 3 differences + one consequence

Gram-positive bacteria have thick PG and TA/LTA, whereas Gram-negative bacteria have thin PG, periplasm and LPS-containing outer membrane.

Example｜举例题

One organism + one disease/mechanism

C. botulinum produces botulinum toxin, blocks neuromuscular release and causes flaccid paralysis.

2. Gram-positive vs Gram-negative｜革兰阳性 vs 革兰阴性

Item｜项目

Gram-positive｜革兰阳性

Gram-negative｜革兰阴性

Cell wall

Thick PG, 15-80 nm; several layers

Thin but strong PG, around 10 nm; single layer

Outer membrane

Absent

Present; asymmetric; LPS in outer leaflet

Periplasm

Not the same as G- periplasm

Clear periplasmic space with soluble proteins + PG

Special polymers

WTA anchored in PG; LTA anchored in membrane

LPS = Lipid A + core + O-antigen

Main immune/clinical point

PG fragments, TA/LTA, lysozyme resistance

Lipid A = endotoxin; TLR4; permeability barrier

Minimal answer / 最简答案：Gram-positive bacteria have a thick peptidoglycan layer and teichoic/lipoteichoic acids, but no outer membrane. Gram-negative bacteria have a thin peptidoglycan layer in the periplasm and an outer membrane containing LPS.
★★★ Cell wall vs cell envelope ｜ cell wall 通常强调 PG 层；cell envelope 包括 membrane + wall + periplasm/outer membrane 等整体包膜。
★★★ Peptidoglycan function: shape + protection against osmotic rupture + scaffold for surface structures. ｜ PG 功能：维持形态、抗渗透压裂解、作为表面结构锚定框架。
★★★ Lipid A of LPS is the endotoxin. ｜ LPS 的 Lipid A 才是内毒素活性部分。

3. Gram-negative surface, LPS and appendages｜革兰阴性表面、LPS和附属结构

3.1 Inner membrane, periplasm and outer membrane｜内膜、周质、外膜

★★★ Inner membrane
cytoplasmic membrane, symmetric phospholipid bilayer, selective transport and protein translocation. ｜ 内膜：细胞质膜，对称磷脂双层，负责选择性营养运输和蛋白转运。
★★★ Periplasm
contains soluble proteins, thin PG mesh and membrane-derived oligosaccharides. ｜ 周质：含可溶性蛋白、薄PG网和膜来源寡糖。
★★★ Outer membrane
asymmetric bilayer; inner leaflet phospholipids; outer leaflet LPS. ｜ 外膜：非对称双层；内侧叶是磷脂，外侧叶是LPS。
★★ Porins and OM proteins regulate permeability and can serve as phage or nutrient receptors. ｜ 孔蛋白和外膜蛋白调控通透性，也可作为噬菌体/营养物受体。
★★ Braun lipoprotein Lpp covalently links OM to PG and helps control periplasm width/mechanics. ｜ Braun脂蛋白Lpp共价连接外膜和PG，维持周质宽度和包膜机械性质。

3.2 LPS structure and endotoxin｜LPS结构与内毒素

Part｜部分

Composition｜组成

Exam function｜考试功能

Lipid A

Conserved phosphorylated glucosamine disaccharide + fatty acid chains

Hydrophobic anchor; endotoxin active component; PAMP for TLR4

Core oligosaccharide

Inner core Kdo + heptoses; outer core hexoses

Links Lipid A to O-antigen; stabilizes LPS

O-antigen

Repeating sugar units, highly variable

Serotyping, immune interaction, complement evasion; non-toxic

★★★ LPS is a major OM component and permeability barrier. ｜ LPS是外膜主要成分，也是通透性屏障。
★★★ Endotoxin effects: pyrogenic, immune activation, high fever, sepsis, toxic shock. ｜ 内毒素效应：致热、激活免疫、高热、败血症、毒性休克。
★★★ LPS recognition: LBP binds LPS → CD14/MD-2 → TLR4 recognition/dimerization/endocytosis. ｜ 识别链：LBP结合LPS → CD14/MD-2 → TLR4识别、二聚化和内吞。
★★ MyD88 pathway
IRAK/TRAF6 → NF-kB and AP-1 → inflammatory cytokines. ｜ MyD88通路：IRAK/TRAF6 → NF-kB和AP-1 → 炎症细胞因子。
★★ TRIF pathway
TRAF3 → IRF3 → type I interferons IFNα/IFNβ. ｜ TRIF通路：TRAF3 → IRF3 → I型干扰素IFNα/IFNβ。
★★ Parenteral pharmaceutical products must control pyrogenic endotoxin; LAL assay is classic. ｜ 注射用药品需要控制内毒素热原，LAL试验是经典检测。
★★ Polymyxins/colistin bind negatively charged LPS, displace Mg2+/Ca2+, disorganize OM and increase permeability. ｜ 多黏菌素/黏菌素结合带负电LPS，置换Mg2+/Ca2+，破坏外膜并提高通透性。

3.3 O-antigen high-yield points｜O抗原高频点

★★ O-antigen is immunodominant but non-toxic. ｜ O抗原免疫显性，但不是毒性活性部分。
★★ Smooth strains have O-antigen; rough strains lack O-antigen. ｜ 光滑型有O抗原，粗糙型缺乏O抗原。
★★ Basis for serological classification: O, H(flagellar), and sometimes K(capsule) antigens. ｜ 血清分型依据：O抗原、H鞭毛抗原，有时还有K荚膜抗原。
★ Molecular mimicry: H. pylori O-antigen can resemble Lewis X blood group antigens. ｜ 分子模拟：幽门螺杆菌O抗原可类似Lewis X血型抗原。

3.4 Pili/fimbriae and flagella｜菌毛/伞毛与鞭毛

★★★ Pili/fimbriae
thin hair-like protein tubes; adhesion, invasion, biofilm, DNA uptake, gene transfer. ｜ 菌毛/伞毛：细的蛋白管；黏附、侵袭、生物膜、DNA摄取、基因转移。
★★ Chaperone-usher pili: common in E. coli, Shigella, Salmonella; UPEC type I pili/FimH binds bladder; P pili/PapG binds kidney. ｜ 伴侣-usher菌毛：常见于E. coli等；UPEC I型菌毛/FimH偏膀胱，P菌毛/PapG偏肾脏。
★★ Type IV pili
dynamic polymerization/depolymerization; adhesion, biofilm, DNA uptake, microcolonies. ｜ IV型菌毛：动态聚合/去聚合；黏附、生物膜、DNA摄取、微菌落。
★★★ F pilus/T4SS
conjugation, donor-recipient contact, horizontal gene transfer, antibiotic resistance spread. ｜ F菌毛/T4SS：接合、供受体接触、水平基因转移、耐药传播。
★★★ Flagella
long whip-like motility organelles; also adhesion, biofilm, protein export and innate immune recognition. ｜ 鞭毛：长鞭状运动结构；也参与黏附、生物膜、蛋白输出和先天免疫识别。
★★ Flagellum structure: filament(flagellin), cap FliD, hook, basal body/motor, rings, rod, stator MotA/MotB, export apparatus. ｜ 鞭毛结构：丝状体(flagellin)、帽FliD、钩、基体/马达、环、杆、定子MotA/MotB、输出装置。
★★ Flagellin is a PAMP recognized by TLR5; signaling activates MAPK/NF-kB and pro-inflammatory cytokines. ｜ 鞭毛蛋白是PAMP，被TLR5识别，激活MAPK/NF-kB和促炎因子。

4. Gram-positive surface and peptidoglycan｜革兰阳性表面与肽聚糖

4.1 Gram-positive surface components｜革兰阳性表面成分

★★★ Gram-positive surface = cytoplasmic membrane + thick PG + WTA/LTA + surface proteins + optional S-layer/capsule. ｜ 革兰阳性表面=细胞质膜+厚PG+WTA/LTA+表面蛋白+可选S层/荚膜。
★★★ WTA
anionic polymers, polyol repeats, covalently bound in PG. ｜ WTA壁磷壁酸：阴离子聚合物，多元醇重复单位，共价结合在PG中。
★★★ LTA
anchored in cytoplasmic membrane via glycolipid anchor. ｜ LTA脂磷壁酸：通过糖脂锚定在细胞质膜。
★★ Surface proteins mediate colonization/virulence, act as adhesins/proteases, and include PG hydrolases/autolysins. ｜ 表面蛋白介导定植/毒力，可为黏附素/蛋白酶，也包括PG水解酶/自溶素。
★★ Anchoring
LPXTG motif via sortase; LysM/SPOR/WXL/SH3b non-covalent; SLH/CWB domains for polysaccharide anchoring. ｜ 锚定：LPXTG经sortase共价锚定；LysM等非共价；SLH/CWB用于多糖锚定。

4.2 Peptidoglycan composition and synthesis｜PG组成和合成

★★★ PG is a rigid glycan-peptide mesh determining bacterial shape and protecting against external aggressors. ｜ PG是刚性糖肽网，决定形态并保护细菌免受外界损伤。
★★★ Backbone
alternating GlcNAc and MurNAc; peptide side chain attached to MurNAc. ｜ 骨架：GlcNAc和MurNAc交替；MurNAc上连接肽侧链。
★★★ Typical peptide includes L-Ala, D-Glu, meso-DAP or L-Lys, D-Ala-D-Ala. ｜ 典型肽链包括L-Ala、D-Glu、meso-DAP或L-Lys、D-Ala-D-Ala。
★★ Cross-linking/reticulation gives mechanical strength; cross-link patterns differ by species. ｜ 交联/网状化提供机械强度，不同菌种交联模式不同。
★★★ Cytoplasmic stage: precursor synthesis → MraY/MurG → Lipid I/Lipid II. ｜ 胞质阶段：前体合成 → MraY/MurG → Lipid I/Lipid II。
★★★ Extra-membrane stage: PBPs catalyze transglycosylation and transpeptidation. ｜ 膜外阶段：PBP催化转糖基和转肽反应。
★★ Cell division
PG synthesis/remodeling concentrates at septum under divisome control; antibiotics targeting PG stop division. ｜ 细胞分裂：PG合成/重塑集中在隔膜处，受divisome控制；抗PG合成药物阻断分裂。

4.3 Antibiotic targets and resistance｜抗生素靶点与耐药

★★★ Penicillins/β-lactams target PBPs responsible for PG cross-linking. ｜ 青霉素/β内酰胺靶向PBP，阻断PG交联。
★★★ Penicillin resistance: β-lactamase production or PBPs with lower sensitivity. ｜ 青霉素耐药：产生β内酰胺酶或选择低敏感性PBP。
★★★ Glycopeptides target D-Ala-D-Ala and block PG cross-linking/reticulation. ｜ 糖肽类靶向D-Ala-D-Ala，阻断PG交联/网状化。
★★ Glycopeptide resistance: D-Ala-D-Ala → D-Ala-D-Lac or D-Ala-D-Ser; reduced affinity. ｜ 糖肽耐药：D-Ala-D-Ala变为D-Ala-D-Lac或D-Ala-D-Ser，亲和力下降。
★★★ Four broad antibiotic targets: cell wall synthesis, protein synthesis, nucleic acid synthesis, folate metabolism. ｜ 四大抗生素靶点：细胞壁、蛋白合成、核酸合成、叶酸代谢。
★★★ Four broad resistance mechanisms: enzymatic inactivation, target modification, efflux, decreased permeability. ｜ 四大耐药机制：酶灭活、靶点改变、外排、通透性降低。

4.4 PG recycling, host recognition and lysozyme｜PG回收、宿主识别和溶菌酶

★★ PG is continuously remodeled by PG hydrolases during growth and division. ｜ 生长分裂时PG持续被PG水解酶重塑。
★★ Hydrolases include glucosaminidase, endopeptidases, carboxypeptidase, muramidase/lytic transglycosylase, amidase. ｜ 水解酶包括葡萄糖胺苷酶、内肽酶、羧肽酶、胞壁酸酶/裂解转糖基酶、酰胺酶。
★★★ Lysozyme cleaves between N-acetylglucosamine and N-acetylmuramic acid. ｜ 溶菌酶切开N-乙酰葡萄糖胺和N-乙酰胞壁酸之间的键。
★★ Bacteria can modify PG with N-deacetylases to reduce lysozyme action; C. difficile PgdA is the example. ｜ 细菌可用N-脱乙酰酶改造PG降低溶菌酶作用；C. difficile PgdA是例子。
★★★ NOD1 senses iE-DAP; NOD2 senses MDP; both lead to NF-kB activation and inflammation. ｜ NOD1识别iE-DAP；NOD2识别MDP；二者都导致NF-kB激活和炎症。

5. Capsule and S-layer｜荚膜与S层

★★★ Capsule
outer polysaccharide or polypeptide layer; blocks phagocytosis and masks epitopes. ｜ 荚膜：外层多糖或多肽结构；阻断吞噬并遮蔽表位。
★★ Capsules are vaccine antigens: S. pneumoniae, H. influenzae examples. ｜ 荚膜可作为疫苗抗原，如肺炎链球菌、流感嗜血杆菌。
★★ B. anthracis capsule: poly-γ-D-glutamic acid, non-immunogenic; capABCDE for synthesis/anchoring; capD affects virulence. ｜ 炭疽芽孢杆菌荚膜：poly-γ-D-glutamic acid，免疫原性低；capABCDE合成/锚定，capD影响毒力。
★★ S-layer
self-assembled two-dimensional crystalline protein network, often the most external envelope layer. ｜ S层：自组装二维晶体蛋白网络，常为最外层包膜结构。
★★ S-layer roles
cell integrity, permeability barrier, division, adhesion, aggregation/biofilm, anti-phagocytosis. ｜ S层作用：维持完整性、通透屏障、分裂、黏附、聚集/生物膜、抗吞噬。
★ C. difficile S-layer: SlpA precursor cleaved into HMW-SLP and LMW-SLP; variability contributes to antigenic diversity. ｜ 艰难梭菌S层：SlpA前体裂解为HMW-SLP和LMW-SLP；变异导致抗原多样性。

6. Host recognition and innate immunity｜宿主识别与先天免疫

★★★ PAMPs
LPS, peptidoglycan, lipopeptides, lipoarabinomannans, flagellin, bacterial DNA motifs. ｜ PAMP：LPS、PG、脂肽、脂阿拉伯甘露聚糖、鞭毛蛋白、细菌DNA基序。
★★★ PRRs
scavenger receptors, C-type lectin receptors, TLRs, NOD-like receptors/NLRs, RLRs, DNA sensors. ｜ PRR：清道夫受体、C型凝集素受体、TLR、NLR/NOD样受体、RLR、DNA传感器。
★★★ Recognition logic: PAMP recognition → signaling cascade → transcription factors → cytokines/inflammation. ｜ 识别逻辑：PAMP识别 → 信号级联 → 转录因子 → 细胞因子/炎症。
★★ TLRs are type I transmembrane proteins with LRR domains and a cytoplasmic TIR domain. ｜ TLR是I型跨膜蛋白，具有LRR结构域和胞质TIR结构域。
★★★ Surface TLRs
TLR1/2/4/5/6/11 recognize extracellular PAMPs; endosomal TLR3/7/8/9/10 recognize nucleic-acid PAMPs. ｜ 表面TLR识别胞外PAMP；内体TLR3/7/8/9/10识别核酸类PAMP。

Sensor｜受体

Ligand｜配体

Exam output｜输出

TLR4

LPS / Lipid A

NF-kB/AP-1 cytokines; TRIF/IRF3 type I IFN

TLR5

Flagellin

MAPK/NF-kB, pro-inflammatory cytokines

NOD1

iE-DAP

Cytosolic surveillance, NF-kB inflammation

NOD2

MDP

Cytosolic surveillance, NF-kB inflammation

NLRC4

Flagellin or T3/T4 secretion-system signals

Inflammasome, caspase activation, IL-1β/IL-18

★★ Inflammasome
professional innate immune cells and epithelial cells can activate caspases and mature inflammatory mediators. ｜ 炎症小体：天然免疫细胞和上皮细胞中可激活caspase并成熟炎症介质。
★★ Bacterial immune evasion mechanisms include surface modification, complement inhibition, antigenic variation, impaired signaling, toxin secretion, phagocytosis inhibition and cell-death modulation. ｜ 免疫逃逸包括表面改变、补体抑制、抗原变异、干扰信号、分泌毒素、抑制吞噬和调控细胞死亡。

7. Horizontal gene transfer and mobile elements｜水平基因转移与移动遗传元件

★★★ Gene flow increases microbial diversity and may affect metabolism, antigenic composition, virulence and antibiotic resistance. ｜ 基因流增加微生物多样性，可影响代谢、抗原组成、毒力和抗生素耐药。
★★★ Foreign DNA must be stably acquired by homologous recombination, autonomous replication as plasmid, or integration; otherwise it is diluted/lost. ｜ 外源DNA必须通过同源重组、自主复制质粒或整合稳定存在，否则会被稀释/丢失。
★★★ Homologous recombination: RecA-mediated double crossing-over, substitution/allelic exchange. ｜ 同源重组：RecA介导双交换，实现替换/等位基因交换。

7.1 Mobile elements｜移动元件

★★★ Transposons
DNA elements with transposase; move without sequence homology; can carry resistance or virulence genes. ｜ 转座子：带transposase的DNA元件；不需序列同源即可移动；可携带耐药/毒力基因。
★★ Transposon properties: move chromosome ↔ plasmid; not replicative; can modify gene expression positively or negatively. ｜ 转座子特性：可在染色体和质粒间移动；不自我复制；可正/负调控基因表达。
★★ Integrons
gene capture and expression systems; intI integrase + attI site + Pc promoter; cassettes often promoterless resistance ORFs. ｜ 整合子：基因捕获和表达系统；intI整合酶+attI位点+Pc启动子；盒常为无启动子的耐药ORF。
★★ Integrons are not mobile by themselves but often sit on transposons or plasmids. ｜ 整合子本身通常不能移动，但常位于转座子或质粒上。
★★★ Plasmids
extrachromosomal DNA, non-essential, autonomous replication; circular usually; copy number 1 to >100. ｜ 质粒：染色体外DNA，非必需，自主复制；通常环状；拷贝数1到>100。

7.2 Three transfer mechanisms｜三种基因转移机制

Mechanism｜机制

Definition｜定义

High-yield keywords｜高频关键词

Transformation

Uptake of free/naked DNA by competent bacteria

Competence, pilus/channel, ssDNA entry, RecA homologous recombination; artificial heat shock/electroporation

Conjugation

DNA transfer by direct contact

F factor, pilus, tra region, relaxase, donor/recipient; plasmid and resistance spread

Transduction

DNA transfer carried by bacteriophage

Phage receptor, DNA injection; generalized = non-specific; specialized = genes near attB site

Minimal answer / 最简答案：Transformation = free DNA uptake. Conjugation = direct cell-to-cell DNA transfer, often plasmid-mediated. Transduction = bacteriophage-mediated DNA transfer.

8. Gene expression and regulation｜基因表达与调控

★★ Bacterial genes
average ~1000 bases, usually no introns, many genes per bacterium, high coding density. ｜ 细菌基因平均约1000碱基，通常无内含子，编码密度高。
★★★ Operon
DNA segment with genes under the same promoter; one mRNA can encode several proteins. ｜ 操纵子：多个基因受同一启动子控制；一条mRNA可编码多个蛋白。
★★ In bacteria, transcription and translation can be coupled. ｜ 细菌中转录和翻译可耦联进行。
★★ ORF
open reading frame; one codon = 3 bases; common start ATG, sometimes TTG; stop codons TAA/TGA/TAG. ｜ ORF开放阅读框；1个密码子=3个碱基；起始常ATG，有时TTG；终止TAA/TGA/TAG。
★★★ Transcription stages: initiation, elongation, termination. ｜ 转录阶段：起始、延伸、终止。
★★★ Sigma factor forms RNAP holoenzyme and directs RNA polymerase to promoter regions. ｜ σ因子形成RNA聚合酶全酶并引导其识别启动子。
★★★ E. coli sigma70 promoter consensus: -35 TTGACA and -10 TATAAT. ｜ E. coli σ70启动子共识序列：-35 TTGACA 和 -10 TATAAT。
★★ Termination
intrinsic hairpin + U-rich sequence, Rho-dependent termination, or Mfd-associated activity. ｜ 终止：内源性发夹+U-rich，Rho依赖，或Mfd相关。
★★ Translation initiation uses RBS/Shine-Dalgarno sequence. ｜ 翻译起始依赖RBS/Shine-Dalgarno序列。

8.1 Regulation levels｜调控层次

★★★ Constitutive genes are continuously expressed; regulated genes are expressed only under specific conditions/stimuli. ｜ 组成型基因持续表达；调控型基因仅在特定条件/刺激下表达。
★★★ Alternative sigma factors redirect transcription under stress/developmental conditions; often controlled by anti-sigma factors. ｜ 替代σ因子在应激/发育条件下重定向转录；常由anti-σ因子控制。
★★ Transcription factors bind DNA near promoter or polymerase; HTH and RHH are important groups. ｜ 转录因子结合启动子附近DNA或聚合酶；HTH和RHH是重要类型。
★★★ Lac operon
without lactose, LacI repressor binds operator and blocks lacZYA; with lactose, repression is relieved. ｜ 乳糖操纵子：无乳糖时LacI阻遏子结合operator阻断lacZYA；有乳糖时解除阻遏。
★★ Riboswitches regulate transcription termination/antitermination or translation depending on ligand binding. ｜ 核糖开关根据配体结合调控转录终止/抗终止或翻译。
★★ Hfq binds RNA and helps sRNAs pair with target mRNAs; it can suppress or protect protein synthesis. ｜ Hfq是RNA结合蛋白，帮助sRNA与靶mRNA配对，可抑制或保护蛋白合成。
★★ Post-translational regulation: proteolytic cleavage, phosphorylation, acetylation, methylation, disulfide bridge formation. ｜ 翻译后调控：蛋白水解切割、磷酸化、乙酰化、甲基化、二硫键形成。
★★ Two-component systems: sensor histidine kinase detects stimulus and phosphorylates response regulator, often an HTH DNA-binding protein. ｜ 双组分系统：组氨酸激酶感受刺激并磷酸化反应调节蛋白，后者常为HTH DNA结合蛋白。

9. Host-pathogen interaction: cytoskeleton hacking｜宿主-病原相互作用：细胞骨架劫持

★★ Cytoskeleton types: microfilaments(actin), microtubules(tubulin), intermediate filaments(various), septins. ｜ 细胞骨架类型：微丝actin、微管tubulin、中间丝多种蛋白、septins。
★★ Actin exists as G-actin and F-actin; polymerization is polarized with barbed + end and pointed - end. ｜ 肌动蛋白有G-actin和F-actin；聚合有极性，+端快、-端慢。
★★ Rho-family small GTPases: Rho, Rac, Cdc42 regulate actin remodeling. ｜ Rho家族小GTP酶Rho/Rac/Cdc42调节actin重塑。

9.1 Bacterial toxins acting on cytoskeleton｜作用于细胞骨架的毒素

★★ Direct action
ADP-ribosylation of G-actin blocks polymerization while depolymerization continues. ｜ 直接作用：ADP核糖基化G-actin，阻断聚合而去聚合继续。
★★ Rho inactivation: glycosylation/N-acetylglucosaminylation or ADP-ribosylation blocks interaction with effectors. ｜ Rho失活：糖基化/N-乙酰葡萄糖胺化或ADP核糖基化阻断与效应因子相互作用。
★★ Rho activation
CNF/DNT deamidation keeps Rho active, causing stress fibers, multinucleation, lamellipodia/ruffles. ｜ Rho激活：CNF/DNT脱酰胺化使Rho持续活化，导致应力纤维、多核、片状伪足等。

9.2 Entry into non-phagocytic cells｜进入非吞噬细胞

Mechanism｜机制

Key features｜关键特征

Exam memory｜记忆

Zipper

Close contact; receptor recognition such as cadherin/integrin; tyrosine kinase/PI3K; minor actin reorganization; one bacterium at a time

贴近膜、受体介导、actin变化小

Trigger

Brief contact; TTSS/T3SS effectors; massive actin rearrangement and macropinocytosis; several bacteria internalized

T3SS效应蛋白、actin大重排、巨胞饮

★★ Why enter cells
immune escape/niche and deep tissue invasion. ｜ 为什么进入细胞：逃避免疫/建立生态位和深部组织侵袭。

9.3 Salmonella, Listeria and EPEC examples｜沙门菌、李斯特菌和EPEC

★★ Salmonella invasion: bacterial proteins act as GEFs for Rac/Cdc42 → WASP → Arp2/3 activation → actin rearrangement. ｜ 沙门菌侵袭：效应蛋白作为Rac/Cdc42的GEF → WASP → Arp2/3 → actin重排。
★★ Salmonella intracellular survival depends on all three networks: MFs maintain vacuolar membrane; MTs control membrane dynamics/Sifs; IFs maintain perinuclear/Golgi localization. ｜ 沙门菌胞内存活依赖三类骨架：微丝维持液泡膜，微管控制膜动力学/Sifs，中间丝维持核周/Golgi定位。
★★ Listeria ActA mimics WASP, recruits/activates Arp2/3, binds G-actin and VASP, forming actin tails for cell-to-cell spread. ｜ 李斯特菌ActA模拟WASP，招募/激活Arp2/3，结合G-actin和VASP，形成actin尾进行细胞间传播。
★ EPEC induces extracellular actin polymerization under attachment sites, forming pedestals through Tir/Nck/N-WASP/Arp2/3. ｜ EPEC在附着位点下诱导actin聚合形成pedestal，涉及Tir/Nck/N-WASP/Arp2/3。

10. Bacterial spores｜细菌孢子

★★★ Bacterial spores are dormant cellular forms of Gram-positive bacteria with high stability and extreme resistance. ｜ 细菌孢子是革兰阳性菌的休眠细胞形态，稳定性高，耐受极端条件。
★★★ Not a reproduction form. It is a survival form. ｜ 不是繁殖形式，而是生存形式。
★★ Major spore-forming human pathogens: B. anthracis, B. cereus, C. tetani, C. botulinum, C. perfringens, C. difficile. ｜ 主要人类致病产孢菌：炭疽芽孢杆菌、蜡样芽孢杆菌、破伤风梭菌、肉毒梭菌、产气荚膜梭菌、艰难梭菌。

10.1 Spore structure｜孢子结构

★★★ Core
DNA, RNA, enzymes, ATP/NADH; very low water; lower pH; contains Ca-DPA and SASP. ｜ 核心：DNA、RNA、酶、ATP/NADH；水分极低、pH较低；含Ca-DPA和SASP。
★★★ DPA + Ca2+ decreases core water content and is absent from vegetative cells. ｜ DPA与Ca2+形成复合物，降低核心含水量，营养细胞中没有。
★★★ SASP proteins bind/saturate spore DNA and protect against UV and other damaging agents. ｜ SASP蛋白结合/饱和孢子DNA，保护其免受UV等损伤。
★★★ Inner membrane is very low permeability and protects the core even against water. ｜ 内膜通透性极低，甚至阻止水进入核心。
★★★ Cortex is a thick special PG layer with muramic-δ-lactam; degraded during germination. ｜ 皮层是厚的特殊PG层，含muramic-δ-lactam；萌发时被降解。
★★ Coat is made of several protein layers; basal layer, inner coat and outer coat; protects against chemicals and enzymes. ｜ 孢衣由多层蛋白组成：基底层、内孢衣、外孢衣；抵抗化学物和酶。
★★ Exosporium exists in B. cereus, B. thuringiensis, B. anthracis, but not B. subtilis. ｜ 外孢层存在于B. cereus、B. thuringiensis、B. anthracis，不存在于B. subtilis。

10.2 Sporulation and germination｜产孢与萌发

★★★ Sporulation trigger: prolonged nutrient deprivation. ｜ 产孢触发：长期营养缺乏。
★★★ Stages
chromosome condensation → asymmetric division → engulfment → cortex synthesis → coat synthesis → mother-cell lysis/spore release. ｜ 阶段：染色体凝缩 → 不对称分裂 → 吞入 → 皮层合成 → 孢衣合成 → 母细胞裂解释放。
★★★ Spo0A phosphorylation is the decision point to enter sporulation; Spo0A-P controls many genes/operons. ｜ Spo0A磷酸化是进入产孢的决策点；Spo0A-P调控大量基因/操纵子。
★★ Sigma cascade
SigF in forespore and SigE in mother cell at stage II; later SigG in forespore and SigK in mother cell. ｜ σ级联：II期forespore激活SigF，母细胞激活SigE；后期forespore SigG，母细胞SigK。
★★★ Germination
germinant sensing → ion release → Ca-DPA release → cortex-lytic enzymes → cortex hydrolysis → core rehydration → metabolism/outgrowth. ｜ 萌发：识别萌发物 → 离子释放 → Ca-DPA释放 → 皮层裂解酶 → 皮层水解 → 核心复水 → 代谢恢复/长出。
★★ B. subtilis germinant: L-alanine. C. difficile germinants: taurocholate and glycine. ｜ B. subtilis萌发物：L-alanine；C. difficile萌发物：taurocholate和glycine。

10.3 Spore resistance and pathogen examples｜孢子抵抗性与致病例子

★★ Resistance factors: low core water, SASP/DNA repair, DPA, inner membrane, cortex, coat, exosporium, detoxifying enzymes. ｜ 抵抗因素：核心低含水量、SASP/DNA修复、DPA、内膜、皮层、孢衣、外孢层、解毒酶。
★★ Spore heat resistance can be about 100 times greater than vegetative cells. ｜ 孢子耐热性可约为营养细胞的100倍。

Organism｜菌

Disease / key point｜疾病/关键词

Memory sentence｜背诵句

B. anthracis

Anthrax; spores survive in soil for decades

Spore + anthrax toxin + respiratory/wound route

B. cereus

Food toxic infection; rice/plants

Diarrheal and emetic symptoms

C. botulinum

Botulism; flaccid paralysis

Spores survive pasteurization and germinate in anaerobic stored foods

C. perfringens

Diarrhea and gas gangrene

Spores in food or wound; toxins after germination

C. tetani

Tetanus; lockjaw/spasms

Spores contaminate wound; toxin causes spastic paralysis

C. difficile

Antibiotic-associated diarrhea, pseudomembranous colitis

Gut dysbiosis allows spores to germinate; toxins damage colon

11. Bacterial toxins｜细菌毒素

★★★ Endotoxin
cell-associated LPS released after bacterial disruption; active component is Lipid A. ｜ 内毒素：细胞相关LPS，细菌破裂后释放；活性部分是Lipid A。
★★★ Exotoxin
protein toxin synthesized inside bacteria and released/delivered to target cells. ｜ 外毒素：细菌内合成的蛋白毒素，释放或递送到靶细胞。
★★ Toxin genes may be on chromosome, plasmids, bacteriophages or pathogenicity islands; same species may be toxigenic or non-toxigenic. ｜ 毒素基因可在染色体、质粒、噬菌体或致病岛上；同一物种可有产毒/不产毒株。
★★ Pathogenicity island: cluster of virulence genes acquired by horizontal gene transfer; flanked by repeats/IS/tRNA genes. ｜ 致病岛：通过水平转移获得的一簇毒力基因，侧翼常有重复序列/插入序列/tRNA基因。

11.1 Three toxin classes in the slides｜课件三大毒素类

Class｜类别

Mechanism｜机制

Examples｜例子

Pore-forming toxins (PFTs)

Bind membrane → oligomerize → pore → ion/molecule leakage and lysis

Colicins, ClyA, S. aureus Hla/PVL, aerolysin, C. perfringens ε-toxin

Membrane-damaging SMases/PLases

Hydrolyze sphingomyelin/phospholipids; destroy membrane and tissues

B. cereus SMase C, P. aeruginosa ExoU, C. perfringens alpha-toxin/CPA

Toxins with intracellular targets

Bind receptor → internalization → translocation → enzymatic activity

Botulinum, tetanus, diphtheria, cholera, anthrax, C. difficile TcdA/TcdB

★★★ PFT general mechanism: soluble monomer → receptor binding → oligomerization/pre-pore → transmembrane pore → permeability change. ｜ PFT总机制：可溶单体 → 受体结合 → 寡聚/预孔 → 跨膜孔 → 通透性改变。
★★ α-PFTs insert α-helices; β-PFTs assemble β-strand domains into mushroom-like pores. ｜ α-PFT插入α螺旋；β-PFT用β链结构形成蘑菇样孔。
★★ SMases/PLases are esterases targeting glycerophospholipids, sphingolipids and cholesterol of cell membranes. ｜ SMase/PLase是酯酶，靶向膜上的甘油磷脂、鞘脂和胆固醇。
★★ ExoU of P. aeruginosa is a patatin-like PLA2 delivered by T3SS and activated by host cofactors. ｜ 铜绿假单胞菌ExoU是patatin样PLA2，经T3SS递送并由宿主辅助因子激活。
★★ C. perfringens alpha-toxin/CPA hydrolyzes PC and SM, producing DAG and ceramide, causing inflammation/myonecrosis/gas gangrene. ｜ 产气荚膜梭菌α毒素/CPA水解PC和SM，产生DAG/ceramide，引发炎症、肌坏死和气性坏疽。

11.2 Intracellular toxin families and high-yield examples｜胞内靶向毒素家族和例子

★★ ABRT
A catalytic chain + B chain with receptor-binding and translocation domains. ｜ ABRT：A催化链 + B链具有受体结合和转位结构域。
★★ AB5
one catalytic A + pentameric B binding domain. ｜ AB5：一个催化A链 + 五聚体B结合域。
★★ AnB7
catalytic A components bind to B heptamer/oligomer after surface binding. ｜ AnB7：催化A组分结合B七聚体/寡聚体后进入细胞。

Toxin｜毒素

Target/mechanism｜靶点/机制

Clinical effect｜临床效果

Botulinum toxin

SNARE cleavage; BoNT/A,E target SNAP25; B,D,F,G target VAMP; C targets syntaxin/SNAP25

Blocks neurotransmitter release at NMJ → flaccid paralysis

Tetanus toxin

TeTx targets VAMP; retrograde transport; blocks inhibitory interneurons

Spastic paralysis, rigidity, spasms

Diphtheria toxin

ADP-ribosylates EF2 using NAD+

Stops protein synthesis; throat gray membrane, heart/liver necrosis

C. difficile TcdA/TcdB

Glucosylates/inactivates Rho, Rac, Cdc42

Actin disruption, colitis

Cholera toxin

ADP-ribosylates Gsα → adenylate cyclase↑ → cAMP↑ → PKA → CFTR Cl- secretion

Watery diarrhea, dehydration

Anthrax toxin

PA binds/forms pore; EF = calmodulin-dependent adenylate cyclase; LF = zinc protease cleaving MAPKK

Edema + lethal/apoptotic signaling

Iota toxin

Ia ADP-ribosylates G-actin; Ib binding/pore component

Actin depolymerization, cell rounding

One-line toxin recall / 毒素一行背：Botulinum = flaccid paralysis; tetanus = spastic paralysis; diphtheria = EF2/protein synthesis off; cholera = cAMP/CFTR watery diarrhea; anthrax = PA + EF edema + LF MAPKK cleavage; C. difficile = Rho GTPases/actin disruption.

Organism strategy / 菌种策略：别把代表菌背成百科。每个菌只要能写 2-3 个标签：Gram/morphology + disease + one virulence/resistance mechanism。

13. Ready-made short-answer templates｜可直接套用的短答模板

Describe the Gram-negative envelope.：The Gram-negative envelope consists of an inner/cytoplasmic membrane, a periplasmic space containing thin peptidoglycan, and an asymmetric outer membrane. The outer leaflet of the outer membrane contains LPS, composed of Lipid A, core oligosaccharide and O-antigen. It provides a permeability barrier and contributes to endotoxin activity through Lipid A.
Describe the Gram-positive surface.：The Gram-positive surface contains a cytoplasmic membrane and a thick peptidoglycan layer. Wall teichoic acids are anchored in peptidoglycan, whereas lipoteichoic acids are anchored in the membrane. Surface proteins, S-layer and capsule may contribute to adhesion, immune evasion and virulence.
Explain why Lipid A is important.：Lipid A is the conserved active component of LPS endotoxin. It is recognized after transfer by LBP to CD14/MD-2/TLR4. TLR4 signaling activates MyD88-dependent NF-kB/AP-1 pathways and TRIF-dependent IRF3 signaling, leading to cytokines, fever and possibly sepsis or toxic shock.
Compare transformation, conjugation and transduction.：Transformation is uptake of free DNA by competent bacteria. Conjugation requires direct cell-to-cell contact, usually through a pilus and conjugative plasmid. Transduction is bacteriophage-mediated DNA transfer and may be generalized or specialized.
Explain peptidoglycan as an antibiotic target.：Peptidoglycan is a glycan-peptide mesh essential for bacterial shape and osmotic protection. PBPs catalyze transglycosylation and transpeptidation during PG synthesis. β-lactams inhibit PBPs, whereas glycopeptides bind D-Ala-D-Ala and block cross-linking.
Explain spore resistance.：Spore resistance depends on low core water content, Ca-DPA, SASP proteins protecting DNA, DNA repair enzymes, low-permeability inner membrane, cortex, coat and sometimes exosporium. These structures protect against heat, UV, chemicals, lysozyme and disinfectants.
Explain cholera toxin mechanism.：Cholera toxin binds GM1, is internalized and transported to the ER. The A1 chain enters the cytoplasm and ADP-ribosylates Gsα, keeping adenylate cyclase active. cAMP increases, PKA activates CFTR chloride secretion, and water follows into the intestinal lumen, causing watery diarrhea.
Explain zipper vs trigger entry.：Zipper entry requires close receptor-mediated contact, such as cadherin or integrin recognition, and produces minor actin rearrangement. Trigger entry uses bacterial T3SS effectors to induce massive actin rearrangement and macropinocytosis, allowing several bacteria to enter.

14. Final 60-line memorization list｜最后直接背的60句

★★★ Gram-positive bacteria have thick peptidoglycan and teichoic/lipoteichoic acids but no outer membrane. ｜ 革兰阳性菌有厚PG和TA/LTA，没有外膜。
★★★ Gram-negative bacteria have inner membrane, periplasm with thin PG, and outer membrane with LPS. ｜ 革兰阴性菌有内膜、含薄PG的周质和含LPS的外膜。
★★★ Outer membrane is asymmetric: LPS outside, phospholipids inside. ｜ 外膜非对称：外侧LPS，内侧磷脂。
★★★ LPS = Lipid A + core oligosaccharide + O-antigen. ｜ LPS=Lipid A+核心寡糖+O抗原。
★★★ Lipid A is the endotoxin-active component. ｜ Lipid A是内毒素活性部分。
★★ O-antigen is variable, immunodominant, non-toxic and useful for serotyping. ｜ O抗原多变、免疫显性、无毒，用于血清分型。
★★★ TLR4 recognizes LPS/Lipid A through LBP, CD14 and MD-2. ｜ TLR4通过LBP、CD14、MD-2识别LPS/Lipid A。
★★★ MyD88 pathway activates NF-kB/AP-1; TRIF pathway activates IRF3/type I IFN. ｜ MyD88激活NF-kB/AP-1；TRIF激活IRF3/I型IFN。
★★★ PG backbone is GlcNAc-MurNAc with peptide side chains. ｜ PG骨架是GlcNAc-MurNAc，带肽侧链。
★★★ PBPs perform PG cross-linking; β-lactams target PBPs. ｜ PBP负责PG交联；β内酰胺靶向PBP。
★★★ Glycopeptides bind D-Ala-D-Ala. ｜ 糖肽类结合D-Ala-D-Ala。
★★★ NOD1 senses iE-DAP; NOD2 senses MDP. ｜ NOD1识别iE-DAP；NOD2识别MDP。
★★ Lysozyme cleaves between GlcNAc and MurNAc. ｜ 溶菌酶切GlcNAc和MurNAc之间的键。
★★ N-deacetylases help bacteria resist lysozyme. ｜ N-脱乙酰酶帮助细菌抵抗溶菌酶。
★★★ Capsule blocks phagocytosis and masks epitopes. ｜ 荚膜阻断吞噬并遮蔽表位。
★★ S-layer is a self-assembled crystalline protein layer. ｜ S层是自组装晶体蛋白层。
★★★ Pili/fimbriae mediate adhesion; F pilus is linked to conjugation. ｜ 菌毛介导黏附；F菌毛与接合有关。
★★ Flagella mediate motility, adhesion, biofilm, protein export and TLR5 recognition. ｜ 鞭毛参与运动、黏附、生物膜、蛋白输出和TLR5识别。
★★★ Transformation = uptake of free DNA. ｜ 转化=摄取游离DNA。
★★★ Conjugation = direct cell-to-cell DNA transfer. ｜ 接合=细胞直接接触转移DNA。
★★★ Transduction = bacteriophage-mediated DNA transfer. ｜ 转导=噬菌体介导DNA转移。
★★★ Transposons move with transposase and can carry resistance/virulence genes. ｜ 转座子依赖转座酶移动，可携带耐药/毒力基因。
★★ Integrons capture gene cassettes, often resistance genes. ｜ 整合子捕获基因盒，常是耐药基因。
★★★ Plasmids replicate autonomously and may spread resistance. ｜ 质粒自主复制，可传播耐药。
★★★ Operon = several genes under one promoter, one polycistronic mRNA. ｜ 操纵子=多个基因受一个启动子控制，一条多顺反子mRNA。
★★★ Sigma factors determine promoter recognition by RNA polymerase. ｜ σ因子决定RNA聚合酶识别启动子。
★★ Sigma70 consensus: -35 TTGACA, -10 TATAAT. ｜ σ70共识序列：-35 TTGACA，-10 TATAAT。
★★ Alternative sigma factors redirect transcription under stress or sporulation. ｜ 替代σ因子在应激或产孢时重定向转录。
★★ Hfq helps sRNAs regulate mRNA translation/stability. ｜ Hfq帮助sRNA调控mRNA翻译/稳定性。
★★ Two-component systems use histidine kinase and response regulator. ｜ 双组分系统用组氨酸激酶和反应调节蛋白。
★★ Zipper entry = receptor-mediated, close contact, minor actin rearrangement. ｜ Zipper进入=受体介导、贴近接触、actin变化小。
★★ Trigger entry = T3SS effectors, massive actin rearrangement, macropinocytosis. ｜ Trigger进入=T3SS效应蛋白、actin大重排、巨胞饮。
★★ Salmonella manipulates microfilaments, microtubules and intermediate filaments. ｜ 沙门菌操纵微丝、微管和中间丝。
★★ Listeria ActA mimics WASP and forms actin tails. ｜ 李斯特菌ActA模拟WASP并形成actin尾。
★★★ Spores are dormant resistant survival forms of Gram-positive bacteria. ｜ 孢子是革兰阳性菌的休眠耐受生存形式。
★★★ Spore core contains DNA, low water, Ca-DPA and SASP. ｜ 孢子核心含DNA、低水分、Ca-DPA和SASP。
★★★ Cortex is special PG; coat is a protein shell; exosporium exists only in some species. ｜ 皮层是特殊PG；孢衣是蛋白壳；外孢层只在部分物种有。
★★★ Spo0A phosphorylation initiates sporulation. ｜ Spo0A磷酸化启动产孢。
★★ Germination releases ions and Ca-DPA, activates CLEs, degrades cortex and rehydrates core. ｜ 萌发释放离子和Ca-DPA，激活CLE，降解皮层并使核心复水。
★★★ Endotoxin is Lipid A of LPS; exotoxins are protein toxins. ｜ 内毒素是LPS的Lipid A；外毒素是蛋白毒素。
★★ PFTs bind membranes, oligomerize and form pores. ｜ 成孔毒素结合膜、寡聚并形成孔。
★★ SMases and phospholipases damage membranes by lipid hydrolysis. ｜ 鞘磷脂酶和磷脂酶通过水解脂质破坏膜。
★★★ Botulinum toxin causes flaccid paralysis. ｜ 肉毒毒素导致弛缓性麻痹。
★★★ Tetanus toxin causes spastic paralysis. ｜ 破伤风毒素导致痉挛性麻痹。
★★★ Diphtheria toxin ADP-ribosylates EF2 and stops protein synthesis. ｜ 白喉毒素ADP核糖基化EF2，停止蛋白合成。
★★★ Cholera toxin increases cAMP and causes watery diarrhea. ｜ 霍乱毒素升高cAMP，导致水样腹泻。
★★★ Anthrax toxin = PA + EF + LF. ｜ 炭疽毒素=PA+EF+LF。
★★ C. difficile toxins inactivate Rho-family GTPases and disrupt actin. ｜ 艰难梭菌毒素失活Rho家族GTP酶并破坏actin。
★★ S. aureus = Gram+ cocci in clusters, abscess and toxin diseases. ｜ 金黄色葡萄球菌=革兰阳性成簇球菌，脓肿和毒素病。
★★ S. pyogenes = chains, pharyngitis and skin infection. ｜ 化脓链球菌=链状，咽炎和皮肤感染。
★★ S. pneumoniae = diplococcus, pneumonia/otitis/sinusitis/meningitis. ｜ 肺炎链球菌=双球菌，肺炎/中耳炎/鼻窦炎/脑膜炎。
★★ E. coli is the classic UTI organism. ｜ E. coli是经典尿路感染菌。
★★ P. aeruginosa = hospital opportunist, biofilm, intrinsic resistance. ｜ 铜绿假单胞菌=医院机会致病、生物膜、固有耐药。
★★ N. meningitidis = meningitis/septicemia; N. gonorrhoeae = STI. ｜ 脑膜炎奈瑟菌=脑膜炎/败血症；淋病奈瑟菌=性传播感染。
★★ Mycobacterium = acid-fast, mycolic acids, chronic persistence. ｜ 分枝杆菌=抗酸、含分枝菌酸、慢性持留。
★★ Bacillus is usually aerobic spore former; Clostridium is anaerobic spore former. ｜ 芽孢杆菌属多为需氧产孢；梭菌属为厌氧产孢。
★★ C. difficile follows gut dysbiosis and causes pseudomembranous colitis. ｜ 艰难梭菌常在肠道菌群失调后导致伪膜性结肠炎。
★★ C. botulinum spores survive pasteurization and germinate in anaerobic foods. ｜ 肉毒梭菌孢子可耐巴氏灭菌，在厌氧食物中萌发。
★★ C. tetani spores contaminate wounds and germinate anaerobically. ｜ 破伤风梭菌孢子污染伤口并在厌氧条件下萌发。

15. Last exam trap list｜最后避坑清单

★★★ Do not confuse outer membrane with cytoplasmic/inner membrane. ｜ 不要把外膜和内膜/细胞质膜混。
★★★ Do not call O-antigen the endotoxin; endotoxin-active component is Lipid A. ｜ 不要把O抗原叫内毒素；内毒素活性部分是Lipid A。
★★★ Do not say spores are reproductive forms; they are dormant survival forms. ｜ 不要说孢子是繁殖形式；它们是休眠生存形式。
★★★ Do not write only 'Gram+ thick, Gram- thin'. Add OM/LPS/TA/LTA and consequence. ｜ 不要只写阳性厚阴性薄，要加外膜/LPS/TA/LTA和后果。
★★ Do not mix transformation and transduction: transformation is free DNA; transduction is phage. ｜ 不要混转化和转导：转化是游离DNA，转导是噬菌体。
★★ Do not mix botulinum and tetanus: botulinum flaccid; tetanus spastic. ｜ 不要混肉毒和破伤风：肉毒弛缓，破伤风痉挛。
★★ Do not write 'all Gram-negative are highly resistant'; write lower permeability because of OM and possible intrinsic resistance. ｜ 不要写所有阴性菌都高度耐药；写外膜导致通透性低、可有固有耐药。
Appendix
Key Bacterial Envelope Diagrams｜附录：细菌包膜与分层图示

Use this section for last-pass visual memorization. Focus on layer names first, then functions.

Figure A1. Gram+ vs Gram- envelope overview｜革兰阳性/阴性包膜总对比

Gram+ envelope = cytoplasmic membrane + thick multilayer peptidoglycan cell wall + teichoic acids/lipoteichoic acids; no outer membrane and no LPS.

Gram− envelope = inner membrane + periplasmic space with thin peptidoglycan + outer membrane containing LPS and porins.

Gram+ cell wall is thick and mainly made of PG; Gram− cell envelope includes both the cell wall and the outer membrane.

PG protects bacteria against osmotic rupture and contributes to bacterial shape.

Figure A2. Gram-positive surface｜革兰阳性菌表面结构

Must know｜必背：

Gram+ surface = cytoplasmic membrane + thick peptidoglycan + teichoic acids/lipoteichoic acids + surface proteins ± S-layer ± capsule.

WTA is covalently anchored in peptidoglycan, whereas LTA is anchored in the cytoplasmic membrane.

Gram+ bacteria have no outer membrane and no LPS.

Surface proteins, S-layer and capsule contribute to adhesion, colonization, virulence and immune evasion.

Figure A3. LPS and Gram-negative outer membrane｜LPS 与革兰阴性菌外膜

Must know｜必背：

Gram− outer membrane is an asymmetric bilayer: phospholipids in the inner leaflet and LPS in the outer leaflet.

LPS = Lipid A + core oligosaccharide + O-antigen.

Lipid A anchors LPS in the outer membrane and is the endotoxin-active component.

Core oligosaccharide links Lipid A to O-antigen.

O-antigen is highly variable and is used for serotyping and host immune interaction.

Figure A4. LPS/TLR4 signaling｜LPS 通过 TLR4 激活先天免疫

Must know
LBP -> CD14/MD-2 -> TLR4 -> MyD88 / TRIF pathways -> cytokines and type I IFN.

Lipid A is the endotoxin-active part of LPS.

LBP transfers LPS to CD14/MD-2, allowing TLR4 recognition and dimerization.

TLR4 activates two pathways: MyD88-dependent pathway → NF-κB/AP-1 → inflammatory cytokines; TRIF-dependent pathway → IRF3 → type I interferons.

Main effects
inflammation, fever, innate immune activation, sepsis/toxic shock in severe cases.

Figure A5. PAMPs on bacterial surfaces｜细菌表面 PAMPs 对比 可被先天免疫识别的主要细菌表面 PAMPs

Gram-positive bacteria have a thick peptidoglycan layer with teichoic acids, polysaccharides and flagella, but no outer membrane and no LPS.革兰阳性菌具有厚肽聚糖层，并含 teichoic acids、polysaccharides 和 flagella，但没有外膜，也没有 LPS。

Gram-negative bacteria have an outer/external membrane containing LPS, a periplasmic space with thin peptidoglycan, an inner/cytoplasmic membrane, and surface structures such as pili/fimbriae and flagella.革兰阴性菌具有含 LPS 的外膜/外侧膜，周质空间内有薄肽聚糖，内侧为细胞质膜，并可有 pili/fimbriae 和 flagella 等表面结构。

High-yield PAMPs
LPS, peptidoglycan, teichoic acid, flagellin, and pili/fimbriae-associated structures.高频 PAMPs：LPS、peptidoglycan、teichoic acid、flagellin，以及 pili/fimbriae 相关结构。

Recognition of these PAMPs by PRRs such as TLRs and NOD-like receptors activates NF-κB signaling, cytokine production and inflammation.这些 PAMPs 被 TLRs、NOD-like receptors 等 PRRs 识别后，可激活 NF-κB 信号、细胞因子产生和炎症反应。

Must know｜必背
Gram+ = thick PG + teichoic acid + no outer membrane + no LPS.

Gram− = outer membrane + LPS + periplasm + thin PG + inner membrane.

Figure A6. Bacterial spore layers｜细菌芽孢分层结构

Must know
Core → Internal membrane → Primordial wall / germ cell wall → Cortex → External membrane → Coat → Exosporium

核心 → 内膜 → 原始壁/胚细胞壁 → 皮层 → 外膜 → 孢衣 → 外被
