2. 课程总地图 / Overall map of TU02 Bacteriology

• Module 1
Bacterial surface - Gram-negative and Gram-positive envelopes, appendages, capsule, S-layer.
• Module 2
Peptidoglycan biology - composition, synthesis, remodeling, recycling, host recognition, antibiotic targets.
• Module 3
Gene transfer - plasmids, transposons, integrons, transformation, conjugation, transduction.
• Module 4
Gene regulation - promoter, sigma factors, operon, transcription factors, lac operon, riboswitch, Hfq/sRNA.
• Module 5
Host-pathogen interaction - PAMPs, PRRs, TLR4, NOD1/2, inflammasome, cytoskeleton hijacking.
• Module 6
Spores and toxins - spore structure/formation/germination and major bacterial toxins.

3. 核心框架：Gram-positive vs Gram-negative

这是整门细菌学最重要的起点。后面的 LPS、endotoxin、peptidoglycan、teichoic acids、capsule、host recognition，几乎都从这里分叉出去。

项目

Gram-positive

Gram-negative

Cell wall

厚 peptidoglycan

薄 peptidoglycan + periplasm

Outer membrane

无

有，且 outer leaflet 含 LPS

Special polymers

Teichoic acids / lipoteichoic acids

LPS

Main host-recognition highlight

PG fragments, TA/LTA

Lipid A / LPS

General consequence

强结构支撑

额外 permeability barrier + endotoxin source

最简答案 / Minimal answerGram-positive bacteria have a thick peptidoglycan layer, contain teichoic acids, and do not have an outer membrane.Gram-negative bacteria have a thin peptidoglycan layer, a periplasm, and an outer membrane containing LPS.

思路分析 / Logic

• 如果题目只问 difference，不要只写“thick vs thin”。最好顺手加上：Gram− 的 outer membrane contains LPS；Gram+ 有 TA/LTA。

• 这一步答对，后面才容易接上 endotoxin、TLR4、PBP、colistin、complement evasion 等内容。

4. Gram-negative bacterial surface

Gram-negative envelope 可以分成三个层次来背：inner membrane -> periplasm with thin PG -> outer membrane with LPS。

• Inner membrane
cytoplasmic membrane, symmetric phospholipid bilayer；负责 selective nutrient transport 和 protein translocation。
• Periplasm
含 soluble proteins 与薄层 peptidoglycan mesh；帮助抵抗 turgor pressure，并维持 cell shape。
• Outer membrane
asymmetric bilayer；inner leaflet 是 phospholipids，outer leaflet 是 LPS；它是重要的 permeability barrier。
LPS 结构 / Structure of LPSLipid A：保守的疏水锚定部分，是 endotoxin 的 active component。Core oligosaccharide：内核常含 Kdo 和 heptoses，外核多为 hexoses。O-antigen：可变的 repeating sugar units，是 serogroup/serotyping 的重要基础。

O-antigen 的考点 / High-yield points

• Highly variable
不同菌株之间差异大，所以常用于 serological classification。

• Immunodominant but non-toxic：免疫上很显眼，但真正的 endotoxin active component 是 Lipid A，不是 O-antigen。

• 可以帮助 complement evasion：O-antigen chain elongation 可使 C3b / MAC deposition 远离表面。

Lipid A / endotoxin

• Lipid A 是 PAMP，可被 LBP -> CD14 / MD-2 -> TLR4 识别。

• TLR4 signaling 包括 MyD88-dependent pathway 和 TRIF-dependent pathway。

• 后果
cytokine release, fever, strong innate immune activation；严重时可到 sepsis / toxic shock。
最简答案 / Minimal answerThe Gram-negative outer membrane is asymmetric and contains LPS in its outer leaflet.Lipid A is the endotoxin-active component of LPS and activates innate immunity through TLR4 signaling.O-antigen is highly variable and contributes to serotyping and immune interaction.

Cell surface appendages in Gram-negative bacteria

• Pili / fimbriae
较细，常与 adhesion、biofilm、DNA uptake、gene transfer 相关。

• Chaperone-usher pili：常见于 E. coli / Shigella / Salmonella；UPEC type I pili 与膀胱黏附相关，P pili 与肾脏相关。

• Type IV pili
动态聚合/去聚合，可参与 adhesion、biofilm、DNA uptake、microcolony formation。

• Conjugative pilus / F pilus：horizontal gene transfer 的关键结构之一。

• Flagella
长鞭状结构，主要负责 motility，但也参与 adhesion、biofilm 和 virulence。

Flagella 高频句

• Flagella are not only motility organelles; they can also contribute to adhesion, biofilm formation, protein export, and innate immune recognition.

5. Gram-positive bacterial surface and peptidoglycan biology

Gram-positive surface 的关键词是：thick PG, teichoic acids, lipoteichoic acids, surface proteins, S-layer, capsule。

• Cytoplasmic membrane + thick peptidoglycan layer。

• Wall teichoic acids (WTA)：anchored in PG；通常是 anionic polymers。

• Lipoteichoic acids (LTA)：anchored in the membrane via glycolipid anchor。

• Surface proteins
可通过 sortase 识别的 LPXTG motif 共价连接到 PG，也可通过 LysM 等 motif 非共价结合。

• Some species have an S-layer；它是最外层的 protein crystalline network。

• Capsule 可位于最外层，帮助 immune evasion，尤其是抗吞噬。

Peptidoglycan composition

• PG 的 backbone 由 N-acetylglucosamine (GlcNAc) 和 N-acetylmuramic acid (MurNAc) 重复组成。

• MurNAc 上连 peptide side chain；典型末端是 D-Ala-D-Ala。

• Cross-linking 决定细胞壁的机械强度；不同菌种的 cross-linking pattern 可以不同。

最简答案 / Minimal answerPeptidoglycan is a rigid mesh made of glycan chains cross-linked by peptides.It determines bacterial shape and protects the cell from osmotic rupture.Gram-positive bacteria have a thick peptidoglycan layer and teichoic/lipoteichoic acids.

PG synthesis & antibiotics

• Cytoplasmic stage：形成前体，再经 MraY / MurG 形成 Lipid I / Lipid II。

• Extramembrane stage：由 PBP 催化 transglycosylation 与 transpeptidation，完成 PG 聚合与交联。

• Penicillins / β-lactams：靶向 PBPs，阻断 PG cross-linking。

• Glycopeptides
靶向 D-Ala-D-Ala，阻断 reticulation / cross-linking。
耐药机制 / Resistance mechanismsAgainst penicillins: β-lactamase production or selection of PBPs with lower sensitivity.Against glycopeptides: target modification such as D-Ala-D-Ala -> D-Ala-D-Lac or D-Ala-D-Ser.

PG recycling and host defense

• 生长和分裂时 PG 持续重塑；多种 PG hydrolases 分别切不同键。

• Lysozyme 可切开 GlcNAc 与 MurNAc 之间的键。

• 某些菌通过 N-deacetylases 改造 PG，降低 lysozyme 效果；C. difficile 的 PgdA 是课件中的例子。

思路分析 / Logic

• PG 在考试里不是孤立知识点。它同时连着：cell shape、Gram+ structure、antibiotic target、host recognition、lysozyme resistance。

• 答题时尽量把“结构 + 功能 + 药物 + 宿主反应”串起来，分数会更完整。

6. Host recognition of bacteria: PAMPs, PRRs, TLR4, NOD1, NOD2

这一块是细菌学和免疫学的交叉高频区。你要会的不只是名词，而是“谁识别谁，随后发生什么”。

• PAMPs
lipopolysaccharide, peptidoglycan, lipopeptides, flagellin, lipoarabinomannan, bacterial DNA motifs。
• PRRs
scavenger receptors, C-type lectin receptors, TLRs, NLRs/NOD-like receptors, DNA sensors。
• 核心逻辑
PAMP recognition -> signaling cascade -> transcription factors -> cytokines / inflammation。

TLR4 and lipid A

• Lipid A is recognized after transfer by LBP to CD14 / MD-2 / TLR4 complex。

• TLR4 dimerization and endocytosis trigger two pathways: MyD88-dependent and TRIF-dependent。

• MyD88-dependent signaling activates IRAK / TRAF6 / TAK1 / IKK -> NF-kB and AP-1 -> pro-inflammatory genes。

• TRIF-dependent signaling can activate IRF3 -> type I interferons (IFNa / IFNb).

最简答案 / Minimal answerLipid A is the endotoxin-active component of LPS and is recognized by the CD14/MD-2/TLR4 system.TLR4 signaling activates NF-kB, AP-1 and IRF3, leading to inflammatory cytokine and type I interferon production.Pathological outcomes include fever, sepsis and toxic shock.

NOD1 and NOD2

• NOD1 senses iE-DAP (D-Glu-meso-diaminopimelic acid).

• NOD2 senses muramyl dipeptide (MDP).

• Both are cytosolic sensors and lead to NF-kB activation and inflammation.

Inflammasome / NLRC4 and others

• Flagellin and some secretion-system-associated signals can activate NLRC4-related inflammasome pathways。

• Inflammasome activation links bacterial sensing to caspase activation and inflammatory mediator maturation。

• 在复习层面，记住“flagellin -> NLRC4”这个配对就很值分。

易混点 / Common confusion

• TLR4 主要联想到 LPS / Lipid A；NOD1/NOD2 联想到 PG fragments。

• O-antigen 是可变的免疫学部分，但 endotoxin active component 是 Lipid A，不是 O-antigen。

• NOD1 对 iE-DAP，NOD2 对 MDP，这两个不要记反。

7. Horizontal gene transfer and mobile genetic elements

这部分建议你按两层来背
第一层是 DNA acquired 后如何稳定存在；第二层是 DNA 怎么从一个菌到另一个菌。

• 外源 DNA 进入细菌后，要么通过 homologous recombination 取代原位序列，要么作为 plasmid autonomous replication，要么作为 integrative DNA 插入；否则会被稀释丢失。

• 转座子、integron、plasmid 都是高频移动遗传元件；它们常和 resistance / virulence 相关。

Homologous recombination

• RecA is the key protein in homologous recombination.

• Double crossover leads to substitution / allelic exchange and allows donor DNA to become stably inherited.

Transposons, integrons and plasmids

• Transposons
依赖 transposase；可在 chromosome 与 plasmid 间移动；不要求 sequence homology；可携带 resistance or virulence genes。
• Integrons
gene capture and expression system；依赖 integrase；常收集 promoterless gene cassettes，特别是 resistance genes；integrons 本身 often are not self-mobile，但常位于 transposons 或 plasmids 上。
• Plasmids
distinct from chromosomal DNA, non-essential for survival, autonomous replication；copy number can vary widely。
最简答案 / Minimal answerTransposons are mobile DNA elements that move without requiring extensive sequence homology and may carry resistance or virulence genes.Integrons capture and express gene cassettes, often antibiotic-resistance cassettes.Plasmids are extrachromosomal DNA molecules capable of autonomous replication.

机制

本质

高频关键词

Transformation

Free DNA uptake by competent bacteria

Natural competence; single-stranded DNA enters and may recombine via RecA

Conjugation

Direct contact between donor and recipient

Pilus/F plasmid/tra genes; important for plasmid and resistance spread

Transduction

Bacteriophage-mediated DNA transfer

Generalized = non-specific; Specialized = genes near phage attachment site

How to distinguish them in an exam

• Transformation
关键词是 competent bacteria, free DNA, RecA。
• Conjugation
关键词是 direct contact, pilus, F factor, tra genes。
• Transduction
关键词是 bacteriophage；generalized vs specialized。

8. Gene expression and regulation in bacteria

这部分是典型机制题区域。你至少要会 promoter、sigma factor、operon、lac operon、riboswitch、Hfq。

• Bacterial genes often lack introns and can be grouped in operons.

• One promoter can drive one polycistronic mRNA and therefore several proteins.

• In bacteria, transcription and translation are coupled.

Promoter, transcription and sigma factors

• Promoter recognition is the first step of transcription initiation.

• For the classical sigma 70 promoters in E. coli, the consensus sequences are around -35 TTGACA and -10 TATAAT.

• Sigma factors bind the core RNA polymerase and direct it to promoter regions.

• Alternative sigma factors allow transcription of specific stress- or developmental-response genes.

最简答案 / Minimal answerSigma factors determine promoter recognition by RNA polymerase.Alternative sigma factors redirect transcription under specific conditions such as stress or sporulation.An operon is a group of genes under the control of a single promoter, producing one polycistronic mRNA.

Operon and lac operon

• Operon = several genes under one promoter.

• lacI encodes the repressor; in the absence of lactose, the repressor binds the operator and blocks transcription of lacZ, lacY, lacA.

• When lactose is present, repression is relieved and the operon can be expressed.

Other levels of regulation

• Transcription factors can positively or negatively regulate transcription by binding DNA near promoters or interacting with RNA polymerase.

• Riboswitches regulate transcription termination/antitermination or translation depending on ligand binding.

• Hfq is an RNA-binding protein that helps regulatory small RNAs pair with target mRNAs and can inhibit or protect translation.

• Post-translational regulation includes proteolytic cleavage, phosphorylation, acetylation, methylation, and disulfide bond formation.

思路分析 / Logic

• 如果题目问 global regulation，不要只写 operon；应至少提 alternative sigma factors + transcription factors + riboswitch + Hfq/sRNA。

• 如果题目问 lac operon，关键不是讲代谢，而是讲 repression / derepression 逻辑。

9. Host-pathogen interaction and cytoskeleton hijacking

这部分很容易被讲得很复杂，但考试里通常抓几个大机制：actin、Rho GTPases、entry into cells、intracellular survival、actin-based motility。

• Actin exists as G-actin and F-actin; polymerization, depolymerization and treadmilling are dynamic processes.

• Small Rho-family GTPases such as Rho, Rac and Cdc42 regulate actin remodeling.

• Many bacterial virulence factors directly or indirectly modify actin or these small GTPases.

Two entry mechanisms: zipper and trigger

• Zipper mechanism
细菌紧贴膜面，识别受体如 integrins/cadherins，使用宿主 signal transduction；actin reorganization 相对较小；通常一次进入一个细菌。

• Trigger mechanism：通过 TTSS/T3SS effectors 诱导 massive actin rearrangement 和 macropinocytosis；可同时内化多个细菌。

最简答案 / Minimal answerZipper entry relies on close receptor-mediated interaction and limited actin rearrangement.Trigger entry relies on bacterial effectors, strong actin remodeling and macropinocytosis.

Salmonella and intracellular survival

• Salmonella trigger invasion involves bacterial effectors acting as GEFs for Rac/Cdc42, leading to WASP and Arp2/3 activation.

• During intracellular survival, actin helps maintain vacuole integrity, microtubules control membrane dynamics, and intermediate filaments help perinuclear localization.

• 课件里一个关键结论是
Salmonella simultaneously interferes with microfilaments, microtubules and intermediate filaments.

Actin-based motility

• Listeria uses ActA, a WASP mimic, to recruit Arp2/3, G-actin and Ena/VASP, producing actin tails.

• This allows intracellular movement and spread to adjacent cells.

• Some extracellular pathogens such as EPEC can also induce actin polymerization beneath attachment sites, forming pedestals.

10. Bacterial spores

孢子这一块非常适合出结构题和机制题。建议按 definition -> layers -> sporulation -> germination -> clinical examples 的顺序背。

• Bacterial spores are dormant cellular forms of Gram-positive bacteria.

• They are highly stable, can survive for years, and resist many harsh environmental conditions.

• Spore-forming pathogens are epidemiologically important in foodborne disease, environmental persistence and hospital transmission.

Spore structure

• 主要层次
core -> inner membrane -> primordial peptidoglycan / germ cell wall -> cortex -> coat -> exosporium (only in some species).
• Core
含 DNA, RNA, enzymes, ATP/NADH；water content 很低，pH 较低。

• DPA (dipicolinic acid) with Ca2+：显著降低核心含水量，是耐受性的重要来源。

• SASP proteins
small acid-soluble proteins，结合并保护 DNA，尤其抗 UV damage。
最简答案 / Minimal answerThe spore core contains DNA, enzymes, low water content, Ca-DPA and SASP proteins.The cortex is a specialized peptidoglycan layer and the coat is a multilayered protein shell.Some species also possess an exosporium outside the coat.

Cortex and coat

• Cortex 是厚的特殊 peptidoglycan；其中大量 MurNAc residues undergo modification and muramic-d-lactam formation。

• Coat is made of several protein layers and contains many spore-specific proteins.

• Exosporium 并非所有孢子都有；B. anthracis, B. cereus 等可有，B. subtilis 没有。

Sporulation: stages and regulation

• Sporulation is triggered by prolonged nutrient deprivation.

• Key developmental logic：asymmetric division produces a forespore and a mother cell；engulfment follows；then cortex and coat are synthesized；finally mother cell lyses and releases the mature spore.

• Spo0A phosphorylation is the decision point for entry into sporulation.

• Alternative sigma factor cascade：SigF and SigE appear first in forespore/mother cell respectively；later SigG and SigK act in later stages.

一定要会写的流程 / Short workflowNutrient deprivation -> Spo0A activation -> asymmetric septation -> engulfment -> cortex synthesis -> coat synthesis -> mother-cell lysis -> spore release

Germination

• Dormant spores sense favorable conditions through germinant receptors.

• 典型过程：ion release -> Ca-DPA release -> activation of cortex-lytic enzymes -> cortex hydrolysis -> core rehydration -> metabolism resumes -> vegetative cell outgrowth。
  • A• For
  • Bsubtilis, L-alanine is a classic germinant；for
  • Cdifficile, taurocholate and glycine are important germinants.

Clinical examples of major spore-forming pathogens

• Bacillus anthracis -> anthrax; spores persist in soil for decades.

• Bacillus cereus -> food poisoning, especially associated with contaminated food such as rice.

• Clostridium botulinum -> botulism; spores survive pasteurization and can germinate in anaerobically stored foods.

• Clostridium perfringens -> diarrheal toxic infection and gas gangrene.

• Clostridium tetani -> tetanus; wound contamination by spores.

• Clostridioides difficile -> pseudomembranous colitis after gut dysbiosis.

Why spores are resistant

• Low core water content protects against wet heat.

• SASP and DNA repair systems protect genome integrity.

• Inner membrane, coat and exosporium increase resistance to chemicals, lysozyme and disinfectants.

11. Bacterial toxins

毒素章节最容易出“分类 + 举例 + 机制”题。不要只背名字，要记“target + biochemical activity + clinical effect”。

先分两大类 / First big distinctionEndotoxin: cell-associated LPS released after bacterial disruption; active component = Lipid A.Exotoxin: protein toxin synthesized by bacteria and delivered to target cells.

Toxin genes can be located on chromosome, plasmids, bacteriophages or pathogenicity islands, which explains why strains of the same species may be toxigenic or non-toxigenic.

11.1 Pore-forming toxins (PFTs)

• PFTs are the largest class of bacterial toxins; they bind to membranes, oligomerize and form pores.

• The pore alters membrane permeability and allows ions or molecules to pass.

• Two structural groups：a-PFTs and b-PFTs.

• a-PFT examples
colicins, Cytolysin A (ClyA).
• b-PFT examples
S. aureus alpha-hemolysin, PVL, aerolysin-family toxins including C. perfringens epsilon-toxin.
最简答案 / Minimal answerPore-forming toxins bind membranes, oligomerize and create transmembrane pores.This causes membrane permeabilization, ion imbalance, cell injury and sometimes cell lysis.

11.2 Membrane-damaging sphingomyelinases and phospholipases

• These toxins are esterases that hydrolyze membrane lipids.

• Consequences include tissue destruction, plasma membrane disruption, nutrient release, inflammatory mediator production, and escape from phagosomes.

• Sphingomyelinases act on sphingomyelin; phospholipases act on phospholipids.

• Bacillus cereus SMase C contributes to early immune evasion.

• ExoU of Pseudomonas aeruginosa is a patatin-like phospholipase A2 delivered by T3SS.

• C. perfringens alpha-toxin (PLC/CPA) damages membranes and promotes severe inflammation and myonecrosis.

11.3 Toxins with intracellular targets

• General shared process：binding to receptor -> internalization -> translocation of catalytic domain -> intracellular enzymatic activity.

• Three common organizational families in the slides：ABRT, AB5, and AnB7 toxins.

ABRT toxins

• Botulinum toxins
prevent neurotransmitter release at the neuromuscular junction -> flaccid paralysis.
• Tetanus toxin
blocks inhibitory interneurons in the CNS -> spastic paralysis.

• Both are proteases targeting SNARE-related proteins such as SNAP25, VAMP or syntaxin depending on toxin type.

• Diphtheria toxin ADP-ribosylates elongation factor 2 (EF2), stopping protein synthesis.

• Large clostridial toxins such as C. difficile toxins A and B inactivate Rho-family GTPases and disrupt actin cytoskeleton.

AB5 toxins

• Cholera toxin binds GM1 ganglioside, reaches the ER, and ADP-ribosylates Gs alpha.

• This keeps adenylate cyclase active -> increases cAMP -> activates PKA -> stimulates CFTR chloride secretion -> watery diarrhea.

• Heat-labile toxin of E. coli is closely related to cholera toxin.

• Shiga/Shiga-like toxins and pertussis toxin also belong to AB5-type toxins in the course classification.

AnB7 toxins

• Anthrax toxin is tripartite：protective antigen (PA) + edema factor (EF) + lethal factor (LF).

• PA binds receptor and forms the pore; EF is an adenylate cyclase that increases cAMP (edema); LF is a zinc protease that cleaves MAPKK (lethality/apoptotic signaling).

• Some actin ADP-ribosylating binary toxins are also grouped here.

高频例子一页记法 / One-line recallBotulinum -> SNARE cleavage -> no ACh release -> flaccid paralysisTetanus -> inhibitory interneuron blockade -> spastic paralysisDiphtheria -> EF2 inactivation -> stop protein synthesisCholera -> Gs activation / cAMP increase -> chloride and water secretionAnthrax -> PA pore + EF edema + LF MAPKK cleavage

12. Last-minute memory list

下面这部分是最后 1-2 轮复习时直接看的。建议你把这些句子读到能脱口而出。

1
Gram-positive bacteria have a thick peptidoglycan layer and teichoic acids, but no outer membrane.
2
Gram-negative bacteria have an outer membrane containing LPS and a thin peptidoglycan layer in the periplasm.
3
Lipid A is the endotoxin-active component of LPS.
4
O-antigen is highly variable and is important for serotyping and immune interaction.
5
Peptidoglycan determines cell shape and protects against osmotic rupture.
6
Penicillins target PBPs; glycopeptides target D-Ala-D-Ala.
7
NOD1 recognizes iE-DAP; NOD2 recognizes muramyl dipeptide.
8
TLR4 signaling leads to NF-kB/AP-1 activation and inflammatory cytokine production.
9
Transformation is uptake of free DNA by competent bacteria.
10
Conjugation requires direct contact and often involves a pilus and conjugative plasmid.
11
Transduction is bacteriophage-mediated gene transfer.
12
An operon is a group of genes controlled by one promoter and expressed as one polycistronic mRNA.
13
Alternative sigma factors redirect RNA polymerase to different promoters under specific conditions.
14
Spo0A phosphorylation initiates sporulation.
15
Spore resistance depends on low water content, Ca-DPA, SASP proteins and protective layers.
16
Botulinum toxin causes flaccid paralysis; tetanus toxin causes spastic paralysis.
17
Diphtheria toxin blocks protein synthesis by modifying EF2.
18
Cholera toxin increases cAMP and causes watery diarrhea.
19
Anthrax toxin consists of PA, EF and LF.
20
Large clostridial toxins disrupt Rho-family GTPases and the actin cytoskeleton.

13. 常见短答题模板 / Short-answer templates

Describe the Gram-negative envelope.

• 最简答案
Inner membrane + periplasm with thin peptidoglycan + outer membrane with LPS. Mention Lipid A, core and O-antigen, and say the OM is a permeability barrier.

Explain why Lipid A is important.

• 最简答案
It is the endotoxin-active component of LPS, recognized by the CD14/MD-2/TLR4 system, and induces strong innate immune activation, fever and possibly sepsis.

What is peptidoglycan and why is it important?

• 最简答案
A glycan-peptide mesh that determines shape and protects against osmotic lysis; also a major antibiotic target and immune-stimulating structure.

Compare transformation, conjugation and transduction.

• 最简答案
Transformation = free DNA uptake; conjugation = direct cell-to-cell transfer often through a pilus/plasmid; transduction = phage-mediated transfer.

What is an operon?

• 最简答案
A set of genes controlled by one promoter and expressed as one polycistronic mRNA.

How do bacterial spores resist harsh conditions?

• 最简答案
Low core water, Ca-DPA, SASP proteins, inner membrane, cortex, coat and sometimes exosporium all contribute.

Explain the mechanism of cholera toxin.

• 最简答案
AB5 toxin binds GM1, A1 reaches cytosol, ADP-ribosylates Gs alpha, increases cAMP, activates PKA and CFTR, causing chloride and water secretion.

Differentiate botulinum and tetanus toxins.

• 最简答案
Both are neurotoxins that cleave SNARE-related targets, but botulinum blocks neurotransmitter release causing flaccid paralysis, whereas tetanus blocks inhibitory interneurons causing spastic paralysis.

03细菌学 4 小时冲刺

TU02 Bacteriology – 4-Hour Exam-Oriented Review

细菌学 4 小时冲刺版 | 从考试得分出发的 cheatlist + 时间安排 + 最简答案

先说结论：上一版细菌学 cheatlist 够你拿来紧急复习，但不算“完整课件版”。它更像一份通过考试用的骨架：主干概念、高频对比、代表菌、抗生素/耐药机制都抓到了；但如果老师特别喜欢冷门细菌、细枝末节生化特征或某些单独课件例子，那一版并没有做到“全覆盖”。这份新文档按 仅剩 4 小时 的现实情况重排，目标是：四小时后你能写出核心题，而不是看过很多页。

1. 4 小时怎么分配最划算

第 1 小时
Gram+ / Gram−、细胞壁与表面结构、LPS / endotoxin、capsule / pili / flagella。
第 2 小时
抗生素靶点、耐药机制、horizontal gene transfer。
第 3 小时
代表菌：Staphylococcus、Streptococcus、Enterococcus、E. coli、Salmonella、Shigella、Pseudomonas、Neisseria、Mycobacterium。
第 4 小时
毒素、spores、最简答案回忆、易错点、临考 1 页速背。

2. 先背最值钱的总论

2.1 Gram positive vs Gram negative

Gram positive
thick peptidoglycan, teichoic acids, no outer membrane.
Gram negative
thin peptidoglycan, outer membrane, LPS, periplasmic space.
考试最简答案
Gram-positive bacteria have a thick peptidoglycan layer and no outer membrane, whereas Gram-negative bacteria have a thin peptidoglycan layer plus an outer membrane containing LPS.
必记一句
Lipid A of LPS is the endotoxin.
思路
这块最适合出对比题、结构功能题、抗生素与免疫识别联动题。先会“厚/薄 peptidoglycan、outer membrane、有无 LPS”，再补后果。

2.2 Surface structures

Capsule
anti-phagocytosis, immune evasion, persistence.
Flagella
motility.
Pili / fimbriae
adhesion; sex pilus is linked to conjugation.
考试最简答案
Major bacterial surface structures contribute to colonization, motility and virulence.

2.3 Horizontal gene transfer

Transformation
uptake of naked DNA.
Conjugation
DNA transfer by cell-to-cell contact, often plasmid-mediated.
Transduction
phage-mediated DNA transfer.
考试最简答案
Horizontal gene transfer promotes bacterial adaptation and dissemination of antibiotic resistance genes.

3. 抗生素与耐药：高概率机制题

四大靶点
cell wall synthesis, protein synthesis, nucleic acid synthesis, folate metabolism.
四大耐药机制
enzymatic inactivation, target modification, efflux pumps, decreased permeability.
考试最简答案
Major mechanisms of antibiotic resistance are enzymatic inactivation, target modification, efflux and reduced permeability.
延伸一句
Gram-negative bacteria are often intrinsically less permeable because of the outer membrane.
最像会考的问法
main antibiotic targets / main resistance mechanisms / why Gram− are more resistant / compare intrinsic and acquired resistance.

4. 代表菌：只抓能写出来的标签

4.1 Gram-positive cocci

Staphylococcus aureus: clusters; skin/soft tissue infection, abscess, toxin-related disease.

Streptococcus pyogenes: pharyngitis, skin infection, invasive disease.

Streptococcus pneumoniae: pneumonia, meningitis, otitis/sinusitis.

Enterococcus
opportunistic / nosocomial, resistance problem.

4.2 Gram-negative rods

Escherichia coli
gut commensal but major opportunistic pathogen; especially UTI.
Salmonella
foodborne, invasive potential.
Shigella
dysentery-type intestinal infection.

Pseudomonas aeruginosa: opportunistic, hospital-acquired, biofilm, high resistance.

4.3 Gram-negative cocci

Neisseria meningitidis: meningitis / septicemia line.

Neisseria gonorrhoeae: sexually transmitted infection line.

4.4 Special cell envelope

Mycobacterium
acid-fast, mycolic acids, chronic/intracellular persistence image.

5. 毒素与 spores：短小但很会考

5.1 Endotoxin vs exotoxin

Endotoxin
Lipid A of LPS; structural part of Gram− bacteria; heat-stable.
Exotoxin
secreted protein; often highly potent and target-specific.
必记例子
cholera toxin, diphtheria toxin, tetanus toxin, botulinum toxin, anthrax toxin.
考试最简答案
Endotoxin is the Lipid A of LPS, whereas exotoxins are secreted proteins with specific targets.

5.2 Spores

Definition
dormant highly resistant survival form.
经典属
Bacillus, Clostridium.
考试最简答案
Bacterial spores are dormant resistant structures that allow survival under harsh environmental conditions.

6. 最容易出的题型与答题骨架

对比题
Gram+ vs Gram−; endotoxin vs exotoxin; transformation vs conjugation vs transduction.
机制题
antibiotic resistance; role of capsule/pili/flagella; horizontal gene transfer.
结构功能题
cell wall, LPS, capsule, toxins, spores.
举例题
name one bacterium associated with pneumonia / UTI / meningitis / STI / dysentery / nosocomial infection.
通用答法
Definition first → key structural/functional points → one mechanism → one example. 别只堆术语。

7. Common traps / 容易失分点

不要只背 Gram 染色颜色，不写结构差异和后果。

说 LPS 时别只写 “Gram− 有 LPS”，要补 “Lipid A is the endotoxin”。

说 capsule 时别停在“外面有一层”，要补 anti-phagocytosis / immune evasion。

说 resistance 时别只写 “mutation”，四大机制要能列出来。

HGT 三兄弟最容易混
transformation = naked DNA；conjugation = contact；transduction = phage。

代表菌不要背成百科全书，只要抓“最像它的感染标签”。

8. 4 小时结束前必须能默出的 12 句

1
Gram-positive bacteria have a thick peptidoglycan layer and no outer membrane.
2
Gram-negative bacteria have a thin peptidoglycan layer, an outer membrane and LPS.
3
Lipid A of LPS is the endotoxin.
4
Capsule helps bacteria evade phagocytosis.
5
Pili promote adhesion and conjugation-related transfer.
6
Major antibiotic targets are cell wall, protein synthesis, nucleic acids and folate metabolism.
7
Major resistance mechanisms are enzymatic inactivation, target modification, efflux and reduced permeability.
8
Transformation is uptake of naked DNA.
9
Conjugation is DNA transfer by direct cell-to-cell contact.
10
Transduction is bacteriophage-mediated gene transfer.
11
Exotoxins are secreted proteins; endotoxin is part of LPS.
12
Spores are dormant resistant structures that ensure survival.

9. 最后 20 分钟怎么收尾

不用再看新内容，只回忆大框架。

口头讲一遍
Gram+ / Gram−，resistance，HGT，toxins，spores。
快速过代表菌
S. aureus, S. pyogenes, S. pneumoniae, E. coli, Salmonella, Shigella, Pseudomonas, Neisseria, Mycobacterium。

如果你脑子空白，就只背第 8 部分那 12 句。

TU02 Bacteriology – 4-Hour Exam-Oriented

Supplement Pack

细菌学 4 小时冲刺补充装｜只补最值钱的代表菌、毒素和高频对比点｜配合主 cheatlist 使用

先说结论：这份补充装不是为了把整门细菌学补完整，而是为了把主 cheatlist 里最可能需要“再补一点才更稳”的部分补上。优先顺序：先背主 cheatlist，再看本补充装的前两部分；如果时间继续不够，只看最后的“最赚补充清单”。
代表菌补充装
最值得额外背的 14 个 / High-yield extra organisms

• Staphylococcus aureus：Gram+ cocci in clusters；skin/soft tissue infection，abscess，toxin-related disease；考试里很适合拿来举“脓肿、医院感染、毒素病”的例子。

• Streptococcus pyogenes：Gram+ cocci in chains；pharyngitis，skin infection，invasive disease；记住它常和咽炎、蜂窝织炎、毒素相关综合征挂钩。

• Streptococcus pneumoniae：Gram+ diplococcus；pneumonia，otitis，sinusitis，meningitis；是“呼吸道侵袭性感染”最稳的代表菌之一。

• Enterococcus faecalis / faecium：nosocomial，UTI，device-associated infection，resistance problem；用来举“机会致病菌 + 医院感染 + 耐药”很合适。

• Escherichia coli
gut commensal but opportunistic pathogen；UTI 最典型；也可引起 sepsis / meningitis（不同 pathotypes 背不动就不要细抠）。
• Salmonella
food-borne；invasive potential；如果题目问 enteric Gram− rods，可与 Shigella 对照着背。
• Shigella
dysentery image；low infectious dose；记住“肠道、侵袭、黏液血便印象”就够。

• Pseudomonas aeruginosa：opportunistic，hospital-acquired，biofilm，high intrinsic resistance；如果只能多背一个 Gram− rod，就优先它。

• Neisseria meningitidis：Gram− diplococcus；meningitis / septicemia line；一看到 Neisseria 至少分清 meningitidis 和 gonorrhoeae。

• Neisseria gonorrhoeae：sexually transmitted infection；和 meningitidis 的最大区分是“STI”这条线。

• Mycobacterium tuberculosis：acid-fast，mycolic acids，chronic / intracellular persistence image；如果题里碰到 acid-fast，就先想到它。

• Clostridium tetani：spore-forming anaerobic Gram+ rod；经典高频点是 tetanospasmin neurotoxin。

• Clostridium botulinum：spore-forming anaerobic Gram+ rod；botulinum toxin，flaccid paralysis。

• Clostridioides difficile：antibiotic-associated colitis，toxins，nosocomial context；如果老师爱联系抗生素相关并发症，这个很赚。

• Bacillus anthracis：spore-forming Gram+ rod；anthrax toxin；用来和 Clostridium 一起记“spore + toxin”。

2
高频对比题补充 / Extra comparisons that are easy points

• Staphylococcus vs Streptococcus：前者 clusters，后者 chains；考试里至少把“cluster vs chain”这个最粗标签记住。

• Neisseria meningitidis vs N. gonorrhoeae：一个偏 meningitis/septicemia，一个偏 STI。

• Salmonella vs Shigella：都可致肠道感染；前者更偏 food-borne / invasive potential，后者更偏 dysentery / low infectious dose。

• Bacillus vs Clostridium：都可形成 spores；Bacillus 偏 aerobic，Clostridium 偏 anaerobic，这个对比很适合出基础机制题。

• Exotoxin vs endotoxin：secreted protein with specific target vs Lipid A of LPS；这个一定能秒写。

• Intrinsic resistance vs acquired resistance：先天结构性不敏感 vs 后天获得耐药机制；如果题目问 Gram− 为什么常更难治，可以把 intrinsic resistance 写进去。

3
毒素补充装 / Toxin add-on
• Cholera toxin
经典 AB toxin 代表；一看到“increase cAMP / secretory diarrhea”可联想到它。
• Diphtheria toxin
经典 AB toxin 代表；如果题目只让举 exotoxin 例子，这个很稳。
• Tetanus toxin
spastic paralysis 印象。
• Botulinum toxin
flaccid paralysis 印象。
• Anthrax toxin
组合毒素代表；不一定要求讲深，但要眼熟。
• 考试最短写法
Exotoxins are secreted proteins with specific targets; classic examples are tetanus, botulinum, cholera, diphtheria and anthrax toxins.
4
如果老师偏临床，会最容易碰到的关联 / Clinical association quick lines

• Pneumonia / meningitis / otitis / sinusitis → Streptococcus pneumoniae

• Pharyngitis / skin infection → Streptococcus pyogenes

• Abscess / skin-soft tissue / toxin-related disease → Staphylococcus aureus

• UTI → Escherichia coli（最典型）

• Hospital-acquired / biofilm / resistant Gram− rod → Pseudomonas aeruginosa

• Meningitis / septicemia, Gram− diplococcus → Neisseria meningitidis

• STI, Gram− diplococcus → Neisseria gonorrhoeae

• Antibiotic-associated colitis → C. difficile

• Chronic acid-fast rod → Mycobacterium tuberculosis

5
Common traps / 容易失分的补充点

• 不要把“所有 Gram− 都是高度耐药菌”写死。更稳的说法是：Gram− bacteria often show lower permeability because of the outer membrane and may display important intrinsic resistance.

• 不要把 endotoxin 写成“any toxin from Gram− bacteria”。更准确：endotoxin = Lipid A of LPS。

• 写 exotoxin 例子时，至少带一个功能标签：tetanus = spastic paralysis；botulinum = flaccid paralysis；cholera = secretory diarrhea。

• 写 horizontal gene transfer 时，不要忘了 phage：transduction = phage-mediated gene transfer。

• 写 conjugation 时，别只写“DNA transfer”，最好补“direct cell-to-cell contact”。

• 写 capsule 时，不要只写“protective layer”；最好补 anti-phagocytosis / immune evasion。

• 写 spores 时，不要说是“reproduction form”；更稳是 dormant resistant survival form。

6
最赚的补充 10 句 / 10 extra lines worth memorizing

Staphylococcus aureus is a Gram-positive coccus in clusters associated with abscesses, skin infection and toxin-related disease.

Streptococcus pyogenes is a Gram-positive coccus in chains associated with pharyngitis, skin infection and invasive disease.

Streptococcus pneumoniae is a leading cause of pneumonia, otitis, sinusitis and meningitis.

Escherichia coli is the most typical cause of urinary tract infection.

Pseudomonas aeruginosa is an opportunistic hospital-acquired pathogen with biofilm formation and high intrinsic resistance.

Neisseria meningitidis is linked to meningitis and septicemia, whereas N. gonorrhoeae is linked to sexually transmitted infection.

Mycobacterium tuberculosis is an acid-fast bacterium rich in mycolic acids and associated with chronic infection.

Clostridium tetani and Clostridium botulinum are spore-forming anaerobic Gram-positive rods producing potent neurotoxins.

Clostridioides difficile is associated with antibiotic-related colitis and toxin-mediated disease.

Bacillus anthracis is a spore-forming Gram-positive rod associated with anthrax toxin.

7
最后 30 分钟怎么用这份补充装 / How to use in the last 30 minutes

• 先扫第 1 部分的代表菌标签，只求“看到名字能想到 2 个关键词”。

• 再扫第 2 部分的高频对比；这些最容易直接变成卷面答案。

• 最后只背第 6 部分的 10 句；如果时间崩了，这 10 句优先级最高。

Bacteriology supplement pack

04病毒学总重点

TU02 Virology – Printable Exam Master Review

病毒学打印版总重点｜中英对照｜按重要性标注｜一次打印到位

Goal / 目标
背这一份，优先覆盖最容易考、最容易失分、最容易被追问的病毒学内容；本版在前次病毒学整理基础上，专门加强 virion membrane / layer naming 和 virus classification answer banks。
Legend / 重要性说明：【★★★★★】必背 / Must know 【★★★★】高频 / High-yield 【★★★】次重点 / Useful extra
★★★★★ Part I. Must-know map / 第一部分：先把最值钱的东西背下来

Recommended order / 建议顺序：1) virion layer naming → 2) virus structure + transmission → 3) persistent infection → 4) influenza → 5) HIV → 6) HBV/HCV → 7) dengue/Zika/herpesviruses → 8) classification answer banks → 9) figure analysis template.

Exam reality / 考试现实：2021考 transmission / influenza / herpes / HIV phases / severe dengue / HBV replication；2022考 persistent infection / Zika / HCV / HIV variability；2024考 virus structure and transmission / HIV stages / negative-strand polymerase / influenza H-N / DENV figure analysis。

If you truly master the first five blue sections plus the final answer banks, you already cover the recurring spine of the uploaded virology exams. / 如果你把前五个蓝色大节和最后的答题银行背熟，已经覆盖上传往年卷反复出现的主干。

★★★★★ Part II. Absolute must-know virion layer naming drill / 第二部分：绝对必背——病毒从外到内分层与结构名称
★★★★★ 1. Universal outside → inside naming / 通用从外到内命名模板
Outside → inside / 从外到内：Spikes / glycoproteins (if present) → Envelope (if present) → Matrix or Tegument (if present) → Capsid / nucleocapsid → Genome ± polymerase / enzymes
EN
Not every virus has every layer; the safest wording is capsid ± envelope, then add matrix / tegument only when the virus truly has it. 中: 并不是所有病毒都有所有层。最稳妥的说法是先写 capsid ± envelope，再只在该病毒确实有时补 matrix 或 tegument。
EN
The nucleocapsid = genome + capsid. 中: nucleocapsid = genome + capsid（基因组 + 衣壳）。
EN
When present, the envelope is the structural element that most strongly changes transmission because it makes the virus fragile in the environment. 中: 若存在，envelope 是最影响传播方式的结构，因为它使病毒在环境中更脆弱。
★★★★★ 2. Influenza outside → inside / 流感病毒从外到内
Outside → inside / 从外到内：HA + NA + M2 → Envelope → Matrix M1 → Helical RNP / nucleocapsid → Segmented ssRNA(-) + polymerase PA / PB1 / PB2
EN
Exam-safe sentence: Influenza is an enveloped segmented negative-sense ssRNA virus with a helical nucleocapsid. 中: 考场稳句：Influenza 是有包膜、分节段、负链单股 RNA 病毒，具有螺旋核衣壳。
EN
Hemagglutinin is on the envelope, not inside the capsid. 中: HA 位于包膜表面，不在 capsid 里面。
★★★★★ 3. HIV outside → inside / HIV 从外到内
Outside → inside / 从外到内：gp120 / gp41 spikes → Envelope → Matrix p17 → Cone-shaped capsid p24 → Nucleocapsid p7 → Diploid RNA + RT + integrase + protease
EN
Exam-safe sentence: HIV is an enveloped retrovirus with a diploid RNA genome and a cone-shaped capsid. 中: 考场稳句：HIV 是有包膜逆转录病毒，具有双拷贝 RNA 基因组和锥形 capsid。
EN
Do not say HIV enters as DNA; the infectious particle contains RNA. 中: 不要写 HIV 以 DNA 形式进入；infectious particle 中装的是 RNA。
★★★★★ 4. Herpesvirus outside → inside / 疱疹病毒从外到内
Outside → inside / 从外到内：Glycoprotein spikes → Envelope → Tegument → Icosahedral capsid → Linear dsDNA
EN
Tegument is the protein layer between capsid and envelope. 中: Tegument 是位于 capsid 与 envelope 之间的蛋白层。
EN
If the examiner gives a herpes virion picture, the easiest labels are envelope, tegument, icosahedral capsid, linear dsDNA genome. 中: 如果考官给疱疹病毒结构图，最稳的标签就是 envelope、tegument、icosahedral capsid、linear dsDNA genome。
★★★★ 5. Poxvirus complex structure / 痘病毒复杂结构（低一档但值得认）
Outside → inside / 从外到内：Complex envelope / outer membrane → Surface tubules → Lateral bodies → Inner membrane / palisade layer → Biconcave core with nucleoprotein
EN
Poxviruses are large brick-shaped viruses with a complex internal structure, not a simple classic capsid picture. 中: 痘病毒是大型砖块状病毒，内部结构复杂，不是简单的经典衣壳图。
★★★★★ 6. Helical vs icosahedral / 螺旋 vs 二十面体
• EN
Animal viruses with helical capsids are always enveloped. 中: 动物病毒中，helical capsid 一定有包膜。
• EN
Icosahedral viruses may be naked or enveloped. 中: Icosahedral 病毒可以无包膜，也可以有包膜。
• EN
Helical examples: influenza, rabies, RSV, measles. Icosahedral examples: adenovirus, papillomavirus, herpesvirus. 中: Helical 例子：influenza、rabies、RSV、measles；Icosahedral 例子：adenovirus、papillomavirus、herpesvirus。
★★★★★ Part III. Core synthesis topics / 第三部分：综合题主干
★★★★★ 1. Virus structure and modes of transmission / 病毒结构与传播方式
Minimal scoring answer / 最得分答案：A virus is an obligate intracellular parasite containing either DNA or RNA, surrounded by a capsid and sometimes by an envelope. The capsid protects the genome and may participate in attachment. The envelope carries glycoproteins involved in attachment and fusion, but because it is fragile in the environment, enveloped viruses are usually transmitted by close contact, droplets, blood, sexual or vertical routes, whereas naked viruses are more resistant and are often transmitted indirectly or by the fecal–oral route. / 病毒是专性细胞内寄生物，只含一种核酸（DNA 或 RNA），外有衣壳，有些还有包膜。衣壳保护基因组并可参与附着；包膜上的糖蛋白参与结合和融合，但包膜在环境中较脆弱，因此包膜病毒更常经近距离接触、飞沫、血液、性传播或母婴传播，而无包膜病毒更耐环境，更常见于间接接触或粪口传播。

1) EN: Start with genome + capsid ± envelope. 中: 先从 genome + capsid ± envelope 开始。

2) EN: Name the function of each structure, not only the names. 中: 写出每个结构的功能，不要只罗列名称。

3) EN: Link envelope fragility to epidemiology. 中: 把 envelope 的脆弱性直接连到流行病学后果。

4) EN: If asked about entry points, always give body site + virus example. 中: 若问 entry points，一定写“进入部位 + 病毒例子”。

Safe examples / 稳定例子：Respiratory = influenza / RSV / SARS-CoV-2; fecal–oral = HAV / HEV / rotavirus; blood = HIV / HBV / HCV; sexual = HIV / HSV-2; vertical = HIV / HBV / CMV / HSV / rubella; vector-borne = dengue / Zika / yellow fever.

• EN
Common trap: never say one virion contains both DNA and RNA as its genome. 中: 常见陷阱：绝不要说同一个 virion 同时含 DNA 和 RNA 作为基因组。
• EN
Common trap: do not stop at “enveloped viruses are fragile”; continue with “therefore they are usually transmitted by ...”. 中: 常见陷阱：不要只写“包膜病毒脆弱”，要继续写“因此它们通常通过……传播”。
★★★★★ 2. Persistent infection / 持续性感染
Minimal scoring answer / 最得分答案：Persistent infection is a long-term infection in which the viral genome is maintained in the host because the virus is not cleared by the immune system. The two main categories are latent infection and chronic infection. / 持续性感染是指病毒基因组在宿主体内长期保留，因为免疫系统没有将其清除。两大类型是 latent infection（潜伏感染）和 chronic infection（慢性感染）。
• EN
Latent infection: the viral genome persists with no or minimal production of infectious particles; reactivation can later restore productive replication. 中: 潜伏感染：病毒基因组持续存在，但感染性颗粒几乎不产生或极少；之后可再激活。
• EN
Chronic infection: continuous or repeated production of virus over long periods, with long-term transmission and inflammation. 中: 慢性感染：长期持续或反复产生病毒，伴长期传播和炎症。
• EN
Classic latent examples: HSV / VZV in sensory neurons, HCMV in myeloid cells, EBV in memory B cells. 中: 经典潜伏例子：HSV / VZV 在感觉神经元；HCMV 在髓系细胞；EBV 在 memory B cells。
• EN
Classic chronic examples: HBV, HCV, HIV. 中: 经典慢性感染例子：HBV、HCV、HIV。

Viral factors to mention / 必提病毒因素：latency, sanctuary sites, interference with antigen presentation, antigenic variation, replication in immune cells, low cytopathic effect.

Host factors to mention / 必提宿主因素：immunodeficiency, extreme ages, pregnancy, comorbidities, genetic factors.

• EN
Common trap: latency is not eradication; the genome remains. 中: 常见陷阱：latency 不是被清除；基因组仍然存在。
• EN
If you cite HIV, the safer wording is chronic infection with reservoirs, not “purely latent”. 中: 如果写 HIV，更稳的表达是“带有 reservoirs 的慢性感染”，而不是“纯潜伏”。
★★★★★ Part IV. High-yield short-answer bank / 第四部分：高频简答题答案银行
★★★★★ 1. Influenza / 流感病毒
• EN
Family: Orthomyxoviridae. Enveloped, helical nucleocapsid, segmented ssRNA(-). Influenza A and B usually have 8 segments. 中: 家族：Orthomyxoviridae。有包膜、螺旋核衣壳、分节段负链单股 RNA。Influenza A 和 B 通常有 8 个节段。
• EN
Envelope proteins to remember: HA, NA and M2; matrix protein M1 lies under the envelope. 中: 要记住的包膜相关蛋白：HA、NA、M2；包膜下是 M1 matrix。
• EN
HA has three exam-safe roles: receptor binding to sialic acid, membrane fusion in endosomes, and major immunogenicity / target of neutralizing antibodies. 中: HA 三个稳得分作用：与唾液酸受体结合、在内体中介导膜融合、是主要免疫原和中和抗体靶点。
• EN
H = hemagglutinin; N = neuraminidase. Human subtypes commonly cited: H1N1 and H3N2. 中: H = hemagglutinin；N = neuraminidase。人群中最常写的亚型：H1N1 和 H3N2。
• EN
Antigenic drift = point mutations, mainly in HA; antigenic shift = reassortment and occurs only in influenza A. 中: Antigenic drift = 点突变，主要在 HA；antigenic shift = reassortment，只发生在 influenza A。
• EN
Because influenza is a negative-strand RNA virus, its genome is not directly translatable and the virion must carry its own RNA-dependent RNA polymerase. 中: 因为流感是负链 RNA 病毒，其基因组不能直接翻译，所以 virion 必须自带 RNA-dependent RNA polymerase。
Treatment / 治疗
supportive care in mild disease; neuraminidase inhibitor such as oseltamivir is the classic named antiviral; prevention = seasonal vaccine.

One-line answer / 一句话背诵：Influenza is an enveloped segmented ssRNA(-) virus with HA and NA on the envelope; HA binds sialic acid, mediates fusion, and is the main neutralizing-antibody target.

★★★★★ 2. HIV / 人类免疫缺陷病毒
• EN
Family Retroviridae, genus Lentivirus. Enveloped retrovirus with a diploid RNA genome, matrix p17, cone-shaped capsid p24, RT, integrase and protease. 中: Retroviridae 家族，Lentivirus 属。有包膜逆转录病毒，具有双拷贝 RNA、matrix p17、锥形 capsid p24、RT、integrase 和 protease。
• EN
Target cells: CD4 T lymphocytes mainly; also monocytes/macrophages, dendritic-lineage cells and microglia. 中: 靶细胞：主要是 CD4 T 淋巴细胞；还包括单核/巨噬细胞、树突状细胞谱系和 microglia。
• EN
Receptor and coreceptors: CD4 first, then CCR5 or CXCR4. 中: 受体和共受体：先 CD4，再 CCR5 或 CXCR4。
• EN
Transmission: sexual, blood-borne, and mother-to-child including pregnancy, delivery and breastfeeding. 中: 传播：性传播、血液传播、母婴传播，包括妊娠、分娩和哺乳。
• EN
Natural history: primary infection → clinical latency → AIDS. 中: 自然病程：初感染 → 临床潜伏期 → AIDS。
• EN
Marker evolution: viral load very high in primary infection, lower at set point during clinical latency, then rises again in AIDS; CD4 falls early, partially recovers, then progressively declines and becomes very low in AIDS. 中: 指标变化：初感染时 viral load 很高，临床潜伏期降至 set point，AIDS 再上升；CD4 先下降、部分回升，然后持续下降，AIDS 时很低。
• EN
Why HIV is highly variable: low fidelity RT / no proofreading, huge replication dynamics, and recombination during reverse transcription. 中: HIV 高变异原因：RT 低保真且无校对、复制量极大、逆转录时发生重组。
Treatment / 治疗
combination antiretroviral therapy (ART). Key exam words = reverse transcriptase inhibitors, integrase inhibitors, protease inhibitors.
Reservoirs / 储存库
lymph nodes, GALT, blood, CNS and other tissues; persistence continues despite therapy.
★★★★★ 3. HBV and HCV / HBV 与 HCV

HBV / 乙肝病毒

• EN
Family Hepadnaviridae. Enveloped virus with an icosahedral capsid and a partially double-stranded circular DNA genome of about 3.2 kb. 中: Hepadnaviridae 家族。有包膜、二十面体 capsid，基因组为约 3.2 kb 的部分双链环状 DNA。
• EN
HBV receptor: NTCP, coherent with hepatic tropism. 中: HBV 受体：NTCP，与肝嗜性一致。
• EN
Key replication logic: rcDNA enters the nucleus → becomes cccDNA → cccDNA transcribes viral RNAs including pgRNA → pgRNA is packaged with polymerase → reverse transcription makes the negative DNA strand → RNase H degrades pgRNA → an RNA oligo primes incomplete positive-strand DNA synthesis. 中: 关键复制逻辑：rcDNA 入核 → 变为 cccDNA → cccDNA 转录产生包括 pgRNA 在内的病毒 RNA → pgRNA 与 polymerase 被包装 → 逆转录生成负链 DNA → RNase H 降解 pgRNA → 残余 RNA 片段引发不完整正链 DNA 合成。
• EN
cccDNA persists in hepatocyte nuclei and explains persistence / reactivation. 中: cccDNA 在肝细胞核内持续存在，是持续感染和再激活的重要原因。
• EN
Useful markers: HBsAg, anti-HBs, anti-HBc IgM, HBeAg, anti-HBe, HBV DNA. 中: 有用标志物：HBsAg、anti-HBs、anti-HBc IgM、HBeAg、anti-HBe、HBV DNA。
Treatment / 治疗
tenofovir or entecavir are the classic named agents; pegylated interferon may also be cited.

Most likely trap / 最易失分：never write HBV as a “standard dsDNA virus” without saying partially double-stranded and reverse transcription from pgRNA.

HCV / 丙肝病毒

• EN
Family Flaviviridae, genus Hepacivirus. Enveloped ssRNA(+) virus of about 9.6 kb. 中: Flaviviridae 家族，Hepacivirus 属。有包膜正链单股 RNA，约 9.6 kb。
• EN
Its genome is translated as one polyprotein, then cleaved by host and viral proteases into structural and non-structural proteins. 中: 其基因组先翻译成一个 polyprotein，再由宿主和病毒蛋白酶切割成结构蛋白和非结构蛋白。
• EN
Blood-borne transmission and frequent chronicity are the two simplest exam tags. 中: 血源传播和高慢性化倾向，是最简洁的两个考试标签。
• EN
Genotypes and quasispecies explain diversity and treatment relevance. 中: Genotypes 和 quasispecies 解释了其多样性和治疗相关性。
Treatment / 治疗
direct-acting antivirals (DAAs). Safe wording = curative all-oral DAA combinations.
★★★★★ 4. Dengue, Zika, Yellow fever / 登革热、寨卡、黄热

Dengue / 登革热

• EN
Vector: Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus. 中: 媒介：Aedes aegypti 和 Aedes albopictus。
• EN
4 serotypes: DENV-1 to DENV-4. 中: 4 个血清型：DENV-1 到 DENV-4。
• EN
Classic dengue: high fever, severe headache, retro-orbital pain, myalgia / arthralgia, nausea, vomiting, rash. 中: 经典登革：高热、剧烈头痛、眼后痛、肌痛/关节痛、恶心、呕吐、皮疹。
• EN
Severe dengue is characterized by three pillars: plasma leakage, bleeding and organ impairment. 中: 重症登革热的三个核心柱子：plasma leakage、bleeding、organ impairment。
• EN
Risk is higher during secondary infection with another serotype because of ADE. 中: 再次感染异型血清型时，因 ADE 重症风险更高。
Treatment / 治疗
no specific antiviral in standard teaching; supportive care, fluid management, monitoring for shock/bleeding.

Most likely trap / 最易失分：do not define severe dengue with symptoms only; write the three defining pillars.

Zika / 寨卡

• EN
Transmission: mosquito bites, sexual transmission, blood transfusion, vertical transmission. 中: 传播：蚊虫叮咬、性传播、输血、母婴传播。
• EN
Most infections are asymptomatic or mild. 中: 多数感染无症状或较轻。
• EN
Major complications: Guillain–Barré syndrome and congenital Zika syndrome. 中: 主要并发症：Guillain–Barré syndrome 和 congenital Zika syndrome。
Treatment / 治疗
supportive care; no classic specific antiviral to name in this course context.

Yellow fever / 黄热（实用补面）

• EN
Orthoflavivirus with 1 serotype; vector mainly Aedes aegypti. 中: Orthoflavivirus，1 个血清型；媒介主要是 Aedes aegypti。
• EN
Severe forms develop after a remission period and involve hemorrhagic disease plus liver and kidney injury. 中: 重症常在短暂缓解后出现，表现为出血性疾病并累及肝和肾。
• EN
50% mortality can be cited for severe forms. 中: 重症可写 50% 死亡率。
Treatment / 治疗
supportive care; prevention = vaccination.
★★★★★ 5. Herpesviruses / 疱疹病毒
• EN
Common structure: linear dsDNA, icosahedral capsid, tegument, envelope with glycoproteins. 中: 共同结构：线性 dsDNA、二十面体 capsid、tegument、带糖蛋白的 envelope。
• EN
Their hallmark is lifelong latency followed by reactivation. 中: 它们的标志特点是终身潜伏和可再激活。
• EN
Latency sites to memorize: HSV / VZV in sensory neurons, HCMV in myeloid cells, EBV in memory B cells. 中: 要记的潜伏部位：HSV / VZV 在感觉神经元；HCMV 在髓系细胞；EBV 在记忆 B 细胞。

Ready-made three-virus answer set / 三病毒现成答案组：

• EN
HSV-1 (Herpes simplex virus type 1) → oral herpes / gingivostomatitis / keratitis / encephalitis. 中: HSV-1（单纯疱疹病毒 1 型）→ 口唇疱疹 / 牙龈口炎 / 角膜炎 / 脑炎。
• EN
HSV-2 (Herpes simplex virus type 2) → genital herpes / neonatal herpes. 中: HSV-2（单纯疱疹病毒 2 型）→ 生殖器疱疹 / 新生儿疱疹。
• EN
VZV (Varicella-zoster virus) → chickenpox in primary infection, shingles in reactivation. 中: VZV（水痘-带状疱疹病毒）→ 初次感染为水痘，再激活为带状疱疹。

Useful extras / 可加分补充：HCMV = congenital infection / retinitis in immunocompromised patients; EBV = infectious mononucleosis / Burkitt lymphoma / nasopharyngeal carcinoma.

Treatment / 治疗
HSV / VZV → acyclovir or valacyclovir; HCMV → ganciclovir / valganciclovir; EBV usually supportive (no routine specific anti-EBV treatment).
• EN
Common trap: herpesvirus latency is episomal persistence, not integration into the host genome. 中: 常见陷阱：疱疹病毒的潜伏是 episome 形式持续存在，不是整合入宿主基因组。
• EN
Common trap: do not say HSV-1 is exclusively oral and HSV-2 exclusively genital. 中: 常见陷阱：不要把 HSV-1 说成只能口部、HSV-2 说成只能生殖器。
★★★★ 6. RSV and respiratory extras / RSV 与呼吸道补充
• EN
RSV belongs to Pneumoviridae, has a negative-sense ssRNA genome, and is the most common cause of hospitalization for acute lower respiratory infection in children under 2 years of age. 中: RSV 属于 Pneumoviridae，基因组为负链 ssRNA，是 2 岁以下儿童急性下呼吸道感染住院最常见原因。
• EN
Key structure names: nucleocapsid with N/P/L, matrix, and envelope proteins F, G and SH. 中: 结构关键词：含 N/P/L 的 nucleocapsid、matrix，以及包膜蛋白 F、G、SH。
• EN
Bronchiolitis in infants is the classic disease tag. 中: infants bronchiolitis 是最经典疾病标签。
• EN
Transmission is mainly respiratory; persistence on hands and objects for a few hours matters in epidemiology. 中: 主要经呼吸道传播；在手和物体表面可短时间存活，这在流行病学上有意义。
Treatment / 治疗
mostly supportive care; prevention in infants at risk may be cited with monoclonal antibodies such as nirsevimab / palivizumab.
★★★★★ Part V. Virus classification answer banks / 第五部分：病毒分类答题银行（专门加强）
★★★★★ 1. Baltimore classification quick map / Baltimore 分类速记图
Group I dsDNA → HSV-1 / VZV / HCMV / EBVGroup II ssDNA → low-yield in this TU02 courseGroup III dsRNA → RotavirusGroup IV ssRNA(+) → HAV / HCV / Dengue / Zika / Yellow fever / SARS-CoV-2 / ChikungunyaGroup V ssRNA(-) → Influenza / RSV / Rabies / Ebola / MeaslesGroup VI RNA-RT → HIVGroup VII DNA-RT → HBV
EN
If the examiner asks for genome-based classification, Group I / IV / V / VI / VII are the most profitable groups to cite in this course. 中: 如果考官按基因组分类来问，本课里最值得写的是 I / IV / V / VI / VII 组。
★★★★★ 2. Ready-made three-example bank: enveloped dsDNA / 现成三例：有包膜 dsDNA
• EN
HSV-1 → oral herpes / gingivostomatitis / keratitis / encephalitis → acyclovir or valacyclovir. 中: HSV-1 → 口唇疱疹 / 牙龈口炎 / 角膜炎 / 脑炎 → acyclovir 或 valacyclovir。
• EN
VZV → chickenpox / shingles → acyclovir or valacyclovir. 中: VZV → 水痘 / 带状疱疹 → acyclovir 或 valacyclovir。
• EN
HCMV → congenital infection / retinitis / pneumonitis in immunocompromised patients → ganciclovir or valganciclovir. 中: HCMV → 先天感染 / 视网膜炎 / 免疫抑制者肺炎 → ganciclovir 或 valganciclovir。
Safe label / 稳定标签
linear dsDNA + icosahedral capsid + tegument + envelope + latency.
★★★★★ 3. Ready-made three-example bank: enveloped ssRNA(+) / 现成三例：有包膜正链 RNA
• EN
HCV → chronic hepatitis / cirrhosis / hepatocellular carcinoma → direct-acting antivirals. 中: HCV → 慢性肝炎 / 肝硬化 / 肝癌 → 直接抗病毒药物 DAA。
• EN
Dengue virus → dengue / severe dengue → supportive care, no classic specific antiviral. 中: Dengue virus → 登革热 / 重症登革热 → 支持治疗，无经典特异抗病毒药。
• EN
Zika virus → mild Zika disease, congenital Zika syndrome, Guillain–Barré syndrome → supportive care. 中: Zika virus → 轻症寨卡、先天性寨卡综合征、格林-巴利综合征 → 支持治疗。

Useful substitutes / 可替换例子：HAV is ssRNA(+) but naked; Yellow fever is also a good flavivirus example with supportive care + vaccination.

★★★★★ 4. Ready-made three-example bank: ssRNA(-) respiratory / 现成三例：负链 RNA 呼吸道组
• EN
Influenza virus → seasonal flu / viral pneumopathy / ARDS → supportive care ± oseltamivir. 中: Influenza virus → 季节性流感 / 病毒性肺炎 / ARDS → 支持治疗 ± oseltamivir。
• EN
RSV → bronchiolitis in infants → supportive care; prevention at risk with nirsevimab / palivizumab. 中: RSV → 婴幼儿细支气管炎 → 支持治疗；高危婴儿可预防性用 nirsevimab / palivizumab。
• EN
Rabies virus → rabies encephalitis → post-exposure prophylaxis with vaccine + immunoglobulins before disease onset. 中: Rabies virus → 狂犬病脑炎 → 发病前暴露后预防：疫苗 + 免疫球蛋白。
Safe label / 稳定标签
helical nucleocapsid, enveloped, negative-sense RNA, polymerase carried in the virion.
★★★★★ 5. Ready-made three-example bank: hepatitis viruses / 现成三例：肝炎病毒
• EN
HAV → acute hepatitis A → supportive care; vaccine prevention. 中: HAV → 甲型肝炎 → 支持治疗；疫苗预防。
• EN
HBV → acute and chronic hepatitis B → tenofovir / entecavir ± peg-IFN. 中: HBV → 急慢性乙肝 → tenofovir / entecavir ± 聚乙二醇干扰素。
• EN
HCV → chronic hepatitis C → direct-acting antivirals. 中: HCV → 丙型肝炎慢性感染 → 直接抗病毒药。

If they insist on transmission in the same answer / 若答案还要写传播：HAV = fecal–oral; HBV = blood / sex / vertical; HCV = blood-borne.

★★★★ 6. If asked “three herpesviruses + disease + treatment” / 如果问“三种疱疹病毒 + 疾病 + 治疗”
HSV-1 → oral herpes / keratitis / encephalitis → acyclovir or valacyclovir; HSV-2 → genital herpes / neonatal herpes → acyclovir; VZV → varicella / zoster → acyclovir or valacyclovir. If allowed, add HCMV → congenital infection / retinitis → ganciclovir.
★★★★★ Part VI. Figure analysis template / 第六部分：实验图分析模板

This is not optional. A figure-analysis question appears in all uploaded virology exams. / 这不是可选项。上传的三套病毒学试卷都有读图题。

★★★★★ Fixed answer order / 固定答题顺序

1) EN: State the experimental settings: cell line, condition, virus, MOI, time post-infection, and technique. 中: 先写实验设置：细胞系、处理条件、病毒、MOI、感染后时间、技术。

2) EN: State exactly what is measured. 中: 明确写“测量的是什么”。

3) EN: Comment on controls. 中: 一定评论 controls。

4) EN: Describe the result in the direction of change. 中: 描述结果变化方向。

5) EN: Give a conclusion: the host factor is proviral or antiviral. 中: 最后下结论：该宿主因子是 proviral 还是 antiviral。

★★★★★ Techniques to memorize / 必背技术与读数
• EN
RT-qPCR = viral RNA levels. 中: RT-qPCR = 病毒 RNA 水平。
• EN
Western blot / immunoblot = protein expression or KO / rescue efficiency. 中: Western blot / immunoblot = 蛋白表达或 KO / rescue 效率。
• EN
Flow cytometry after staining with anti-envelope protein antibody = percentage of infected cells. 中: 用抗 envelope protein 抗体染色后的 flow cytometry = 感染细胞百分比。
• EN
anti-dsRNA staining = viral replication intermediates / replication foci. 中: anti-dsRNA 染色 = 病毒复制中间体 / 复制灶。
• EN
DAPI = nuclei staining for visualization / counting / normalization. 中: DAPI = 细胞核染色，用于观察、计数或归一化。
★★★★★ Recurrent technical words / 高频技术词
• EN
MOI = multiplicity of infection. 中: MOI = multiplicity of infection。
• EN
siRNA = gene knockdown. 中: siRNA = 基因敲低。
• EN
siNT = non-targeting negative control. 中: siNT = 非靶向阴性对照。
• EN
KO = gene knockout. 中: KO = 基因敲除。
• EN
Trans-complementation / rescue = re-expression of the missing gene to prove specificity of the phenotype. 中: Trans-complementation / rescue = 把缺失基因补回去，以证明表型的特异性。
★★★★★ General conclusion rule / 通用结论规则
• EN
If loss of the host factor decreases infection or replication, the factor is proviral / a host dependency factor. 中: 如果去掉该宿主因子后感染或复制下降，则该因子是 proviral / host dependency factor。
• EN
If loss of the host factor increases infection, the factor is antiviral. 中: 如果去掉该宿主因子后感染上升，则该因子是 antiviral。

Most likely trap / 最易失分：do not only say “panel B is lower”; write what the y-axis measures, how it was measured, which control it is compared with, and what statistical summary is displayed (mean ± SD, n, p value).

★★★★★ Part VII. Last-page hard-memorization sheet / 第七部分：最后一页硬背版
★★★★★ 12 lines worth forcing into memory / 最值得硬塞进脑子的 12 句
• EN
A virus contains either DNA or RNA, never both as its genome. 中: 病毒的基因组只能是 DNA 或 RNA 之一，不会两者同时作为基因组。
• EN
The structural element that most changes transmission is the envelope. 中: 最影响传播方式的结构是 envelope。
• EN
Animal viruses with helical capsids are always enveloped. 中: 动物病毒中，helical capsid 一定有包膜。
• EN
Influenza is an enveloped segmented ssRNA(-) virus with HA and NA on its envelope. 中: Influenza 是有包膜、分节段、负链 ssRNA 病毒，包膜上有 HA 和 NA。
• EN
Hemagglutinin binds sialic acid, mediates fusion, and is a major neutralizing-antibody target. 中: HA 结合唾液酸、介导融合，并且是主要中和抗体靶点。
• EN
HIV uses CD4 plus CCR5 or CXCR4 and is highly variable because of no proofreading, huge replication and recombination. 中: HIV 使用 CD4 加 CCR5 或 CXCR4；其高变异来自无校对、超大量复制和重组。
• EN
HBV is a partially double-stranded circular DNA virus that forms cccDNA and reverse-transcribes pgRNA. 中: HBV 是部分双链环状 DNA 病毒，可形成 cccDNA，并从 pgRNA 逆转录。
• EN
HCV is an enveloped ssRNA(+) virus translated as one polyprotein. 中: HCV 是有包膜正链 RNA 病毒，先翻译成一个 polyprotein。
• EN
Severe dengue = plasma leakage + bleeding + organ impairment. 中: 重症登革热 = plasma leakage + bleeding + organ impairment。
• EN
Zika transmission = mosquito + sexual + blood + vertical; complications = GBS + congenital Zika syndrome. 中: 寨卡传播 = 蚊媒 + 性传播 + 血液 + 母婴；并发症 = GBS + 先天性寨卡综合征。
• EN
Herpesviruses are linear dsDNA viruses with an envelope, an icosahedral capsid and a tegument. 中: 疱疹病毒是线性 dsDNA 病毒，具有 envelope、icosahedral capsid 和 tegument。
• EN
In figure analysis: setup → measure → controls → result → proviral or antiviral. 中: 读图题固定顺序：setup → measure → controls → result → proviral or antiviral。
★★★★ Quick safe treatments to cite / 可快速写出的治疗关键词

Influenza = oseltamivir / supportive; HIV = combination ART; HBV = tenofovir or entecavir; HCV = DAAs; HSV/VZV = acyclovir / valacyclovir; HCMV = ganciclovir; Dengue/Zika/Yellow fever = supportive care; Rabies = post-exposure prophylaxis before symptoms.

Final advice / 最后提醒：If time is short, do not keep rereading. Close the pages and orally reconstruct the outside→inside naming, the 2021–2024 recurring questions, and the treatment words. / 如果时间很紧，不要继续机械重读。合上资料，口头重建 outside→inside 命名、2021–2024 的反复题点，以及治疗关键词。

TU02 Infectiology – Virology Review Pack

病毒学高频重点整理（中英对照 · 以通过考试为目标）

Based on uploaded TU02 course slides and past virology exams (2020, 2022, 2024).

Designed for rapid exam preparation, print-friendly layout, and bilingual memorization.

How to use this pack / 使用方法： The first half contains the most exam-relevant topics that have already reappeared in past papers. Learn these first. The second half adds lower-yield but still useful topics so you can keep a broader overview. Each core chapter contains a minimal scoring answer, a concise explanation, and a logic path for answering the question in English. 前半部分先背，高频且已被往年卷反复覆盖；后半部分用于补面和防止考场发懵。
