Last pitfalls｜最后陷阱

Do not confuse vector with intermediate host definitions: a biological vector is an active intermediate host.

Do not say humans are the definitive host for Plasmodium.

For sexual transmission, the cleanest answer is Trichomonas vaginalis.

For human final host, the cleanest answer is Taenia saginata.

10
次重点扫一眼｜Secondary glance-only topics

这一部分不要求你像前面那样硬背到能默写，但很适合考前扫一遍：一旦老师出得稍微偏一点，你至少知道题目在说什么，也知道怎么往标准答案上靠。

10.1 Forms of parasitism and host specificity｜寄生类型与宿主特异性

Accidental parasitism 偶然寄生：parasite enters an unusual situation by chance. Example: fly larva in a wound.

Optional parasitism 兼性寄生：normally free-living or saprophytic organisms can become parasitic under some conditions. Fungi are the classic example.

Obligatory parasitism 专性寄生：the parasite must spend part or all of its life as a parasite. Temporary examples include Fasciola and Strongyloides; permanent examples include filariae and Plasmodium.

Erratic parasitism 异位寄生：the parasite is found in an abnormal host or abnormal site. Toxocara canis in humans is the classic dead-end / larva migrans example.

Stenoxenic parasites 狭宿主寄生虫：high host fidelity. Euryxenic parasites 广宿主寄生虫：low host fidelity, therefore usually harder to control in the field.

10.2 Resistant / infective forms worth recognizing｜需要眼熟的抗性/感染形式

Quiescent eggs 静止卵：not infectious when excreted; they need maturation outside the host. Example: Ascaris lumbricoides.

Embryonated eggs 胚胎化虫卵：infectious as soon as they are eliminated or after a very short delay. Example: Enterobius vermicularis.

Cysts 包囊：typical resistant form of protists. High-yield examples: Entamoeba histolytica and Giardia intestinalis.

Oocysts 卵囊：important for Toxoplasma gondii and Cryptosporidium parvum. Do not mix them up with simple cysts or eggs.

Metacercariae 囊蚴：encysted larval trematode stage, usually acquired by ingestion of contaminated aquatic plants or intermediate hosts.

Tachyzoites / bradyzoites 速殖子 / 缓殖子：very typical for Toxoplasma gondii. Tachyzoites spread rapidly; bradyzoites persist in tissues.

10.3 Lower-yield oral transmission examples｜经口传播里相对次重点的例子

Cyclospora cayetanensis：sporulated oocysts from contaminated water or food; monoxenous life cycle; diarrhea.

Isospora belli：mature oocysts containing sporozoites; monoxenous cycle; diarrhea, especially worth recognizing in immunocompromised settings.

Angiostrongylus cantonensis / A. costaricensis：oral acquisition from contaminated food or intermediate hosts such as snails/slugs; meningitis or digestive disease.

Dracunculus medinensis：infection by drinking water containing Cyclops; classic old-school example of control by water filtration.

Echinococcus granulosus：heteroxenous oral transmission through embryonated eggs; hydatid cyst mainly in the liver, sometimes lung or brain.

10.4 Muco-cutaneous / contact transmission beyond the safest answers｜接触传播的补充识别

Strongyloides stercoralis：still the safest exam answer for moist-soil skin penetration.

Hookworms 钩虫：another classic moist-soil skin penetration example; useful if the examiner asks for another valid parasite.

Schistosoma spp.：cercariae can penetrate the skin in contaminated fresh water. Important because the snail is an intermediate host, not a vector.

Cutaneous larva migrans 皮肤幼虫移行症：classically from animal hookworm larvae penetrating human skin.

Sarcoptes scabiei：contagious dermatitis by close human contact; useful if the question shifts from life cycle to cutaneous parasitosis.

Tunga penetrans：sand flea / tungiasis; a less central but recognizable muco-cutaneous ectoparasite example.

10.5 Vector-borne names worth recognizing even if not top priority｜最好眼熟的虫媒名称

Human African trypanosomiasis 非洲人类锥虫病：Trypanosoma brucei gambiense and T. b. rhodesiense, transmitted by tsetse flies; CNS involvement, coma, death.

Chagas disease 查加斯病：Trypanosoma cruzi, transmitted by triatomine bugs; cardiomyopathy and digestive megasyndromes.

Lymphatic filariasis 淋巴丝虫病：Wuchereria bancrofti, Brugia malayi, Brugia timori; mosquito-borne; lymphedema.

Loiasis 罗阿丝虫病：transmitted by Chrysops; Calabar swellings and eye worm.

Onchocerciasis onchocerciasis / river blindness 盘尾丝虫病：transmitted by blackflies; itchy skin lesions, nodules, visual impairment, blindness.

10.6 Leishmania and environmental change｜利什曼原虫与环境变化

Climate change 气候变化：changes in temperature and rainfall can modify vector distribution and seasonality.

Globalization 全球化：trade, transport, and migration facilitate the spread of vectors and parasites.

Human pressure 人类活动：deforestation, urbanization, and water management increase contact between humans, vectors, and reservoirs.

Leishmania infantum and dogs：dog is an important reservoir in the Mediterranean basin; this is worth remembering because it links zoonosis, reservoir, and vector transmission in one example.

Aedes albopictus / tiger mosquito and other invasive vectors：these examples are more eco-epidemiology than classical parasitology, but they help explain why emerging vector-borne diseases matter.

10.7 如果出了你没重点背到的偏题，答题怎么保底｜How to rescue marks on a less-familiar question

问Step 1：state the transmission route first. Oral? Vector-borne? Muco-cutaneous? Sexual?
答

Step 2：state the infective form if you know it. Cyst, oocyst, embryonated egg, larva, promastigote, amastigote.

Step 3：state whether the life cycle is monoxenous or heteroxenous.

Step 4：add one hallmark organ or symptom. Example: liver abscess, diarrhea, CNS disease, lymphedema, blindness.

就算你记不住全图，只要把这四步写出来，通常也能拿到过程分。

11
这一部分怎么用｜How to use this added section

建议顺序：先背前面的高频主干；如果还有时间，再把本节当“扫盲补丁”快速看一遍。不要反过来。核心分值仍然主要来自：英文例子表、传播方式、life cycle、Leishmania、vector control、模板题。

11.1 一分钟扫名字｜One-minute glance-only name list

这些不是最优先，但建议眼熟：Cyclospora cayetanensis, Isospora belli, Dracunculus medinensis, Schistosoma spp., Trypanosoma cruzi, Trypanosoma brucei gambiense / rhodesiense, Wuchereria bancrofti, Brugia malayi, Loa loa, Onchocerca volvulus, Sarcoptes scabiei, Tunga penetrans.

11.2 别在次重点上浪费时间｜Do not over-invest here

如果你时间很紧，这一部分只要做到“看见题目不陌生、能写出传播方式和一个代表症状”就够了；真正决定通过率的，仍然是前面的高频主干。

01Class Introduction virology 29-10-24_PourEcampus

历史与病毒学发展History and Progress in Virology

1
19世纪末，巴斯德 End of 19th century, Pasteur

那时“病毒”泛指所有感染性病原体。 At that time, "virus" referred to all types of infectious agents.

“virus”一词拉丁语意为毒药或毒素。 The word "virus" comes from Latin, meaning poison or venom.

如炭疽病毒、疟疾病毒。 For example, anthrax virus, malaria virus. （补充：录音提到巴斯德通过证实病毒存在，推动了病毒学的起步，且在未鉴定病毒时就研发了狂犬病疫苗2。）

2
1892年，伊万诺夫斯基 1892, Ivanowski

发现烟草花叶病原体具有滤过性，可以通过细菌滤器。 Discovered the tobacco mosaic agent was filterable and could pass through bacterial filters.

这是病毒与细菌的重要区别。 This is an important distinction between viruses and bacteria. （补充：老师指出“滤过性”意味着病毒颗粒足够小，可通过滤器，且可通过滤过物传染其他生物体。）

3
1898年，贝耶林克 1898, Beijerinck

发现病毒不仅能滤过，而且具有连续传染性，首次明确提出病毒概念。 Discovered that viruses are not only filterable but also serially transmissible, and first introduced the concept of virus.

提出“活的传染性液体”（contagium vivum fluidum）或“感染性活流体”观点。 Proposed the idea of "contagium vivum fluidum" or "infectious living fluid". （补充：贝耶林克强调，病毒不像细菌那样在培养基中生长，只能在生物体内繁殖。）

4
光学显微镜下不可见的因子 Agents invisible under the optical microscope

“超病毒”或“可滤过病毒”概念由此产生。 Hence the terms "ultravirus" or "filterable virus" were created.

病毒体积过小，普通光学显微镜下不可见。 Viruses are too small to be seen under a regular optical microscope. （补充：老师再次强调常规光镜看不到病毒，实验操作时要注意这一点。）

5
20世纪初人兽疾病其他病毒发现 Other agents of human and animal diseases

1900年发现黄热病毒，1903年狂犬病毒，1909年脊髓灰质炎病毒，1911年鲁氏肉瘤病毒。 The yellow fever virus (1900), rabies virus (1903), poliovirus (1909), and Rous sarcoma virus (1911) were discovered. （录音补充：这些发现推动了病毒与癌症关系及病毒学研究发展，并帮助明确了病毒可致肿瘤2。）

6
1915-1917年，Twort与D’Herelle 1915-1917, Twort and D’Hérelle

发现噬菌体，即感染细菌的病毒。 Discovered bacteriophages, viruses that infect bacteria.

典型噬菌体如T4型，头部和尾部结构分明。 A typical bacteriophage is the T4-like virus, with distinct head and tail structures. （录音补充：噬菌体结构独特，能注入基因进入细菌，被形容为“像蜘蛛一样的形态”。）

7
1939年，冯·考舍 1939, Von Kausche

首次利用电子显微镜观察到烟草花叶病毒。 First observation of a virus (tobacco mosaic virus) under an electron microscope.

电子显微镜突破了病毒不可见的局限。 Electron microscopy overcame the limitation of virus invisibility by light microscopy.

8
1983年，巴尔-西努西和蒙塔尼耶 1983, F. Barré-Sinoussi and L. Montagnier

发现人类免疫缺陷病毒（HIV）。 Discovery of the human immunodeficiency virus (HIV).

9
新病毒不断被发现 New viruses are still being discovered

1989年，乙型肝炎病毒；2001年，人偏肺病毒；2003年，巨拟菌病毒（感染变形虫） 1989, hepatitis C virus; 2001, metapneumovirus; 2003, mimivirus (an amoeba virus)

2007年，多瘤病毒KI与WU；2008年，MCPγV多瘤病毒（与Merkel细胞癌相关） 2007, polyomavirus KI and WU; 2008, polyomavirus MCPγV (responsible for Merkel’s cell carcinoma)

2013年，潘多拉病毒（感染变形虫）；2019年，SARS-CoV-2。 2013, pandoravirus (infects amoeba); 2019, SARS-CoV-2. （录音补充：近年来气候变化等因素促进了新病毒的发现，很多病毒只在免疫抑制人群中致病2。）

10
病毒中的病毒——病毒噬菌体概念的出现 Recent emergence of the virophage concept

斯普特尼克（Sputnik）是一种感染Mimiviridae家族病毒的病毒。 Sputnik is a virophage that infects viruses of the Mimiviridae family.

它是巨病毒的专属寄生物，可整合入巨病毒基因组，导致巨病毒复制减少，产生复制缺陷。 It is a mandatory parasite of the Mimivirus, may integrate into the giant virus genome, and causes decreased Multiplication and production defects of Mimivirus. （录音补充：病毒噬菌体为病毒与病毒之间的新型关系，被认为可能用于抗病毒及疫苗载体的未来应用。）

主要信息补充

病毒不断有新类型被发现，研究空间巨大；很多新发现病毒与气候等环境因素变迁有关。 New types of viruses continue to be discovered, indicating vast research potential; many discoveries are related to environmental changes such as climate change.

病毒噬菌体可望为抗病毒治疗和疫苗技术带来新突破。 Virophages may provide new breakthroughs for antiviral therapies and vaccine technologies.

病毒的定义与基本特征Definitions and Essential Criteria of Viruses

一、1953年André Lwoff对病毒的定义, Definition of viruses by André Lwoff

病毒是具有感染性且可能致病的颗粒。 Viruses are infectious and potentially pathogenic particles.

病毒是由单一类型核酸（RNA或DNA）和蛋白质组成的核蛋白实体。 They are nucleoproteic entities with a single type of nucleic acid (RNA or DNA).

病毒通过宿主细胞利用其遗传物质进行复制。 They are reproduced by the cell from their genetic material.

病毒不能像细胞或细菌那样生长和分裂。 They are unable to grow and divide like cells or bacteria.

病毒没有Lipman系统（即缺乏自身代谢/能量系统）。 They have no Lipman system (i.e., no intrinsic metabolism or energy system)。

二、病毒颗粒Virion

病毒颗粒指的是成熟的、具有感染性并且处于细胞外的病毒粒子。 A virion is a viral particle which is mature, infectious, and extracellular1。

三、病毒的四个基本判据Four Essential Criteria of Viruses

1
单一类型核酸（DNA或RNA） One single type of nucleic acid (DNA or RNA)

病毒基因组只能是DNA或RNA中的一种。 The viral genome is either DNA or RNA, never both。

2
遗传物质驱动的复制 Replication from the genetic material

病毒必须依靠自身基因组完成复制过程。 Viruses replicate by using their own genetic material。

3
绝对细胞寄生性 Mandatory cellular parasitism

病毒缺乏独立的酶系统和能量系统，无法自行代谢。 Viruses lack their own enzymatic and energetic system, so they cannot metabolize independently。

只能在活细胞中培养分离。 They can only be isolated using live cell culture。

这也是抗病毒治疗难度高的原因之一。 This is why antiviral therapy is difficult。

4
明确的结构特征 Defined structure

病毒结构与细菌不同，有其独特的形态。 The structure of viruses is different from that of bacteria; viruses have unique morphology。

常见类型为二十面体或螺旋体衣壳。 Typical capsid types are icosahedral or helical。

补充（录音要点）Additional Teacher’s Notes (from Recording)

病毒需要宿主细胞才能进行生命周期的各个步骤，表现为绝对寄生性。 Viruses require a host cell for all steps of their life cycle, demonstrating absolute parasitism。

病毒无法独立生长、繁殖，亦无自有能量体系，且只含一种核酸。 Viruses cannot independently grow or reproduce, possess no intrinsic energy system, and have only one type of nucleic acid。

“成熟的病毒颗粒＝virion”，只能在细胞外存活并具有感染性。 The mature and infectious form, which is extracellular, is called a "virion"。

小结Summary

病毒是具有感染性、绝对细胞依赖性、结构独特、且只含一种核酸的非细胞生物。 Viruses are infectious, absolutely cell-dependent, structurally unique, and possess a genome of only one nucleic acid type, thus classed as acellular organisms。

病毒的结构Structure of a virus

一、基本结构组成 General structural elements

病毒的结构由核心核酸、衣壳（蛋白外壳），部分病毒还有包膜和核心组成。 The structure of a virus consists of nucleic acid at its core and a capsid (protein shell). Some viruses also possess an envelope and a core.

所有病毒都有核酸（DNA或RNA）和衣壳，这两部分合称“核衣壳”。 All viruses contain nucleic acid (DNA or RNA) and a capsid. Together, these are called the nucleocapsid.

有些病毒还会包含包膜，此外某些病毒带有“核心”，即蛋白与核酸的复合物（如逆转录病毒）。 Some viruses have an envelope. Additionally, some viruses have a “core”, which is a complex of proteins and nucleic acid (e.g., retroviruses).

二、病毒结构示意与重点解析 Schematic and key points

1
病毒的核心是遗传物质，可能是DNA或RNA，只能有其一。 The core of the virus is its genetic material, which can be either DNA or RNA, never both.
2
核酸外包裹蛋白衣壳，衣壳由蛋白质亚单位装配而成，负责保护核酸并参与感染。 Surrounding the nucleic acid is the capsid, formed by protein subunits, which protects the genome and is involved in infection.
3
有些病毒的核酸和蛋白质还会形成一个核心结构，特别是在逆转录病毒中。 In some viruses, the nucleic acid and proteins further assemble into a core structure, especially in retroviruses.
4
部分病毒颗粒外还有包膜，包膜由宿主细胞膜来源，其中嵌有病毒自身糖蛋白。 Some viral particles have an outer envelope, derived from the host membrane and containing viral glycoproteins.

三、两大不变元素及变化结构 Two constant and two variable features

不变成分

1
核酸（DNA或RNA）
2
衣壳 The two constant components:
3
Nucleic acid (DNA or RNA)
4
Capsid

可变成分

1
包膜
2
核心（蛋白+核酸复合物） The two variable components:
3
Envelope
4
Core (protein + nucleic acid complex)

四、病毒的常见形态 Typical shapes of viruses

1
球形（20面体）：如腺病毒、诺如病毒、甲型肝炎病毒。 Spherical (icosahedral): e.g., adenovirus, norovirus, hepatitis A virus.
2
棒状（螺旋体）：代表是烟草花叶病毒。 Stick-shaped (helical): the tobacco mosaic virus is the classic example.
复杂结构
如单纯疱疹病毒（有二十面体衣壳及包膜）、埃博拉病毒（长丝状）、狂犬病毒（弹头状）。 Complex shape: e.g., herpes simplex virus (with icosahedral capsid and envelope), Ebola virus (filamentous), rabies virus (bullet-shaped).
更复杂的病毒形态
如痘病毒（橄榄形、较大）、噬菌体（头部为二十面体，带有尾管和尾丝，形如“蜘蛛”结构）。 Even more complex shapes: e.g., poxvirus (oval and large), bacteriophages (with an icosahedral head, tail tube, and long tail fibers; resembling a “spider” structure).

五、老师补充解读 Teacher’s Additional Explanation

课上强调，不同病毒结构影响其感染机制及应用，如腺病毒和HSV都可作为生物技术的病毒载体。 It was emphasized in class that different viral structures influence their infection mechanisms and applications; for instance, both adenovirus and HSV can be used as viral vectors in biotechnology.

棒状病毒（如烟草花叶病毒）为螺旋对称，显示出与球形病毒完全不同的外观。 Rod-shaped viruses (like tobacco mosaic virus) show helical symmetry and have an appearance totally distinct from spherical viruses.

病毒形态非常多样，尤其复杂病毒如噬菌体、痘病毒体积巨大、结构各异。 Viral morphology is highly diverse. Complex viruses like bacteriophages and poxviruses are large and structurally unique.

不同病毒类型的形态可在电子显微镜下观察，是病毒判别及研究的重要基础。 Different morphologies of viruses can be observed under the electron microscope, providing an important basis for virus identification and research.

六、小结 Summary

病毒结构包括核心核酸、衣壳，部分含有包膜或核心。球形、棒状、复杂结构等多种形态决定了它们的功能和应用。 The structure of a virus includes a core nucleic acid and a capsid, with some having an envelope or core. Spherical, rod-like, and complex forms determine their functions and applications.

病毒大小与基因组结构Size and Genomic Structure of Viruses

一、病毒的大小范围与典型实例Size Range of Viruses and Typical Examples

1
病毒大小差异极大，从最大的巨病毒（Mimivirus，约650纳米）到最小的细小病毒（Parvovirus，约20纳米）。 The size of viruses varies greatly, from the largest mimivirus (about 650 nm) to the smallest parvovirus (about 20 nm).
2
巨病毒最初因其体积可与细菌媲美，曾一度被误认为是细菌。 The mimivirus was initially mistaken for a bacterium due to its large size comparable to bacteria.
3
巨病毒感染变形虫，这代表其宿主范围可以涵盖原生生物。 Mimivirus infects amoebae, showing that their host range can include protozoa.
4
“Mimicking Microbe”意指其具有类似细菌的外观，因此得名Mimivirus。 The term “Mimicking Microbe” highlights its resemblance to bacteria, thus named mimivirus.
5
细小病毒则仅有20纳米左右，是目前已知最小的病毒之一。 Parvoviruses are about 20 nm, making them one of the smallest known viruses.
6
课上强调，病毒肉眼不可见，电子显微镜下才可分辨病毒与细菌的形态与大小差异。 In class it was stressed that viruses cannot be seen under a standard light microscope and can only be distinguished from bacteria by electron microscopy.

二、常见病毒的具体尺寸与类别Examples and Sizes of Common Viruses

1
小型病毒包括细小病毒（18到26纳米）和脊髓灰质炎病毒（27纳米）。 Small viruses include parvovirus (18 to 26 nm) and poliovirus (27 nm).
2
大型病毒如流感病毒（100纳米）、单纯疱疹病毒（150到200纳米）、痘苗病毒（250到300纳米）。 Big viruses include flu virus (100 nm), herpes simplex virus (150 to 200 nm), and vaccinia virus (250 to 300 nm).
3
课上指出，病毒尺寸的了解有助于其研究与分类。 It was noted in class that knowing the size of viruses aids in their research and classification.

三、病毒基因组的主要研究参数Main Parameters for Studying Viral Genomes

本质
每种病毒只含有DNA或RNA之一，二者不会共存。 Nature: Each viral family contains either DNA or RNA, never both.
尺寸
不同病毒其基因组长度相差巨大。 Size: The genome length varies greatly across viruses.
结构
可为单链（ss）、双链（ds）或亚基因组片段。 Structure: The genome may be single-stranded, double-stranded, or contain subgenomic fragments.
拓扑
可为线性或环状。 Topology: The genome can be linear or circular.
碱基组成
关注G/C含量，影响基因组稳定性。 Base composition: G/C content is important for genome stability.
6
可能出现重复序列，包括 3'多聚腺苷段、异常碱基/糖、回文末端等，有助于环化。 Presence of repetitive sequences may include 3' polyadenylated segments, abnormal bases or sugars, and palindromic ends for circularization.
7
基因组类型是病毒分类的重要标准。 The nature of the genome is a key criterion for virus classification.

四、DNA病毒基因组的具体特征Features of the DNA Viral Genomes

1
基因组大小变化范围很大，如乙型肝炎病毒为3200碱基，细小病毒为5700碱基，人巨细胞病毒达235,000碱基，巨病毒则有1,200,000碱基。 The DNA genome size varies a lot: hepatitis B virus has about 3,200 bases, parvovirus 5,700, human cytomegalovirus 235,000, and mimivirus up to 1,200,000 bases.
2
G/C含量可变，范围为32%到75%。 The G/C content ranges from 32% to 75%.
3
某些DNA与碱性蛋白结合，如部分细胞组蛋白（多瘤病毒科、乳头瘤病毒科）或病毒自身蛋白（正疱疹病毒科）。 Some DNA is associated with basic proteins, such as cellular histones (Polyomaviridae, Papillomaviridae) or viral proteins (Orthoherepsviridae).
4
课上强调，DNA与组蛋白结合可影响其在宿主细胞中的生存和表达，并与病毒的潜伏特性、基因治疗应用相关。 In class it was stressed that DNA association with histones affects persistence and expression in host cells, and relates to latency and gene therapy.

五、DNA病毒的结构特点Structural Features of DNA Viruses

1
大多数DNA病毒基因组为双链线性DNA，呈经典的双螺旋三维结构。 Most DNA viruses have double-stranded, linear genomes with the classic three-dimensional double helix structure.
2
部分DNA病毒为环状或部分双链结构，具体依病毒家族而定。 Some DNA viruses are circular or partially double-stranded, depending on the virus family.
3
课上补充，不同DNA结构有助于病毒生命周期的不同阶段、基因表达以及宿主免疫逃逸。 It was added in class that different DNA structures aid different stages of the viral life cycle, gene expression, and immune evasion.

六、小结Summary

1
病毒在大小和基因组结构上多样，既有微小也有巨大类型。 Viruses are diverse in size and genome structure, with both very small and very large types.
2
病毒基因组的种类、尺寸、结构和与蛋白的结合方式，是病毒分类和功能的重要依据。 The genome type, size, structure, and association with proteins are essential for virus classification and function.
3
掌握病毒的这些基本特征，对于理解其生物学行为、致病机制及后续科研应用至关重要。 Understanding these features is crucial for studying viral biology, pathogenesis, and subsequent research applications.

DNA基因组的示意图与常见家族 Schematic Representation of DNA Genomes and Common Families

RNA基因组的特点 Features of RNA Genomes

一、DNA病毒基因组的类型及代表家族 Types of DNA Virus Genomes and Representative Families

DNA病毒的基因组类型主要包括三种
单链、双链和部分双链。 The main types of DNA virus genomes include single-stranded, double-stranded, and partially double-stranded.

单链DNA病毒（如Parvoviridae科）仅有一股DNA链。 Single-stranded DNA viruses (such as Parvoviridae) contain only one DNA strand.

大多数DNA病毒为双链，可分为线性和环状结构。 Most DNA viruses are double-stranded and can be linear or circular in structure.

双链线性DNA代表有Adenoviridae科和Orthoherpesviridae科。 Linear double-stranded DNA examples include Adenoviridae and Orthoherpesviridae.

双链环状DNA常见于Papillomaviridae科和Polyomaviridae科。 Circular double-stranded DNA is found in Papillomaviridae and Polyomaviridae.

部分双链DNA病毒以Hepadnaviridae为代表，其基因组仅部分区域为双链。 Partially double-stranded DNA viruses are represented by the Hepadnaviridae family, where only parts of the genome are double-stranded.

二、DNA病毒家族的结构特点及示意 Structural Features and Schematics of DNA Virus Families

Parvoviridae科的病毒基因组结构为单股DNA，外层为规则五边形对称的衣壳。 Parvoviridae have a single-stranded DNA genome surrounded by a symmetric pentagonal capsid.

Adenoviridae科具有双股线性DNA，衣壳复杂，包括纤突、顶点、六联体蛋白和核心蛋白。 Adenoviridae contain linear double-stranded DNA with a complex capsid including fibers, penton bases, hexon proteins, and core proteins.

Papillomaviridae科的基因组为双链环状DNA，DNA与组蛋白结合，共同被衣壳蛋白包裹。 Papillomaviridae feature circular double-stranded DNA associated with histones, all surrounded by a capsid protein shell.

Hepadnaviridae科包括部分双链DNA，并包裹于富含多种糖蛋白的包膜下，同时存在聚合酶等酶。 Hepadnaviridae have a partially double-stranded DNA genome within an envelope rich in glycoproteins, also containing polymerase.

课上强调，不同基因组结构影响病毒生命周期和感染模式，如组蛋白结合帮助病毒基因组稳定且便于潜伏或持久存在。 In class, it was stressed that different genome structures influence the virus life cycle and infection patterns, for example, histone association helps stabilize the genome and facilitates latency or persistence.

三、RNA病毒基因组的类型与代表 Features and Types of RNA Virus Genomes

大部分RNA病毒的基因组是单股且线性。 Most RNA viruses have a single-stranded, linear genome.

少部分RNA病毒为双链结构，典型代表为Sedoreoviridae科。 A small portion of RNA viruses are double-stranded, with Sedoreoviridae as a typical example.

部分RNA病毒基因组以片段存在，如Sedoreoviridae有10或11个片段，流感病毒有7到8个片段。 Some RNA viruses have segmented genomes: Sedoreoviridae have 10 to 11 fragments, and flu viruses have 7 to 8 fragments.

片段化的基因组有利于基因的快速多样化和易于重组。 Segmented genomes contribute to rapid genetic diversity and easier recombination.

四、总结 Summary

DNA病毒基因组的主要类型包括单链、双链（分为线性和环状）、以及部分双链，代表性家族分别有Parvoviridae、Adenoviridae、Papillomaviridae和Hepadnaviridae。 The main types of DNA virus genomes are single-stranded, double-stranded (linear or circular), and partially double-stranded. Representative families are Parvoviridae, Adenoviridae, Papillomaviridae, and Hepadnaviridae.

RNA病毒基因组以单股线性为主，少数为双股或多片段。 RNA virus genomes are mainly single-stranded and linear, with some being double-stranded or segmented.

病毒基因组结构的多样性是其生物学行为和致病机制多样化的重要基础。 The diversity of viral genome structures forms the basis for the wide range of biological behaviors and pathogenic mechanisms of viruses.

RNA基因组的结构与极性 Schematic Representation and Polarity of RNA Genomes

一、RNA基因组的基本特点 Basic Features of RNA Genomes

大多数RNA病毒的基因组为单链且线性。 Most RNA virus genomes are single-stranded and linear.

少数RNA病毒为双链结构，代表为Sedoreoviridae科。 A few RNA viruses have double-stranded genomes, with Sedoreoviridae as an example.

部分RNA病毒的基因组呈亚基因组片段化，如Sedoreoviridae有10-11个片段，流感病毒有7-8个片段。 Some RNA viruses have subgenomic fragments; Sedoreoviridae have 10–11 fragments, and flu viruses have 7–8 fragments.

片段化的基因组有利于遗传多样性和基因重组。 Segmented genomes facilitate genetic diversity and recombination.

二、RNA基因组的分类与代表性家族 Types and Representative Families of RNA Genomes

单链线性RNA基因组典型家族
Picornaviridae、Togaviridae、Paramyxoviridae。 Typical families with single-stranded linear RNA genomes include Picornaviridae, Togaviridae, and Paramyxoviridae.

单链分段RNA基因组代表有Orthomyxoviridae和Bunyaviridae。 Segmented single-stranded RNA genomes are found in Orthomyxoviridae and Bunyaviridae.

单链环状RNA为Hepatitis D病毒的特征。 Circular single-stranded RNA is characteristic of hepatitis D virus.

双链分段RNA是Sedoreoviridae（如轮状病毒）所特有的。 Segmented double-stranded RNA is unique to Sedoreoviridae, e.g., rotavirus.

三、多片段结构多样化实例 Polygenomic Organization Examples

Orthomyxoviridae（如流感病毒）基因组由7到8个片段组成。 Orthomyxoviridae (such as flu virus) genomes consist of 7–8 fragments.

Sedoreoviridae（如轮状病毒）一般有10个片段，轮状病毒为11个片段。 Sedoreoviridae (such as rotavirus) typically have 10 fragments, and rotavirus has 11 fragments.

这些片段各自编码不同蛋白质，实现多基因表达。 Each fragment encodes a different protein, allowing for polygenic expression.

四、特殊RNA病毒家族结构说明 Unique RNA Families Organization

Retroviridae家族包含2条线性单链RNA，这两条RNA在5'端通过共价键连接。 Retroviridae family viruses contain two linear single-stranded RNAs, covalently linked at their 5' ends.

Hepatitis D病毒的基因组呈环状单链，且为负链伪双链结构。 The hepatitis D virus has a circular, single-stranded RNA genome with negative polarity, forming a pseudo-double-stranded structure.

五、单链RNA的极性 Polarity of Single-stranded RNAs

正链RNA（正极性）：可直接被翻译为病毒蛋白，具有直接感染性。 Positive-sense (positive polarity) RNA: can be directly translated into viral proteins and is directly infectious.

正链RNA的代表家族有Coronaviridae、Picornaviridae、Togaviridae。 Representative families for positive-sense RNA include Coronaviridae, Picornaviridae, and Togaviridae.

一些正链RNA病毒基因组带有帽子结构或者polyA尾，有助于mRNA功能和保护。 Some positive-sense RNA genomes have a cap and/or polyadenylated sequence, aiding mRNA function and protection.

负链RNA（负极性）：需先转录为mRNA后才能翻译。 Negative-sense (negative polarity) RNA: must be transcribed into mRNA before translation.

负链RNA的代表家族为Orthomyxoviridae和Rhabdoviridae。 Representative families for negative-sense RNA include Orthomyxoviridae and Rhabdoviridae.

负链RNA病毒需自带转录酶（RNA依赖的RNA聚合酶），因宿主细胞不具备此功能。 Negative-sense RNA viruses must carry their own RNA-dependent RNA polymerase, since the host cell lacks this enzyme.

六、总结 Summary

RNA病毒基因组存在单链线性、分段、环状和双链分段等多种形式。 RNA virus genomes exist in various forms: single-stranded linear, segmented, circular, and double-stranded segmented.

单链RNA的极性决定其能否直接作为mRNA翻译，以及感染机制。 The polarity of single-stranded RNA determines if it functions as mRNA directly and influences infection mechanisms.

多片段结构和不同极性增加了病毒的生物学多样性与进化能力。 Segmented genome structure and different polarity increase viral biological diversity and evolutionary capacity.

病毒衣壳与螺旋对称性管状衣壳 The Capsid and Tubular Capsid with Helical Symmetry

一、衣壳的基本特性 Key Characteristics of the Capsid

病毒衣壳是由蛋白质构成的高抗性、极其稳定的结构。 The viral capsid is a highly resistant and extremely stable protein structure.

衣壳可由单一类型蛋白或少量不同蛋白聚合而成。 Capsids can polymerize from a single type or a small number of different proteins.

衣壳有两大类
一类为具有螺旋对称性的管状衣壳，另一类为具有立方对称性的二十面体衣壳。 There are two main categories of capsid: tubular capsid with helical symmetry and icosahedral capsid with cubic symmetry.

衣壳的自组装遵循自组装原理，即蛋白质间能自动识别并聚合形成稳定结构。 Capsid assembly follows the principle of self-assembly; proteins can spontaneously recognize and aggregate into a stable structure.

衣壳的三个主要作用：

1
保护基因组免受外界环境损伤。
2
协助无包膜病毒吸附到宿主细胞膜上。
3
将病毒基因组运输至细胞内部。 The capsid has three main roles:
4
Protecting the genome from the external environment;
5
Facilitating attachment of naked viruses to the host cell membrane;
6
Transporting the viral genome inside the cell.

衣壳的对称性是病毒分类的重要标准。 The symmetry of the capsid is a criteria for virus classification.

二、螺旋对称性管状衣壳的模型与实例 Study Model and Examples of Tubular Capsid with Helical Symmetry

经典研究模型为烟草花叶病毒。 The classic study model is tobacco mosaic virus.

烟草花叶病毒感染后的植物叶片可见花叶斑驳。 Leaves of tobacco plants show mosaic mottling after infection by tobacco mosaic virus.

病毒颗粒呈刚性空心棒状，长约300nm，直径约15nm。 The virus particles are rigid hollow rods, about 300nm in length and 15nm in diameter.

三、烟草花叶病毒结构与特性 Structure and Features of Tobacco Mosaic Virus

烟草花叶病毒是第一个被结晶并通过X射线衍射分析的病毒（20世纪30年代）。 Tobacco mosaic virus was the first virus crystallized and analyzed by X-ray diffraction (1930s).

其为无包膜病毒，基因组为正极性单股RNA，全长约6.4kb。 It is a non-enveloped virus with a positive-sense, single-stranded RNA genome, about 6.4kb long.

RNA以螺旋方式被包装在管状衣壳中。 The RNA is helically arranged inside a tubular capsid.

整体由2130个完全相同的衣壳蛋白亚单位组成。 The whole structure comprises 2,130 identical capsid protein subunits.

四、烟草花叶病毒衣壳的装配与结构细节 Assembly and Details of Tobacco Mosaic Virus Capsid

衣壳由158个氨基酸组成的单一蛋白质构成，蛋白为马蹄形（sabot）结构。 The capsid is composed of a single protein of 158 amino acids, in a sabot shape.

基因组核酸长度决定病毒棒状颗粒的长度。 The length of the nucleic acid determines the length of the rod.

螺旋对称性的管状衣壳装配可以以穿鞋带作类比——衣壳蛋白如鞋带，核酸贯穿其中。 The assembly of the helical tubular capsid can be likened to threading a shoelace—the capsid proteins form the tube and nucleic acid is inserted like a lace.

五、动物病毒中螺旋对称性管状衣壳 Animal Viruses with Helical Symmetry Tubular Capsid

动物病毒中具螺旋对称性的病毒总带有包膜。 Animal viruses with helical symmetry are always enveloped.

病毒的核衣壳是柔性的，并在包膜内呈折叠状态。 Their nucleocapsids are flexible and folded inside the envelope.

代表病毒包括狂犬病毒和流感病毒。 Representative viruses include rabies virus and flu virus.

六、狂犬病毒和流感病毒实例 Examples: Rabies Virus and Flu Virus

狂犬病毒核衣壳为螺旋结构，直径15nm，长度120-180nm，宽约50nm。基因组为负极性、线性、单股RNA。 Rabies virus nucleocapsid has a helical structure, 15nm diameter, 120-180nm length, about 50nm width; genome is negative-sense, linear, single-stranded RNA.

流感病毒核衣壳同为螺旋结构，直径8nm，完整病毒颗粒约100nm，基因组为负极性、分节（7或8段）、单股线性RNA。 Flu virus nucleocapsid also has helical structure, 8nm diameter, whole particle about 100nm, genome is negative-sense, segmented (7 or 8 fragments), single-stranded and linear RNA.

补充讲解老师强调要点 Additional Key Teaching Points

老师指出螺旋对称性衣壳的装配可用“穿鞋带”模型直观理解，帮助学生在家用鞋和管辅助理解病毒结构。 The teacher highlighted using the shoe and tube model to help visualize the assembly of helical capsids, allowing students to use household items (shoe and tubing) to better understand virus structure.

动物病毒的螺旋对称性核衣壳一定带包膜，区别于植物病毒。 Animal helical nucleocapsids are always enveloped, unlike plant viruses.

这些结构特征不仅有助于分类，也是病毒感染机制和稳定性的基础。 Such structural features are critical for classification and are also fundamental for understanding infection mechanisms and viral stability.

二十面体衣壳的立方对称性 | Icosahedral Capsid with Cubic Symmetry

一、二十面体基本结构 Icosahedron Basic Structure

二十面体是正二十面体，有20个面、12个顶点、30条边。 An icosahedron is a regular polyhedron with 20 faces, 12 summits (vertices), and 30 edges.

每个面都是等边三角形，构成了高度对称和稳定的结构。 Each face is an equilateral triangle, resulting in a highly symmetrical and stable structure.

二、二十面体衣壳的组成 Components of Icosahedral Capsid

二十面体的面为等边三角形。 The faces of the icosahedron are equilateral triangles.

衣壳由结构亚单位（壳粒，capsomere）组成，包括五聚体（penton）与六聚体（hexon）。 Capsid consists of structural subunits (capsomeres), including pentamers (pentons) and hexamers (hexons).

所有具立方对称性的病毒二十面体衣壳均有12个五聚体，分别位于12个顶点； All viruses with cubic symmetry icosahedral capsids have 12 pentons, each at a summit of the icosahedron.

六聚体数量在不同病毒中变化，分布于面与边上，以调整衣壳大小。 The number of hexons is variable among viruses and is located on edges and faces to adjust capsid size.

三、典型组装方式 Examples of Assembly

最简形式T=1二十面体，仅有12个五聚体，含60个蛋白亚单位。 The simplest T=1 icosahedron has 12 pentons and 60 protein subunits.

复杂型如T=3，含12个五聚体和20个六聚体，共180个蛋白亚单位。 A more complex T=3 form contains 12 pentons and 20 hexons, totaling 180 protein subunits.

随着T数增加，二十面体可包含12个五聚体和更多六聚体，例如T=7有60个六聚体，T=25有240个六聚体。 With higher T numbers, the icosahedron can include 12 pentons and increasing hexons: T=7 has 60 hexons, T=25 has 240 hexons.

相应地，这些大型二十面体分别包含420（T=7）和1500（T=25）个蛋白亚单位。 Accordingly, these large icosahedrons have 420 (T=7) and 1,500 (T=25) protein subunits respectively.

四、病毒实例及二十面体衣壳结构 Virus Examples and Icosahedral Capsid Structure

HPV（乳头瘤病毒，Papillomaviridae）：

二十面体衣壳，由72个壳粒组成，直径52-55nm，基因组为环状双链DNA。

HPV (Papillomaviridae): Icosahedral capsid with 72 capsomeres, viral particle diameter 52–55nm, genome is circular double-stranded DNA.

脊髓灰质炎病毒（小RNA病毒科，Picornaviridae）：

二十面体衣壳，162个壳粒，病毒直径约30nm，基因组为单链正义极性线状RNA。

Poliovirus (Picornaviridae): Icosahedral capsid with 162 capsomeres, particle diameter about 30nm, genome is positive-sense, single-stranded, linear RNA.

腺病毒（腺病毒科，Adenoviridae）：

二十面体衣壳，252个壳粒，直径90-120nm，基因组为双链线状DNA。

Adenovirus (Adenoviridae): Icosahedral capsid with 252 capsomeres, diameter 90–120nm, genome is double-stranded, linear DNA.

五、通用结构原理 Universal Principle of Construction

二十面体衣壳的对称性和几何结构可见于许多自然和工程结构中，如蒙特利尔的地球穹顶、足球和C₆₀巴克球分子。 The symmetry and geometry of the icosahedral capsid are also seen in many natural and man-made structures, such as the geodesic dome in Montreal, football, and buckminsterfullerene (C₆₀) molecules.

教师举例说明，这类高度对称结构具备良好的稳定性和自组装能力，是许多病毒选择二十面体对称性的原因。 The teacher gave examples to illustrate that this highly symmetrical structure provides stability and self-assembly ability, which is why many viruses adopt icosahedral symmetry.

六、总结 Summary

二十面体衣壳由等边三角形面、十二个顶点和三十条边构成，通过五聚体和六聚体实现不同大小的装配，广泛存在于动物及植物病毒。 Icosahedral capsids are formed from equilateral triangular faces, 12 vertices, and 30 edges; with pentons and hexons allowing assembly in various sizes; they are common in animal and plant viruses.

二十面体结构不仅赋予病毒高度机械稳定性，还简化了基因对结构蛋白的编码要求。 The icosahedral structure not only grants viruses high mechanical stability but also minimizes genetic coding requirements for structural proteins.

病毒包膜的结构与来源The Structure and Origin of the Viral Envelope

一、包膜的基本特性 Basic Features of the Viral Envelope

包膜在病毒中属于可有可无的结构。 The envelope is an optional structure for viruses.

包膜具有双重来源，既来自于宿主细胞也来自于病毒本身。 The envelope has a double origin, from both the host cell and the virus.

包膜主要由脂质、糖类和蛋白质组成。 The envelope is mainly composed of lipids, carbohydrates, and proteins.

包膜赋予病毒一定的脆弱性，使病毒对理化因素高度敏感，易受热失活。 The envelope makes viruses fragile and highly sensitive to physico-chemical factors, and heat-sensitive.

这一特性对流行病学和病毒诊断具有重要影响。 This has important implications for epidemiology and diagnosis.

老师补充说明
带包膜病毒在环境中生存能力较差，需要密切接触才能传播（如HIV、流感病毒），而无包膜病毒更耐环境，可经水、食物传播。 The teacher added: Enveloped viruses have poor environmental survival and require close contact to transmit (e.g. HIV, flu), while non-enveloped viruses are more resistant and can transmit via water or food.

二、包膜的来源 Origin of the Viral Envelope

病毒包膜来源于宿主细胞的多种细胞膜系统。 The viral envelope originates from various membrane systems of the host cell.

包膜可来自质膜，如流感病毒、狂犬病毒、HIV。 The envelope can stem from the plasma membrane, as in flu virus, rabies virus, and HIV.

包膜可来自细胞内膜结构，如高尔基体、内质网、胞内囊泡，如丙型肝炎病毒和冠状病毒。 The envelope may also originate from intracellular membranes, such as the Golgi apparatus, endoplasmic reticulum, and intracellular vesicles, as in hepatitis C virus and coronavirus.

有些病毒的包膜还可暂时包裹于核膜，如疱疹病毒。 For some viruses, the envelope transiently wraps from the nuclear membrane, as in herpesvirus.

老师强调，不同病毒通过不同膜系统获得包膜，这影响病毒装配、出芽及感染特性。 The teacher emphasized that the acquisition of the envelope from different membrane systems affects viral assembly, budding, and infection features.

三、病毒获得包膜的方式 Acquisition of the Viral Envelope

病毒通过出芽(budding)的方式从细胞膜或胞内膜系统获得包膜。 Viruses acquire their envelope by budding from cellular or internal membranes.

出芽过程中，衣壳逐渐包裹，由宿主膜折叠形成新的包膜结构。 During budding, the capsid becomes gradually surrounded as the host membrane folds to form the new envelope.

图像展示了包膜的获得过程，两种图示分别显示衣壳与质膜接触并“包裹”形成完整病毒颗粒。 The images show the acquiring process, with the capsid contacting and “wrapping” with the plasma membrane, forming a complete viral particle.

四、流感病毒的结构示例 Schematic Representation: Flu Virus

流感病毒带有包膜，该包膜为脂质双层。 The flu virus is enveloped, with a lipid bilayer membrane.

包膜内侧有M蛋白，包膜上有血凝素（hemagglutinin）和神经氨酸酶（neuraminidase）等糖蛋白。 The inner side of the membrane contains matrix (M) protein, and the envelope carries glycoproteins such as hemagglutinin and neuraminidase.

包裹内部为核衣壳，包含病毒RNA基因组。 The envelope encases the nucleocapsid containing the viral RNA genome.

五、艾滋病毒（HIV）结构示例 Schematic Representation: HIV

HIV的包膜外部有gp120与gp41等糖蛋白，参与病毒附着和入侵。 On the surface of the HIV envelope, glycoproteins gp120 and gp41 facilitate viral attachment and entry.

包膜包裹基质和截锥形对称衣壳，内部为病毒基因组。 The envelope wraps around the matrix and a truncated conical symmetrical capsid; inside is the viral genome.

六、老师讲课补充内容 Teacher's Additional Points

教师特别强调，包膜的存在导致病毒对外界环境敏感，因此包装、运输与诊断需注意保存温度，否则病毒很快失活。 The teacher highlighted that the envelope makes viruses sensitive to the environment, so correct storage temperature is essential for transport and diagnosis; otherwise, viruses lose infectivity quickly.

包膜病毒易受去污剂、乙醇等影响，常需通过血液、密切接触传播；无包膜病毒则更为稳定，可经消化道传播。 Enveloped viruses are easily inactivated by detergents and alcohol, thus usually transmitted via blood or close contact; non-enveloped viruses are more stable and can transmit via gastrointestinal routes.

病毒分类Virus Classification

一、病毒分类的基础与演变 Basis and Evolution of Virus Classification

病毒的传统分类长期基于以下几个方面
The traditional classification of viruses has long been based on:

宿主的种类 The nature of the host

细胞嗜性 The cellular tropism

致病性 The pathogenicity

传播方式 The mode of transmission

近年来，病毒分类由病毒分类国际委员会（ICTV）制定，1975年起实施。 In recent years, classification has been determined by the International Committee on Taxonomy of Viruses (ICTV) since 1975.

老师强调
现在病毒分类更侧重于结构和基因组等科学特征，而非单一传播或致病性等。 The teacher emphasized that now classification focuses on scientific characteristics like structure and genome, not merely transmission or pathogenicity.

二、ICTV标准与现代病毒分类标准 ICTV Standard and Modern Taxonomy Criteria

官方分类主要依据ICTV报告，该报告在线免费提供。 The official classification is mainly based on the ICTV report, which is freely available online.

ICTV分类主要参考三大标准
ICTV classification is mainly based on three standards:
1
病毒基因组类型及其组织方式 Type and organization of the viral genome
2
病毒复制策略 Strategy of viral replication
3
病毒粒子的结构 Structure of the virion
老师补充
标准条例不断修订，部分病毒家族和属划分会调整，保持更新非常重要。 The teacher added: Standards are constantly revised, so families and genera can move or be renamed. Keeping up-to-date is essential.
ICTV官网
https://ictv.global/taxonomy ICTV official website:  https://ictv.global/taxonomy

三、病毒的分级命名与例子 Taxonomic Levels and Naming with Examples

病毒分类体系主要包括以下五个级别
The virus taxonomy system mainly contains five levels:
1
目的 Order，后缀为 -virales，如 Reovirales Order, suffix "-virales", e.g., Reovirales
2
科 Family，后缀为 -viridae，如 Sedoreoviridae Family, suffix "-viridae", e.g., Sedoreoviridae
3
亚科 Subfamily，后缀为 -virinae，如 Sedoreovirinae Subfamily, suffix "-virinae", e.g., Sedoreovirinae
4
属 Genus，后缀为 -virus，如 Rotavirus Genus, suffix "-virus", e.g., Rotavirus
5
种 Species，直接使用病毒名，例如 Rotavirus A Species, known by the virus name, e.g., Rotavirus A

当前已知病毒包括约81个目、11个亚目、314个科、200个亚科、3522个属、14690个种。 There are currently approximately 81 orders, 11 suborders, 314 families, 200 subfamilies, 3,522 genera, and 14,690 species.

病毒分类体系持续进化和更新。 The virus classification system is constantly evolving and updating.

老师特别强调
使用学术、国际标准名称时一定要准确，尤其是家族、属和种的命名不要混淆。 The teacher specifically stressed: Use precise academic and international standard names, especially for family, genus, and species.

四、巴尔的摩分类法及人类感染病毒 Baltimore Classification and Human-Infecting Viruses

巴尔的摩（Baltimore）分类法依据病毒基因组的类型与复制策略，将病毒分为七类： Baltimore classification divides viruses into seven classes based on the genome type and replication strategy:

1
dsDNA 病毒Double-stranded DNA viruses
2
ssDNA 病毒Single-stranded DNA viruses
3
dsRNA 病毒Double-stranded RNA viruses
4
(+)ssRNA 病毒Positive-sense single-stranded RNA viruses
5
(-)ssRNA 病毒Negative-sense single-stranded RNA viruses
6
反转录单链RNA病毒Retro-transcribing single-stranded RNA viruses
7
反转录双链DNA病毒Retro-transcribing double-stranded DNA viruses

每种类型下有不同的病毒家族与属，涵盖感染人类的多种病毒。 Each class contains various families and genera of viruses infecting humans.

巴尔的摩分类法主要从基因组层面分析病毒的复制策略及感染机制。 The Baltimore classification emphasizes genome-level analysis of the virus replication strategy and infection mechanism.

老师补充说明
巴尔的摩分类为病毒学研究和文献查阅提供了统一、生物学意义强的系统。 The teacher added: The Baltimore system provides a unified and biologically meaningful organization for virology studies and literature referencing.

五、总结 Summary

病毒分类由传统浅显标准向分子与结构生物学特征转变，ICTV和Baltimore法是主要依据。 Virus classification has shifted from traditional, superficial criteria to molecular and structural biology features, mainly following ICTV and Baltimore systems.

分类细致而复杂，命名标准统一，保持最新、准确理解各级别是病毒学基础。 Classification is detailed and complex; unified nomenclature and up-to-date knowledge of taxonomic levels are fundamental in virology.

病毒增殖的阶段 The stages of viral multiplication

总览 Overview

病毒增殖分为启动期（Initiation phase）、蚀变期（Eclipse phase）和晚期（Late phase）。 Viral multiplication is divided into the initiation phase, eclipse phase, and late phase.
  • A启动期 Initiation phase

包括病毒吸附（Attachment）和侵入（Entry）。 Includes viral attachment and entry.

病毒首先通过包膜或胶体蛋白与宿主细胞表面受体结合。 The virus first binds to host cell surface receptors via envelope or capsid proteins.

吸附后，病毒通过细胞膜融合或胞吞作用进入细胞。 After attachment, the virus enters the cell via membrane fusion or endocytosis.

对于无包膜病毒，多通过胞吞进入；有包膜病毒可通过膜融合直接释放基因组。 Non-enveloped viruses usually enter via endocytosis, while enveloped viruses may fuse membranes to release their genome directly.

B. 蚀变期 Eclipse phase

病毒去衣壳（Decapsidation/Uncoating），释放遗传物质到细胞内。 The virus undergoes uncoating, releasing its genetic material into the cell.

此后进行复制（Replication）、转录（Transcription）、翻译（Translation）。 Then, replication, transcription, and translation take place.

病毒基因组复制依赖于病毒及宿主细胞的酶。 Viral genome replication depends on both viral and host enzymes.

病毒mRNA由病毒基因组转录产生，并由细胞核糖体翻译成蛋白质。 Viral mRNA is transcribed from the genome and translated into proteins by host ribosomes.

C. 晚期 Late phase

主要包括病毒的组装（Assembly/Maturation）和释放（Release）。 This phase mainly includes viral assembly/maturation and release.

病毒结构蛋白和基因组在细胞内自组装成新的病毒颗粒。 Viral structural proteins and genomes self-assemble into new virus particles.

病毒可通过细胞溶解（lysis）或出芽（budding）方式释放。 Viruses are released via cell lysis or budding.

有包膜病毒多通过出芽从细胞膜获得包膜，无包膜病毒常通过细胞裂解释放。 Enveloped viruses usually gain their envelope by budding from the cell membrane, while non-enveloped viruses are often released by cell lysis.

3
阶段关键点 Key Points of Each Stage

启动期——病毒进入细胞，但未开始复制。 Initiation phase—virus enters the cell but has not yet started replication.

蚀变期——病毒基因组活化，合成mRNA和蛋白，但细胞内尚未检测到新病毒。 Eclipse phase—the genome is active, mRNA and proteins are synthesized, but no new virus particles can be detected yet.

晚期——新病毒颗粒组装、成熟、释放。 Late phase—new virus particles assemble, mature, and are released.

4
老师补充讲解 Teacher’s Additional Explanation
老师强调
蚀变期检测不到完整病毒，新病毒的组装和释放标志着感染周期的最后阶段。 The teacher emphasized: no complete viruses can be detected during the eclipse phase; assembly and release of new viruses mark the final stage of the infection cycle.

不同阶段的理解有助于病毒学实验设计与抗病毒药物研究，例如蚀变期是抑制病毒增殖的关键窗口。 Understanding these phases helps virology experimental design and antiviral drug research; eclipse phase is a key window for inhibiting virus multiplication.

5
小结 Summary
病毒增殖顺序为
吸附与进入→脱壳→复制/转录/翻译→组装→释放。 The order of viral multiplication is: attachment and entry → uncoating → replication/transcription/translation → assembly → release.

每一步都涉及病毒与宿主密切相互作用，是病毒学和病毒干预的研究重点。 Each step involves close interaction between the virus and host; these are focal points for virology and antiviral strategies.

病毒结合与受体识别 Binding of the virus and Cellular Receptors

1
病毒结合是感染所必需的步骤。 Step required for infection.
2
病毒表面糖蛋白或蛋白质与细胞受体相互作用，启动感染过程。 Interactions between viral glycoproteins / proteins and cellular receptors initiate the infection process.
3
病毒蛋白被称为“配体”或“抗受体”。 Viral protein is called a "ligand" or "anti-receptor".
4
配体的完整性对病毒的结合至关重要。 Integrity of the ligand is essential for virus attachment.

突变和中和抗体可影响配体功能，阻断病毒入侵。 Mutations and neutralizing antibodies can impact ligand function and block viral entry.

病毒配体分类
Classification of viral ligands:

有包膜病毒的配体为糖蛋白。 Enveloped viruses use glycoproteins as ligands.

无包膜病毒的配体为蛋白质。 Non-enveloped viruses use proteins as ligands.

有包膜病毒的典型配体实例 Example: Ligand of enveloped viruses

6
流感病毒血凝素（Haemagglutinin, HA）是典型配体。 Flu virus haemagglutinin (HA) is a typical ligand.

HA是三聚体结构。 HA is a trimer.

HA2锚定在病毒包膜上。 HA2 is anchored in the viral envelope.

HA1含有唾液酸结合位点（识别细胞受体）。 HA1 contains the sialic acid fixation site (recognizes cell receptor).

HA1与HA2通过二硫键连接。 HA1 and HA2 are linked by a disulfide bond.

无包膜病毒的典型配体实例 Example: Ligand of non-enveloped viruses

7
腺病毒的纤维蛋白是其主要配体。 Adenovirus fibre protein is its major ligand.

配体位于腺病毒的五聚体顶端。 The fibre is located at the top of the pentons of adenovirus.

末端按钮用于与细胞受体CAR（柯萨奇病毒和腺病毒受体）结合。 Terminal button serves as the fixation site for the cell receptor CAR (Coxsackievirus and Adenovirus Receptor).

细胞受体特点 Characteristics of cellular receptors

8
细胞受体的本质多样，可为蛋白、糖蛋白（或糖基化残基）、糖脂。 Nature of cellular receptors includes proteins, glycoproteins (or glycosylated residues), and glycolipids.
9
表面分子多为细胞生理功能重要成分，如黏附分子、激素受体、酶等。 Cell surface molecules are often essential for cellular life, such as adhesion molecules, hormone receptors, and enzymes.
10
每个细胞表面有500到50万个受体。 Each cell has 500 to 500,000 receptors on its surface.
11
受体分布既有均匀也可局部化。 Distribution of receptors can be homogeneous or localized.
12
目前已知的病毒受体约有100种，但已知病毒有数千种。 About 100 known cellular receptors have been identified for thousands of known viruses.

老师补充讲解 Teacher’s additional points

13
病毒配体发生突变可能改变其对受体的亲和性，可影响传播力，也让某些抗体失去作用。 Mutations in viral ligands can alter their affinity for receptors, affecting infectivity or antibody recognition.
14
疫苗的一个重要作用即产生中和抗体，阻止病毒配体与受体结合，从而阻碍感染。 A key role of vaccines is to induce neutralizing antibodies that block ligand-receptor interactions and prevent infection.
15
病毒常选择细胞生理必需的分子作为受体，因这些受体难以缺失，更利于长期传播。 Viruses often use essential cellular receptors because they are less likely to be lost during evolution, facilitating long-term viral persistence and transmission.

细胞表面糖蛋白 Cell surface glycoproteins

1
病毒利用宿主细胞表面的不同糖蛋白作为受体，实现对细胞的识别和侵入。 Viruses use different glycoproteins on the host cell surface as receptors to recognize and enter cells.
2
图示列举了多种常见病毒对应的宿主细胞受体。 The diagram lists various viruses and their corresponding host cell receptors.

主要受体及对应病毒 Main receptors and corresponding viruses

CD4
HIV-I的主要受体。 CD4: Main receptor for HIV-I.

趋化因子受体（CC和CXC）：HIV-I结合的辅助受体。 Chemokine receptors (CC and CXC): Co-receptors for HIV-I.

PVR（脊髓灰质炎病毒受体）：脊髓灰质炎病毒的受体。 PVR (Poliovirus receptor): Receptor for poliovirus.

CAR（柯萨奇病毒和腺病毒受体）：柯萨奇B病毒及腺病毒2和5型的受体。 CAR (Coxsackie virus and adenovirus receptor): Receptor for Coxsackie B and adenovirus 2&5.

Integrin αvβ3/5
参与腺病毒等病毒的结合。 Integrin αvβ3/5: Participates in adenovirus attachment.
阳离子氨基酸转运蛋白
用于Murine leukemia virus结合。 Cationic amino acid transporter: Used by murine leukemia virus.
氨肽酶N
人冠状病毒的受体。 Aminopeptidase N: Receptor for human coronavirus.
CD46
麻疹病毒的受体。 CD46: Receptor for measles virus.

LDL受体（低密度脂蛋白受体）：某些鼻病毒的受体。 LDL receptor (Low density lipoprotein receptor): Receptor for some rhinoviruses.

表格标签解释 Table legend explanation

PVR = Poliovirus receptor

CAR = Coxsackie virus and adenovirus receptor

LDL = Low density lipoprotein

讲解补充 Additional explanation（结合课堂重点）

1
一个病毒的特异性（嗜性）由其识别的受体决定。 The specificity (tropism) of a virus is determined by the receptor it recognizes.
2
这些受体都是细胞自身生命活动的重要分子。 These receptors are essential molecules for cell life activities.
3
病毒经进化倾向于利用这些不易缺失的关键分子作为受体，提高自己的生存和传播概率。 Viruses tend to use these indispensable key molecules as receptors, increasing their chances of survival and transmission.
4
利用不同受体解释了病毒组织和宿主特异性，比如HIV主要感染T淋巴细胞，脊髓灰质炎病毒主要感染肠道上皮细胞等。 The use of different receptors explains viral tissue and host specificity, e.g., HIV infects T lymphocytes; poliovirus infects intestinal epithelial cells.
5
某些病毒可利用多个受体进入同一种或多种细胞，增加了感染的广泛性。 Some viruses can use multiple receptors to enter one or more cell types, broadening their infectivity.

小结 Summary

细胞表面糖蛋白作为病毒受体，决定病毒感染的细胞类型和范围，是病毒致病和防控的重要靶点。 Cell surface glycoproteins serve as viral receptors, determining the cell types and range a virus can infect; they are critical targets for viral pathogenesis and prevention.

细胞特异性与病毒穿入机制 Cell specificity and penetration mechanisms of viruses

1
细胞特异性 Cell specificity

某些受体只存在于有限类型的细胞上。 Some receptors are present only on a limited number of cell types.

细胞嗜性概念，决定病毒能感染哪些细胞。 The concept of cellular tropism determines which cells a virus can infect.

例：HIV 只感染表面表达CD4受体的T淋巴细胞。 Example: HIV infects only T lymphocytes expressing CD4.

有些受体在多种细胞中广泛分布。 Some receptors are widely distributed in multiple cell types.

例：唾液酸、PGHS（多糖肝素硫酸酯），使病毒能感染多种细胞。 Example: Sialic acid, PGHS (ProteoGlycane Heparin Sulfate) allow viruses to infect several cell types.

同一种病毒可利用不同受体进入细胞。 The same virus may use different receptors to enter cells.

例：疱疹病毒可结合不同的受体实现广泛感染。 Example: Herpesvirus uses different receptors for broad infectivity.

有些病毒利用多种受体和/或辅受体。 Some viruses use multiple receptors and/or co-receptors.

例：HIV既需CD4主受体，又需CCR5/CXCR4等辅助受体。 Example: HIV uses CD4 as a main receptor and CCR5/CXCR4 as co-receptors.

讲解补充
病毒细胞特异性由其表面配体与受体的结合决定，这也是病毒组织嗜性和宿主范围的分子基础。 Teacher’s add: Viral cell specificity is determined by interaction between viral ligands and cell receptors, underlying viral tropism and host range.
2
病毒穿入机制 Penetration of the viruses

病毒进入细胞常见有两种机制。 There are two main mechanisms for virus entry into cells.

内吞作用适用于有包膜和无包膜病毒。 Endocytosis applies to both enveloped and non-enveloped viruses.

内吞指病毒与细胞表面结合后，细胞膜包裹病毒形成囊泡内吞到胞质内。 Endocytosis means that after binding to cell surface, the membrane engulfs the virus into a vesicle.

融合仅限于有包膜病毒。 Fusion occurs only for enveloped viruses.

病毒包膜与细胞膜融合，使病毒基因组直接释放到胞质中。 Viral envelope fuses with the cell membrane, releasing the genome into the cytoplasm.

有争议的转位（Translocation）机制目前仅个别报道，不主流。 The translocation mechanism is controversial and not mainstream, with only rare reports.

讲解补充
病毒进入后，融合或溶解会促使基因组释放到胞质，开始后续复制。 Teacher’s add: After entry, fusion or lysis helps release the viral genome into the cytoplasm, where replication follows.
3
病毒内吞过程的实例与机制 Examples and mechanisms of endocytosis

以Alphavirus为例，电子显微镜下可见病毒囊泡化内吞过程。 Example: Alphavirus shows vesicle-mediated endocytosis under electron microscopy.

经典的内吞机制为包被囊泡（clathrin/涎泡）介导：病毒首先附着于细胞受体，随后细胞膜向内凹陷，形成被膜小窝（coated pit），最终形成包被囊泡，将病毒带入胞内。 Classic mechanism: clathrin-mediated (coated pit) endocytosis, where virus first binds to the receptor, induces membrane invagination, forms a coated pit, and then is internalized in a coated vesicle.

囊泡内病毒会到达早期或晚期内吞体，后由融合、裂解或膜通透化方式将病毒基因组释放到胞质。 Virus in vesicles is transported to early or late endosomes, then genome is released into cytoplasm by fusion, lysis, or permeabilization.

4
主要内吞类型及对应病毒 Main types of endocytosis and examples
包被蛋白介导内吞典型代表病毒
腺病毒（Adenoviridae），黄病毒（Flaviviridae），正黏病毒（Orthomyxoviridae，如流感病毒），乳头瘤病毒（Papillomaviridae）、多瘤病毒（Polyomaviridae）、逆转录病毒（Retroviridae）。 Clathrin-mediated endocytosis: e.g. Adenoviridae (adenovirus), Flaviviridae (dengue/HCV), Orthomyxoviridae (influenza virus), Papillomaviridae (HPV 16), Polyomaviridae (JC virus), Retroviridae.
穴饮蛋白介导内吞代表病毒
HPV 31，肠道病毒和脊髓灰质炎病毒（Picornaviridae），乙肝病毒（Hepadnaviridae）。 Caveolin-mediated endocytosis: e.g. Papillomaviridae (HPV 31), Picornaviridae (enterovirus, poliovirus), Hepadnaviridae (HBV).

每种内吞方式对应特定膜结构和蛋白的参与，决定病毒入胞效率和感染部位。 Each endocytic pathway involves specific membrane structures/proteins, determining viral entry efficiency and infection sites.

5
小结 Summary

受体分布决定病毒的细胞和组织特异性。 Receptor distribution determines viral cell and tissue specificity.

病毒入胞主要依赖内吞或膜融合，不同病毒选择不同途径。 Viral entry mainly relies on endocytosis or membrane fusion, with different viruses choosing different routes.

深入理解病毒入侵机制是病毒学研究和抗病毒策略设计的关键。 In-depth understanding of entry mechanisms is crucial for virology research and antiviral strategy design.

非网格蛋白和非穴饮蛋白介导的内吞作用 Non-clathrin and non-caveolin endocytosis

1
某些病毒可以通过不依赖网格蛋白（clathrin）或穴饮蛋白（caveolin）的内吞途径进入细胞。 Some viruses can enter cells via endocytic pathways independent of clathrin or caveolin.
2
病毒结合并进入早期内吞体，随后到达晚期内吞体（pH约6.0）。 Virus binds and enters early endosome, then moves to late endosome (pH about 6.0).
3
有包膜病毒在内吞体膜处发生融合，释放病毒基因组到胞质。 Enveloped viruses undergo fusion at the endosomal membrane, releasing their genome into the cytoplasm.
4
无包膜病毒则通过内吞体溶解或膜通透作用释放基因组。 Non-enveloped viruses release their genome by lysis or permeabilization of the endosome membrane.
代表性病毒家族及种类
Representative families and viruses:

Arenaviridae（如 Lassa 病毒等） Arenaviridae (e.g., Lassa virus)

Picornaviridae（如肠道病毒、脊髓灰质炎病毒） Picornaviridae (e.g., enterovirus, poliovirus)

Sedoreoviridae（如轮状病毒） Sedoreoviridae (e.g., rotavirus)

巨胞饮作用
内吞作用的一种形式 Macropinocytosis, a form of endocytosis
6
巨胞饮作用是依赖于细胞膜突出，由肌动蛋白驱动，形成较大吞噬泡将病毒包裹入胞的内吞途径。 Macropinocytosis relies on plasma membrane protrusion driven by actin filaments, forming large vesicles to engulf viruses.
7
病毒吸附于细胞表面，可激活细胞信号通路，推动膜褶皱并内吞病毒。 Virus attaches to the surface, activates signaling, drives membrane ruffling and internalization.
8
病毒被包裹进巨胞饮小泡，随后进入晚期巨胞饮体，最后进入晚期内吞体（pH 6.0-5.0）。 Viruses are encapsulated in macropinosomes, then late macropinosomes, and finally late endosomes (pH 6.0–5.0).
9
有包膜病毒可在内吞体与膜融合释放基因组，无包膜病毒通过膜通透等方式释放基因组。 Enveloped viruses release their genome by fusion at endosomal membranes; non-enveloped viruses do so via permeabilization or lysis.
代表性病毒家族及种类
Representative families and viruses:

Poxviridae（如牛痘病毒） Poxviridae (e.g., vaccinia virus)

Herpesviridae（如HHV-8） Herpesviridae (e.g., HHV-8)

Filoviridae（如埃博拉病毒） Filoviridae (e.g., Ebola virus)

补充说明 Teacher’s add:

11
不同病毒可根据其结构和传播策略选择不同的入胞途径，提高感染效率和细胞范围。 Different viruses select specific entry pathways based on structure and transmission strategy to enhance infection efficiency and cell range.
12
病毒基因组最终必须进入细胞质，启动复制周期。 The viral genome must ultimately reach the cytoplasm to begin the replication cycle.

小结 Summary

病毒非经典内吞和巨胞饮机制有助于扩展宿主细胞类型和感染能力，是病毒学及抗病毒策略研究的重要内容。 Non-classical endocytosis and macropinocytosis expand viral cell tropism and infectivity, vital for virology research and antiviral strategies.

胞吞体释放与膜融合机制Exit from the endosome and fusion mechanisms

1
胞吞体释放 Exit from the endosome
有包膜病毒
通过在胞吞体膜上的融合实现释放。 Enveloped viruses: Release via fusion at the endosome membrane.
无包膜病毒
通过胞吞体溶解或膜通透化过程释放。 Non-enveloped viruses: Release by lysis of the endosome or permeabilization process.
2
有包膜病毒在胞吞体膜的融合过程 Fusion at the endosome membrane for enveloped viruses

病毒首先吸附在细胞表面并被内吞进入早期胞吞体。 Virus first attaches to the cell surface and is endocytosed into the early endosome.

随着胞吞体成熟，pH降低（如晚期胞吞体pH约6.0）。 As the endosome matures, pH decreases (late endosome pH ~6.0).

低pH诱导病毒包膜与胞吞体膜发生融合，病毒基因组释放到胞质中。 Low pH induces fusion of the viral envelope with the endosome membrane, releasing the viral genome into the cytoplasm.

有包膜病毒融合机制实例
流感病毒 Example: Enveloped virus (Flu virus)

在中性pH下，HA2的融合肽处于折叠状态，不暴露在外。 At neutral pH, the fusion peptide of HA2 is folded and not exposed.

在酸性pH下（胞吞体环境），α螺旋结构延长，融合肽暴露，启动膜融合。 At acidic pH (endosomal environment), the alpha-helical structure extends, exposing the fusion peptide and initiating membrane fusion.

这种构象变化是病毒释放基因组到胞质的关键。 This conformational change is crucial for viral genome release into the cytoplasm.

无包膜病毒示例
腺病毒进出机制 Example: Non-enveloped virus (Adenovirus) entry and exit
受体识别与内吞机制
Receptor recognition and endocytosis:

首先，腺病毒纤维与CAR受体结合。 First, the fiber of adenovirus binds to the CAR receptor.

接着，五聚体基底与整合素结合，促进内吞。 Then, the penton base binds integrins to promote internalization.

细胞膜内陷，病毒被包裹并形成囊泡进入胞内。 The plasma membrane invaginates, forming a vesicle to internalize the virus.

进入胞吞体后，随着pH降低，在晚期胞吞体内发生溶解，病毒颗粒逃逸到胞质中。 After entering the endosome, as pH drops, there is lysis in the late endosome, and the viral particle escapes into the cytoplasm.

5
仅有包膜病毒可通过膜融合方式直接入胞 Only enveloped viruses can enter by membrane fusion

病毒包膜与细胞质膜融合，使核衣壳直接穿入胞质。 Viral envelope fuses with cellular plasma membrane, allowing nucleocapsid direct entry to cytoplasm.

融合肽在生理pH下即处于活化状态。 The fusion peptide is active at physiological pH.

代表性病毒
HIV、疱疹病毒。 Examples: HIV, herpesvirus.
6
膜融合入胞步骤简图（如HIV、疱疹病毒） Schematic of fusion entry (e.g., HIV, herpesvirus)

病毒先与受体结合，随后病毒包膜与宿主膜融合，内容物释放到胞质。 First, virus binds the receptor. Then, viral and host membranes fuse, and viral content is released into the cytoplasm.

补充讲解 Supplement from lecture

病毒能否通过融合释放取决于包膜与融合肽结构，以及环境pH。 The ability of viruses to use fusion for release depends on the envelope, fusion peptide structure, and environmental pH.

流感病毒等需要酸性激活，HIV等可在生理pH下融合。 Influenza virus requires acidic activation; HIV can fuse at physiological pH.

对于无包膜病毒，主要通过改变胞吞体膜通透性或诱导囊泡溶解实现释放。 For non-enveloped viruses, release is mainly through alteration of endosomal membrane permeability or lysis.

小结 Summary

有包膜病毒可在胞吞体融合或直接膜融合释放基因组，无包膜病毒依赖溶解或膜通透过程。 Enveloped viruses release genome via endosomal or plasma membrane fusion, non-enveloped viruses rely on lysis or permeabilization.

病毒入胞方式与结构紧密相关，是病毒学重要考点。 Entry pathways are closely related to viral structure, an important aspect in virology.

HIV入胞机制Mechanism of HIV entry

pH依赖与非依赖病毒穿透方式pH dependent/independent penetration

核衣壳胞内运输Intracellular transport of nucleocapsids

去衣壳（脱壳）Decapsidation (Uncoating)

1
HIV入胞机制Mechanism of HIV entry

HIV首先通过gp120与靶细胞表面CD4受体结合。 HIV first binds to the CD4 receptor on the target cell surface via gp120.

gp120结合后，暴露协同受体（CoR）的结合位点。 After gp120 binding, the binding site for the coreceptor is exposed.

病毒与协同受体（如CCR5或CXCR4）结合，诱导进一步结构变化。 The virus binds to the coreceptor (such as CCR5 or CXCR4), inducing further conformational changes.

gp41的融合肽插入宿主细胞膜，实现膜接近。 The fusion peptide of gp41 inserts into the host cell membrane, bringing the membranes closer.

形成发夹结构，最终实现病毒膜与细胞膜融合，核衣壳进入胞质。 Hairpin structure forms, leading to fusion of viral and cellular membranes and entry of the nucleocapsid into the cytoplasm.

该过程无需低pH环境，可在生理pH下完成。 This process does not require a low pH environment and occurs at physiological pH.

2
pH依赖与非依赖病毒穿透方式pH dependent/independent penetration
酸性pH依赖穿透
如流感病毒（Orthomyxovirus）、狂犬病毒（Rhabdovirus）、埃博拉病毒（Filovirus）、腺病毒（Adenovirus）、部分鼻病毒（Picornavirus）。 Acid pH dependent penetration: e.g., Orthomyxovirus (Flu A and B), Rhabdovirus (Rabies virus), Filovirus (Ebola virus), Adenovirus, some Picornavirus (Rhinovirus).
pH非依赖穿透
如大多数逆转录病毒（HIV）、单纯疱疹病毒（Herpesvirus HSV-1）、部分脊髓灰质炎病毒（Picornavirus Poliovirus）、呼肠孤病毒（Reovirus）、副黏病毒（Paramyxovirus 麻疹病毒）。 pH independent penetration: e.g., most Retrovirus (HIV), Herpesvirus (HSV-1), some Picornavirus (Poliovirus), Reovirus, Paramyxovirus (Measles virus).

pH依赖与否决定穿膜融合发生在细胞表面还是胞内。 Whether pH is required determines if fusion occurs at the plasma membrane or within the cell.

3
核衣壳胞内运输Intracellular transport of nucleocapsids

病毒核衣壳进入胞质后，可通过微管和肌动蛋白丝运输至细胞核或复制部位。 After the nucleocapsid enters the cytoplasm, it is transported to the nucleus or replication sites via microtubules and actin filaments.

一些病毒（如HSV、腺病毒）运用微管驱动定向运输。 Some viruses (e.g., Herpesvirus, Adenovirus) use microtubule-driven directional transport.

小型病毒可通过自由扩散到达靶向位置，而大病毒常需借助胞内骨架系统。 Small viruses may diffuse freely to their destination, while large viruses require the cytoskeletal system for transport.

胞内转运的效率和方式受病毒大小与结构影响。 The efficiency and mode of intracellular transport depend on virus size and structure.

4
去衣壳（脱壳）Decapsidation (Uncoating)

去衣壳指病毒基因组从蛋白衣壳中释放出来。 Decapsidation refers to the release of the viral genome from the protein capsid.

发生时机可因病毒不同而异，有些发生于胞内转运早期，有些在核膜附近。 The timing varies between viruses; for some, it occurs early after entry, for others near the nuclear membrane.

有些病毒（如腺病毒）去衣壳包含多个分步过程，需要与胞内环境及酶相互作用。 For some viruses (e.g., Adenovirus), uncoating involves several steps and interaction with host enzymes.

去衣壳过程可为完全（基因组全部释放）或部分（如轮状病毒保留部分衣壳）。 The process can be total (full genome release) or partial (e.g., rotavirus retains part of the capsid).

细胞酶常参与去衣壳过程，并能影响病毒复制效率。 Cellular enzymes are often involved and may affect the efficiency of viral replication.

小结 Summary

HIV等病毒通过特殊膜融合机制入胞，分子事件受结构和pH影响。 Viruses like HIV enter cells via specialized membrane fusion mechanisms, with molecular events influenced by structure and pH.

病毒核衣壳进胞后需胞内定位和进一步去衣壳，方可启动复制周期。 After entry, nucleocapsids require intracellular targeting and further uncoating to initiate replication.

病毒增殖和转录/翻译策略Viral multiplication and Transcription/Translation strategies

问1. 病毒增殖需完成的步骤 What does the virus have to do to multiply?
答

病毒必须转录病毒基因组的信息，生成mRNA。 The virus must transcribe its genome information to produce mRNA.

病毒必须翻译这些mRNA，合成蛋白质。 The virus must translate these mRNAs to synthesize proteins.

结构蛋白：衣壳、包膜。 Structural proteins: capsid, envelope.

非结构蛋白：酶、病毒和细胞代谢调控蛋白。 Non-structural proteins: enzymes, virus and cellular metabolism regulatory proteins.

病毒必须复制自身的基因组，以便产生新的病毒颗粒。 The virus must replicate its genome to produce new viral particles.

2
宿主细胞施加的限制 Constraints imposed by the cell

病毒基因组必须包含所有合成病毒蛋白所需的信息。 The viral genome must provide all the information necessary for the synthesis of viral proteins.

病毒相关的合成过程完全依赖于宿主细胞的翻译系统。 The synthesis is entirely carried out by the cellular translation machinery.

病毒遗传信息需满足真核细胞翻译系统的要求。 Viral information must conform to the translation in eukaryotes.

3
转录策略 Transcription strategies

DNA病毒可直接用自身或宿主机制合成mRNA。 DNA viruses can directly use their own or host machinery to synthesize mRNA.

例：小DNA病毒如Parvoviridae等，部分双链DNA病毒如Papovaviridae、Adenoviridae、Herpesviridae、Poxviridae。 For example: small DNA viruses such as Parvoviridae, and some dsDNA viruses such as Papovaviridae, Adenoviridae, Herpesviridae, Poxviridae.

(+)RNA病毒的正链RNA可直接用作mRNA，无需转录。 Positive-strand RNA viruses [(+)RNA] can use their RNA directly as mRNA without transcription.

例：Picornaviridae、Caliciviridae、Togaviridae、Flaviviridae、Coronaviridae。 For example: Picornaviridae, Caliciviridae, Togaviridae, Flaviviridae, Coronaviridae.

dsRNA病毒需通过转录生成mRNA。 Double-stranded RNA (dsRNA) viruses require transcription to produce mRNA.

例：Sedoreoviridae。 For example: Sedoreoviridae.

(-)RNA病毒必须携带或编码RNA依赖型RNA聚合酶，将负链转录为mRNA。 Negative-strand RNA viruses [(-)RNA] must carry or encode RNA-dependent RNA polymerase to transcribe the negative strand to mRNA.

例：Orthomyxoviridae、Paramyxoviridae、Rhabdoviridae、Arenaviridae、Bunyaviridae。 For example: Orthomyxoviridae, Paramyxoviridae, Rhabdoviridae, Arenaviridae, Bunyaviridae.

反转录病毒通过逆转录将(+)RNA转变为cDNA，然后整合到宿主基因组，随后产生mRNA。 Retroviruses reverse transcribe their (+)RNA to cDNA, which integrates into the host genome and then produces mRNA.

4
翻译策略 Translation strategies

真核细胞只识别单顺反子mRNA，即一个mRNA只编码一条多肽链。 Eukaryotic cells only recognize monocistronic mRNA, i.e., one mRNA encodes one polypeptide.

多种病毒发展出不同策略解决此问题： Several strategies have been developed by viruses to address this:

单顺反子mRNA：一个mRNA生成一个蛋白。 Monocistronic mRNA: one mRNA makes one protein.

多顺反子mRNA：翻译得到一个大前体蛋白，然后由病毒或细胞蛋白酶切割成多个蛋白。 Polycistronic mRNA: translation produces a large precursor protein that is cleaved by viral or cellular proteases into multiple proteins.

剪接：通过RNA剪接机制产生多个不同的mRNA。 Splicing: generates several different mRNAs through splicing.

5
课堂补充讲解（录音精要） Key points from lecture

病毒必须高效利用极其有限的基因组空间，因此如多顺反子和剪接等机制十分重要。 Viruses must use extremely limited genome space efficiently, so mechanisms such as polycistronic mRNA and splicing are vital.

负链RNA病毒必须自带转录酶，因为宿主细胞内没有将RNA转录为RNA的酶。 Negative-strand RNA viruses must carry their own transcriptase because host cells do not have enzymes that transcribe RNA from RNA.

病毒的mRNA结构，包括帽子结构和多聚腺苷酸尾巴，是被宿主翻译系统识别的关键。 Structural features of viral mRNA, such as cap structure and poly-A tail, are essential for recognition by the host translation system.

病毒复制、蛋白表达、颗粒组装等基本过程，全部建立在对宿主细胞生物化学的精准适应基础之上。 Viral replication, protein expression, and particle assembly all rely on precise adaptation to host cell biochemistry.

小结 Summary

病毒繁殖依赖转录、翻译和基因组复制，所有环节均需适应宿主细胞内环境和机制。 Viral multiplication depends on transcription, translation, and genome replication, all requiring adaptation to the host cell environment and mechanisms.

病毒通过巧妙基因表达策略突破真核细胞只识别单顺反子mRNA的限制，实现复杂蛋白表达。 Viruses employ ingenious expression strategies to overcome the eukaryotic restriction on monocistronic mRNA, enabling complex protein expression.

DNA病毒的复制与腺病毒和AAV的增殖周期Replication of DNA viruses & Multiplication cycle of Adenovirus and AAV

1
DNA病毒的复制过程Replication of DNA viruses

复制分为早期阶段、DNA复制阶段和晚期阶段。 Replication is divided into early phase, DNA replication phase, and late phase.

早期阶段 Early phase

病毒DNA利用宿主细胞的RNA聚合酶，转录生成早期mRNA。 Viral DNA uses host cell RNA polymerase to transcribe early mRNA.

早期mRNA在细胞质中被翻译成早期蛋白，这些多为酶类（非结构蛋白）。 Early mRNA is translated in the cytoplasm into early proteins, mainly enzymes (non-structural proteins).

这些酶类蛋白介导随后病毒DNA的复制。 These enzymatic proteins mediate subsequent viral DNA replication.

DNA复制阶段 DNA replication phase

早期蛋白（如病毒DNA聚合酶）促进病毒DNA的复制，生成多个病毒基因组副本。 Early proteins (such as viral DNA polymerase) promote replication of viral DNA, generating multiple copies of the viral genome.

晚期阶段 Late phase

病毒DNA再次利用宿主RNA聚合酶转录生成晚期mRNA。 Viral DNA again uses host RNA polymerase to transcribe late mRNA.

晚期mRNA在胞质中翻译成晚期蛋白，这些为结构蛋白。 Late mRNA is translated in the cytoplasm into late proteins, which are structural proteins.

结构蛋白+新复制的DNA基因组共同组装成新病毒颗粒，随后释放。 Structural proteins and newly replicated viral DNA assemble into new virions, which are then released.

2
腺病毒的增殖周期Multiplication cycle of Adenovirus

腺病毒的复制同样分为早期和晚期阶段。 The adenovirus replication cycle likewise has early and late phases.

早期阶段 Early phase

入胞后病毒基因组到达细胞核，先转录E1A、E1B和E2等早期基因。 After entry, the viral genome enters the nucleus, and early genes like E1A, E1B, and E2 are transcribed first.

这些早期蛋白调控细胞周期、促进病毒基因表达和基因组复制。 Early proteins regulate the cell cycle, promote viral gene expression, and viral genome replication.

随后转录E3和E4基因，这些主要参与病毒免疫逃逸。 Next, E3 and E4 genes are transcribed, mainly involved in immune evasion.

DNA复制 DNA replication

在早期蛋白帮助下，病毒DNA进行大量复制。 With the help of early proteins, the viral DNA undergoes massive replication.

晚期阶段 Late phase

复制后转录晚期基因，生成结构蛋白。 After replication, late genes are transcribed to produce structural proteins.

结构蛋白与新基因组组装成病毒颗粒。 Structural proteins assemble with new genomes to form virions.

新病毒颗粒经包装，最后释放出细胞。 New viral particles are packaged and finally released from the cell.

课上强调：病毒复制流程高度时序化，早期基因主要调控、晚期基因主要编码结构蛋白。 In the lecture, emphasis was placed on the strict timing of viral replication: early genes mainly regulate, late genes mainly encode structural proteins.

3
AAV的增殖模式Multiplication of AAV (Adeno-Associated Viruses)

AAV本身不具备独立增殖能力，需辅助病毒如腺病毒协助。 AAV cannot replicate independently and needs a helper virus such as adenovirus for assistance.

溶原周期 Lysogenic cycle

在无辅助病毒时，AAV基因组可整合到宿主染色体（多为19号染色体的AAVS1位点）。 Without a helper virus, the AAV genome can integrate into the host chromosome, often at the AAVS1 site on chromosome 19.

集成状态下病毒处于潜伏状态，不表达不增殖。 In the integrated state, the virus is latent, with no expression or multiplication.

溶菌周期 Lytic cycle

当有辅助病毒感染时，AAV可被激活，转入溶菌周期。 When a helper virus infects the cell, AAV can be activated and enter the lytic cycle.

病毒基因组复制并包装成新病毒颗粒，最后释放。 The viral genome replicates, is packaged into new virions, and finally released.

如果AAV已整合，还可以“救援”出来并复制。 If AAV is already integrated, it can be rescued from the host genome and replicate.

小结 Summary

DNA病毒复制有严格的时序分工，早期主控调节，晚期主控装配。 DNA virus replication has strict temporal division: early for regulation, late for assembly.

腺病毒和AAV是研究真核病毒增殖机制的重要模型。 Adenovirus and AAV are important models for studying eukaryotic viral multiplication mechanisms.

AAV有独特的溶原与溶菌双周期，需辅助病毒激活复制。 AAV uniquely alternates between lysogenic and lytic cycles, requiring a helper virus for productive replication.

RNA病毒的复制与Hepatitis C病毒和HIV的增殖周期Replication of RNA viruses & Multiplication cycle of Hepatitis C virus and HIV

1
RNA病毒的复制基本特征 General features of RNA virus replication

单链基因组（除呼肠孤病毒） Single-stranded genome (except Reovirus)

RNA病毒绝大多数基因组为单链，仅Reovirus为例外。 Most RNA viruses have single-stranded genomes, with Reovirus as an exception.

单链RNA的极性 Polarity of single-stranded RNAs

正链（+）RNA类似细胞mRNA，可直接翻译为蛋白。 Positive-strand (+) RNA is similar to cellular mRNA and can be directly translated into protein.

负链（-）RNA需病毒自带的RNA依赖型RNA聚合酶转录生成+链。 Negative-strand (–) RNA needs a viral RNA-dependent RNA polymerase to transcribe to positive-strand RNA.

反转录病毒（如HIV）携带逆转录酶，将+RNA逆转录为DNA，继而整合入宿主基因组。 Retroviruses (e.g., HIV) have reverse transcriptase, which transcribes +RNA into DNA, which is then integrated into the host genome.

绝大部分RNA病毒在胞质内复制、增殖（正黏病毒科除外，需核内阶段）。 Most RNA viruses multiply in the cytoplasm, except Orthomyxoviridae which require a nuclear phase.

2
正链RNA病毒的复制 Replication of (+)RNA viruses

基因组的双重作用 Two roles for the genomic RNA

基因组RNA既可以直接作为mRNA翻译蛋白， Genomic RNA can serve directly as mRNA for protein translation,

也作为模板合成互补的负链RNA，用于自身复制。 And also as a template for the synthesis of complementary (–)RNA for genome replication.

病毒RNA聚合酶的合成 Synthesis of viral RNA polymerase

正链病毒首先翻译病毒RNA聚合酶，用于后续复制过程。 Positive-strand viruses first translate their RNA polymerase for the subsequent replication process.

新合成正链RNA的两种命运 Two roles for the newly synthesized (+)RNA

一部分用于继续翻译蛋白，一部分作为新的病毒基因组装入衣壳。 Some is used for more protein synthesis, and some is packaged into new virions as the genomic RNA.

3
正链RNA病毒细胞内复制图解 Diagram of intracellular (+)RNA virus replication

病毒正链RNA进入细胞后，直接被翻译（见图）。 After entry, the viral (+)RNA is immediately translated (see diagram).

随后，病毒聚合酶复制出一条负链RNA（dsRNA复制中间体），再以此为模板合成更多正链RNA。 Then, the viral polymerase synthesizes a complementary (–)RNA (dsRNA intermediate), which serves as a template for more (+)RNA synthesis.

新合成的正链RNA既可直接参与翻译，也可被包装成新病毒颗粒。 Newly synthesized (+)RNA can be translated or packaged into new virions.

4
丙型肝炎病毒增殖周期 Multiplication cycle of the hepatitis C virus

病毒首先吸附（Attachment）并内吞（Endocytosis）入胞。 The virus first attaches to the cell and enters by endocytosis.

衣壳后，释放出正链RNA并发生翻译。 After uncoating, the (+)RNA is released and translated.

RNA在胞质内复制，形成新的基因组。 The RNA is replicated in the cytoplasm, creating new genomes.

病毒蛋白和新基因组组装为新颗粒。 Viral proteins and new genomes are assembled into new particles.

最终通过细胞分泌途径释放。 Finally, new virions are released by the secretory pathway.

5
HIV的增殖周期 Multiplication cycle of HIV

HIV通过CD4及协同受体（CCR5/CXCR4）介导入胞。 HIV enters cells via CD4 and a coreceptor (CCR5 or CXCR4).

入胞后，由逆转录酶将+RNA逆转录为DNA。 After entry, reverse transcriptase converts +RNA into DNA.

病毒前整合复合体（PIC）携带DNA进入细胞核。 The pre-integration complex (PIC) transports the viral DNA into the nucleus.

DNA整合酶将病毒DNA整合至宿主基因组。 The viral integrase integrates the DNA into the host genome.

宿主转录系统转录病毒DNA产生mRNA和新基因组RNA。 Host transcription machinery transcribes viral DNA into mRNA and new genomic RNA.

mRNA用于翻译病毒蛋白；新+RNA与蛋白组装并出芽形成新病毒。 mRNA is translated into viral proteins; new +RNA is packaged with proteins and buds off as new viral particles.

新病毒在宿主膜出芽后经历成熟过程获得感染性。 Newly formed virus particles undergo maturation after budding to become infectious.

小结 Summary

RNA病毒主要有单链基因组，复制与基因组极性密切相关。 RNA viruses mostly possess single-stranded genomes, and replication depends heavily on genome polarity.

正链RNA可直接翻译，负链RNA需转录为正链；反转录病毒经逆转录酶介导生成DNA中间体。 Positive-strand RNA is directly translatable, negative-strand RNA must first be transcribed to positive-strand, and retroviruses use reverse transcriptase to generate a DNA intermediate.

HIV和丙型肝炎病毒分别代表反转录和正链RNA病毒的增殖典型路径。 HIV and hepatitis C virus typify the multiplication cycles of retroviruses and positive-strand RNA viruses, respectively.

负链RNA病毒的复制Replication of (-)RNA viruses

流感病毒基因组与增殖周期Flu virus genome and multiplication cycle

一、负链RNA病毒的复制机制Replication of (-)RNA viruses

1
负链RNA病毒基因组与mRNA不同 The genomic (-)RNA of negative-strand RNA viruses is not equivalent to mRNA

负链RNA不能直接翻译，必须先转录为mRNA。 (-)RNA cannot be directly translated and must first be transcribed into mRNA.

2
必须携带病毒编码的RNA依赖型RNA聚合酶（转录酶） A constitutive viral RNA polymerase (transcriptase) is required

宿主细胞不具备从RNA合成RNA的酶，病毒入胞需自带该酶。 Host cells lack RNA-directed RNA polymerase, so the virus must bring its own upon entry.

3
新合成的正链（+）RNA有两种功能 The newly synthesized (+)RNA serves two functions

一部分作为mRNA被翻译成蛋白质。 Part acts as mRNA for translation into proteins.

一部分作为模板合成新的互补负链基因组。 Part serves as a matrix for synthesis of new genomic (–)RNA strands.

4
复制过程示意图 Schematic of replication process

病毒聚合酶首先以负链RNA为模板，合成正链（抗基因组）RNA。 Viral polymerase uses the genomic (–)RNA as a template to synthesize the positive-strand (antigenome) RNA.

正链RNA既可作为mRNA进行翻译，也作为模板再合成新的负链RNA，供子代病毒包装。 (+)RNA can function as mRNA for translation or as a template to produce new (–)RNA for progeny virion assembly.

这一过程是放大的，每个模板可合成多个互补链。 This is an amplification system; multiple polymerases can synthesize several strands simultaneously3.

二、流感病毒基因组特点Flu virus genome features

1
基因组为8条不同长度的负链RNA片段 The genome consists of 8 segments of negative-strand RNA of different lengths

每条RNA序列编码一种蛋白。 Each segment encodes one protein.

如：PB2、PB1、PA、HA、NP、NA、M、NS等蛋白。 For example: PB2, PB1, PA, HA, NP, NA, M, NS proteins.

2
一条片段对应一条mRNA，最终翻译为一种蛋白 One segment corresponds to one mRNA, and each mRNA translates into one protein31.

三、流感病毒的增殖周期Multiplication cycle of the flu virus

1
吸附（HA1与宿主唾液酸受体结合） Attachment (HA1 binds to host sialic acid receptor)
2
内吞入胞 Entry by endocytosis
3
融合与脱壳（M2、HA2参与，低pH环境下） Fusion and uncoating, mediated by M2 and HA2 under low pH in the endosome
4
核酸释放至胞质，随即进入细胞核 Release of nucleic acids into the cytoplasm, then import into the nucleus
5
病毒转录（RdRp复合体和NP介导，负链RNA转录为mRNA） Viral transcription (RdRp complex and NP direct the transcription of vRNA to mRNA in the nucleus)
6
基因组复制（负链RNA经抗基因组正链中间体复制更多负链RNA） Genome replication (vRNA is replicated via a positive-strand antigenome intermediate)
7
mRNA转运到胞质翻译，合成病毒蛋白（包括包膜和结构蛋白） mRNA is transported to cytoplasm for translation, generating viral proteins including the envelope and structural proteins
8
病毒核衣壳组装，并由M1、NS2等蛋白导出至胞质 Ribonucleoprotein complexes are assembled and exported into the cytoplasm via M1, NS2 proteins
9
病毒组装、出芽并离开宿主细胞 Assembly, packaging, and budding to exit the host cell
10
部分病毒（如有包膜）在细胞膜出芽获得包膜 Enveloped virions acquire their envelope by budding at the plasma membrane
11
细胞因感染可发生凋亡，病毒NS1蛋白可拮抗干扰素反应 Infected cells may undergo apoptosis; viral NS1 protein antagonizes the interferon response.

关键总结Key summary

负链RNA病毒需自身携带转录酶完成mRNA转录。 Negative-strand RNA viruses must bring their own RNA polymerase for mRNA synthesis.

新合成的+链既为蛋白翻译所用，也参与基因组复制。 The newly synthesized (+)RNA serves for both protein translation and genome replication.

流感病毒具有分段基因组，每段一蛋白，复制与转录均在细胞核内进行且高度复杂。 The flu virus has a segmented genome, each segment codes for one protein, and its replication and transcription take place in the nucleus with high complexity.

病毒增殖后期阶段Late phase of viral multiplication

1
后期阶段定义及主要内容 Definition and key events of the late phase

后期阶段对应病毒的装配、成熟以及病毒颗粒的释放。 The late phase refers to the assembly, maturation, and release of virus particles.

此阶段是病毒生命周期的最后关键步骤。 This stage represents the final critical steps in the virus life cycle.

2
发生的细胞部位 Location of late phase events

DNA病毒的晚期步骤多数发生在细胞核（痘病毒除外）。 For DNA viruses, late-phase events mostly occur in the nucleus (except for poxvirus).

RNA病毒的晚期步骤一般在细胞质完成。 For RNA viruses, these steps usually take place in the cytoplasm.

3
产量和装配效率 Yield and assembly efficiency

病毒后期的产量仍然较低，并非所有核酸和蛋白都能组装成完整病毒。 Viral yield in the late phase is still low, as not all nucleic acids and proteins successfully assemble into complete virions.

以疱疹病毒为例，装配率有限。 Herpesvirus is an example where assembly efficiency is restricted.

4
病毒颗粒的装配 Assembly of viral particles

新合成的蛋白和核酸自发组装成病毒前体结构，称为前衣壳（procapsid）。 Newly synthesized proteins and nucleic acids spontaneously assemble into a precursor structure called the procapsid.

病毒蛋白在装配后需进行翻译后修饰，如裂解、二硫键形成和糖基化等。 After assembly, viral proteins undergo post-translational modifications such as cleavage, disulfide bridge formation, and glycosylation.

包膜病毒的包膜糖蛋白会发生寡聚化，利于形成病毒外壳。 Envelope glycoproteins of enveloped viruses oligomerize to facilitate viral envelope formation.

5
无包膜病毒的组装实例 Example of naked virus assembly

以脊髓灰质炎病毒为例，其装配为多步级联过程，并涉及前体蛋白剪切。 Poliovirus assembly involves a complex cascade with precursor protein cleavage.

具体步骤为：P1多肽切割→单体原体（protomer，VP0, VP1, VP3）→五聚体单位（capsomere）→六十聚体前衣壳（procapsid）→核酸进入形成前病毒体（provirion）→最终成熟为病毒体（virion，VP4, VP2, VP3, VP1）。 The process: P1 polypeptide cleavage → protomer (VP0, VP1, VP3) → capsomere (five protomers) → procapsid (sixty capsomeres) → RNA enters to form provirion → final mature virion (VP4, VP2, VP3, VP1).

6
病毒颗粒的释放 Release of viral particles

无包膜病毒通过细胞溶解（裂解）释放。 Naked (non-enveloped) viruses are released by cell lysis.

包膜病毒通过出芽（如质膜）或胞吐（内膜）方式释放，病毒成熟阶段是在离开细胞过程中完成，获得感染力。 Enveloped viruses are released by budding (at the plasma membrane) or exocytosis (via internal membranes); a maturation stage during or after release is necessary to acquire infectious power.

以HIV和狂犬病毒为例，包膜病毒在细胞膜出芽，最终成熟为具有感染性的病毒颗粒。 For HIV and rabies virus, viral particles bud from the cell membrane and mature to become infectious virions.

总结 Summary

后期阶段包括病毒的组装、成熟及释放，是病毒生命周期的最后步骤。 The late phase includes assembly, maturation, and release, as the final steps in the viral life cycle.

DNA病毒后期多在核内，RNA病毒多在胞质。 DNA viruses usually complete these steps in the nucleus, RNA viruses in the cytoplasm.

病毒装配自发完成，但效率有限；释放方式依有无包膜而异。 Viral assembly is spontaneous but not fully efficient; release methods differ for naked and enveloped viruses.

包膜病毒需经历出芽和成熟步骤才能具有感染性。 Enveloped viruses must undergo budding and maturation to become infectious.
